समिति का अनुमोदन करेंगे। साक्षात्कार के दौरान सभी सदस्यों का उपस्थित रहना 
अनिवार्य है, आवश्यकता पड़ने पर, संयोजक, कुलपति के अनुमोदन से, उपस्थित न 
हो पाने वाले सदस्य के स्थान पर किसी अन्य सदस्य को नियुक्त करने का प्रयास कर 
सकेंगे; 

(ख) चयन समिति के अध्यक्ष यह सुनिश्चित करेंगे कि विज्ञापित मानदंडों को पूरा न करने 
वाले किसी भी अभ्यर्थी का साक्षात्कार न लिया जाए या चयन हेतु विचार न किया 
जाए, अंतिम परीक्षा में उपस्थित या उपस्थित होने वाले अभ्यर्थियों को अर्हत नहीं 
माना जाएगा, यदि आवेदन प्राप्ति की अंतिम तारीख तक उनका परिणाम प्रतीक्षित 
है। न्यूनतम विज्ञापित अर्हता पूरी न करने वाले अभ्यर्थियों पर निम्न पद के लिए भी 


132

[PART III—SEc.4] 


(छ) 


) 


विचार नहीं किया जा सकता, यदि निम्न पद विज्ञापित न किया गया हो; 

चयन समिति के अध्यक्ष यह सुनिश्चित करेंगे कि साक्षात्कार में आने वाले सभी 
अभ्यर्थियों का चयन समिति द्वारा उचित रूप से साक्षात्कार लिया जाए; 
चयन समिति के सदस्यों को स्वयं को उस चयन प्रक्रिया से अलग रखे जाएंगे, जहाँ 
उनके अपने करीबी रिश्तेदार परीक्षण या साक्षात्कार में दिखाई दे रहे हैं, यदि ऐसा 
हो, तो संबंधित समिति सदस्य को परीक्षा से बाहर रहना होगा और कुलपति के 
अनुमोदन से उसके स्थान पर किसी अन्य व्यक्ति को सदस्य के रूप में नियुक्त किया 
जाएगा; 

अनुसूचित चयन समिति की बैठकों को अल्प सूचना पर रद्द या पुनर्निर्धारित नहीं 
किया सकेगा; इससे साक्षात्कार में उपस्थित होने वाले अभ्यर्थियों, विशेषकर बाहर से 
आने वाले अभ्यर्थियों, को होने वाली असुविधा से बचा जा सकेगा। किसी आकस्मिक 
कारण से अध्यक्ष या परियोजना अन्वेषक की अनुपस्थिति की स्थिति में, साक्षात्कारों 
का विहित समय पर सुचारू संचालन सुनिश्चित करने के लिए, कुलपति के अनुमोदन 
से उनके नामिती की नियुक्ति की जा सकेगी; 

परियोजना के अधीन नियुक्ति हेतु चयनित और विचारित अभ्यर्थियों के आवेदनों 
सहित, संकाय प्रभारी (अनुसंधान और विकास) द्वारा सम्यक रूपसे सिफ़ारिशे की 
गई चयन समिति की रिपोर्ट, अनुमोदन के लिए कुलपति के समक्ष प्रस्तुत की जाएगी; 
कुलपति का अनुमोदन प्राप्त करने के पश्चात, कुलसचिव कार्यालय द्वारा चयनित 
अभ्यर्थियों को नियुक्ति प्रस्ताव जारी किया जा सकेगा।

अधिकार नहीं मिलता है; 

सभी परियोजना कर्मचारी सदस्यों को कार्यभार ग्रहण करते समय गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय 
के साथ ₹100 के गैर-न्यायिक स्टाम्प पेपर पर एक संविदा समझौते पर हस्ताक्षर करना होगा, 
संविदा समझौते पर हस्ताक्षर के पश्चात मुआवजा जारी किया जाएगा, यदि कोई परियोजना 
कर्मचारी सदस्य कार्यभार ग्रहण करने की तारीख से पंद्रह दिन के भीतर संविदा समझौते पर 
हस्ताक्षर नहीं करता है, तो नियुक्ति रद्द कर दी जाएगी। 

(i) चयनित अभ्यर्थीका उपलब्धियां उपरोक्त सारणी में यथा इंगित होगा;

परिणाम केवल परियोजना अन्वेषक की पूर्व लिखित स्वीकृति और परियोजना के

वित्तपोषण अभिकरण के मानदंडों के अनुसार, जो भी न्यूनतम हो; 
किसी परियोजना कर्मचारीवृंद के नए चयन प्रक्रिया से गुजरने की आवश्यकता के बिना एक 
परियोजना से दूसरी परियोजना में स्थानांतरण के मामले में, परियोजना अन्वेषक द्वारा ऐसे 
स्थानांतरण के लिए वैध कारण और औचित्य प्रस्तुत किए जाएँगे और परियोजना कर्मचारी 
सदस्यों की व्यक्तिगत फ़ाइल संख्या वही रहेगी। इसके अतिरिक्त, किसी भी स्थिति में, कुल 
संचयी नियुक्ति पांच वर्ष से अधिक नहीं होगी;

है या पहले कृत्य कर चुका है, तो पिछले उपलब्धियां को नए पद के लिए नियुक्त किए जा रहे 
पद हेतु समेकित वेतन विहित करने के आधार के रूप में लिया जा सकेगा;

(ii) जिस पद के लिए विचार किया जा रहा है, उसके अनुरूप अर्जित अतिरिक्त योग्यताओं

चिकित्सा सुविधाएँ: गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय में नियमित ओपीडी चिकित्सा सुविधा (केवल 
परामर्श और निदान के लिए) का लाभ उठाया जा सकेगा; 
सेवा समाप्ति: परियोजना नियुक्ति की अवधि के दौरान, परियोजना कर्मचारी सदस्यों को एक 
मास के नोटिस के साथ या उसके बिना या उसके बदले में एक मास के प्रतिकार के भुगतान 
पर सेवा से हटाया जा सकेगा, इसी प्रकार, परियोजना कर्मचारी सदस्य भी एक मास का 
नोटिस देकर या नोटिस अवधि के बदले में प्रतिकार देकर त्यागपत्र दे सकते हैं; 
प्रति वर्ष छुट्टी पात्रता: यह नियम विश्वविद्यालय के संविदा कर्मचारियों पर भी समान रूप से 
लागू होता है, जो समय-समय पर परिवर्तित होता रहता है और 
संविदा की समाप्ति या नवीनीकरण पर छुट्टी का नकदीकरण या आगे ले जाने की अनुमति 
नहीं है।

सक्षम प्राधिकारी का अनुमोदन अपेक्षित है। ऐसे व्ययों का रिकॉर्ड परियोजना खातों के निर्दिष्ट 
बजट मद के अधीन दर्ज किया जाएगा। टिकट क्रय सहायता, वीज़ा सहायता, बीमा आदि के लिए 
ट्रैवल अभिकर्ताओं को शुल्क का भुगतान परियोजना निधि से स्वीकार्य होगा। 

(ख) किंतु , यदि वित्तपोषण अभिकरण ने परियोजना के अधीन यात्रा के लिए कोई विशिष्ट शर्तें 
विनिर्दिष्ट की हैं, तो उनका सामान्य रूप से पालन किया जाएगा। 


(23) अस्थायी परियोजना अग्रिम: (क) परियोजनाओं के लिए अपेक्षित विविध व्ययों को पूरा करने 


हेतु, परियोजना प्रमुख परियोजना से प्राप्त धनरकम का उपयोग कर सकेगा और इसके लिए 
अस्थायी अग्रिम ले सकेगा और अग्रिम विभिन्न बजट मदों, जैसे आकस्मिकता, उपभोग्य वस्तुएँ 
और यात्रा, से लिया जा सकेगा; 

(ख) परियोजना प्रमुख विहित प्ररूप में अनुरोध प्रस्तुत करेगा, एक समय में एक परियोजना से 
अधिकतम 4 अग्रिम स्वीकृत किए जाएँगे, और वह भी प्रति व्यक्ति केवल एक; 

(ग) सभी अग्रिम रकम का निपटान उस कार्यकलाप तथा उद्देश्य के पूरा होने की तारीख से पंद्रह 
दिनों के भीतर किया जाना चाहिए जिसके लिए अग्रिम रकम ली गई थी; 

(घ) परियोजना प्रमुख द्वारा व्यय विवरण विहित प्ररूप में सभी मूल बिलों के साथ सम्यक रूपसे 
सत्यापित प्रस्तुत किया जाएगा, परियोजना प्रमुख यह सुनिश्चित करेगा कि सभी बिल सुसंगत 
परियोजना रजिस्टर में ठीक से दर्ज किए गए हैं। 

(ड)गैर-उपभोज्य वस्तुओं को भंडार और क्रय कार्यालय के परिसंपत्ति रजिस्टर में प्रविष्ट किया 
जाएगा, अन्यथा भुगतान के लिए बिल पास नहीं किए जाएँगे। 


(24) परियोजना क्रय: 


(क) (i) किसी परियोजना के अधीन क्रय की गई सभी वस्तुएँ गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय (या यदि

विनिर्दिष्ट हो, तो वित्तपोषण अभिकरण) की संपत्ति होती हैं, हालाँकि उनका उपयोग मुख्य 
रूप से परियोजना के लिए ही किया जाना है, इन्हें गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय द्वारा 
अनुमोदित प्रक्रियाओं का उपयोग करके क्रय किया जाएगा, परियोजना समाप्त होने या 
पूर्ण होने के पश्चात, ये वस्तुएँ गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय के पास ही रहेंगी, जब तक कि 
वित्तपोषण अभिकरण को वापस करने का कोई विशिष्ट उपबंध या अपेक्षा न हो। 

(i) परिणामस्वरूप, खरीदी गई प्रत्येक वस्तु को परिसंपत्ति रजिस्टर में दर्ज किया जाएगा 
और सभी स्थायी परिसंपत्तियों का मूल्यहास गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय मानदंडों के अनुसार 
होगा; 


(ख) परियोजना अन्वेषक मूल बिल और कैश मेमो का सत्यापन करेगा और भुगतान या 


प्रतिपूर्ति के लिए विहित प्ररूप में वित्त कार्यालय को प्रस्तुत करेगा, परियोजना अन्वेषक यह 
सुनिश्चित करेगा कि सभी बिल स्टॉक रजिस्टर में ठीक से प्रविष्ट किए गए हैं, गैर-उपभोग्य 
वस्तुओं को भंडार और क्रय कार्यालय द्वारा बनाए गए परिसंपत्ति रजिस्टर में दर्ज किया 
जाना चाहिए, अन्यथा भुगतान के लिए बिल पास नहीं किए जाएँगे; 


(ग) उच्च-स्तरीय या महंगे उपकरणों के क्रय के लिए, उपस्कारों के तकनीकी पहलुओं और

गया है, तकनीकी क्रय समिति की संरचना इस प्रकार है: 
(i) संकाय प्रभारी (प्रयोगशाला) अध्यक्ष; 
(i) खरीद से संबंधित क्षेत्र में एक संकाय सदस्य सदस्य; 
(ii) यदि उपकरण की अनुमानित लागत 1 करोड़ रुपये या उससे. सदस्य; 
अधिक है, तो खरीद से संबंधित अनुसंधान के क्षेत्र में कार्यरत 
एक विशेषज्ञ 
(५) परियोजना के परियोजना अन्वेषक सदस्य; 


(घ) टीपीसी की ज़िम्मेदारियाँ हैं: 


(i) परियोजनाओं के उद्देश्यों को पूरा करने के लिए क्रय हेतु सबसे उपयुक्त सामान्य 
तकनीकी अपेक्षा को अंतिम रूप देना; 
(i) उपकरण खरीद के तकनीकी विनिर्देश तैयार करना; 
(ii) निविदा प्रक्रिया के दौरान तकनीकी सहायता अनुदत्त करना; और खरीद गति शक्ति 
विश्वविद्यालय की निविदा समिति द्वारा की जाएगी। 


(ड) सार्वजनिक निधि से वस्तुओं या सेवाओं की खरीद भारत सरकार के सामान्य वित्त नियम 


नियमों के अनुरूप होगी। समयबद्ध वितरण और गुणवत्ता संबंधी पहलुओं को ध्यान में 
रखते हुए निजी निधि से वस्तुओं या सेवाओं के क्रय के लिए इन नियमों में छूट दी गई है, 
तथापि, निजी निधि से की गई खरीद विश्वविद्यालय के लागू मानदंडों के अनुरूप होगी। 


(25) बजटीय मानदंड: 
अनुसंधान परियोजनाएँ, एक अनुसंधान परियोजना के कुल प्रभार में गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय 
उपरि और परियोजना के वास्तविक व्यय सम्मिलित होंगे, जिनमें निम्नलिखित सम्मिलित होंगे:- 


(क) क्रय किए जाने वाले स्थायी उपस्कर या निर्मित उपस्कर या मॉडल; 

(ख) उपभोज्य सामग्री; 

(ग) परियोजना कृत्य से संबंधित यात्रा व्यय (यदि बजट में हो या प्रायोजक द्वारा अनुमति 
दी गई हो तो घरेलू और विदेशी); 

(घ) किसी अन्य बाहरी अभिकरण को देय गणना संबंधी या अन्य प्रभार; 

(ड) रिपोर्ट तैयार करने और साहित्य (पुस्तकें, पत्रिकाएँ) तथा प्रस्ताव के अधीन बजट में 
सम्मिलित और वित्त पोषण अभिकरण द्वारा अनुमोदित किसी अन्य मद के लिए सभी 
आकस्मिक व्यय; 

(च) परियोजना कर्मचारियों का प्रतिकर; भुगतान के आधार पर किए जाने वाले कृत्य के 
लिए व्यय, छात्र सहायकों आदि को उपलब्धियां, 

(छ) यात्रा के दौरान उपकरण और जनशक्ति का बीमा। 


(26) परियोजना निगरानी समिति: 
बड़ी परियोजनाओं के लिए, निम्नलिखित संरचना वाली परियोजना निगरानी समिति

परियोजनाओं को समय पर पूरा करने के लिए समय-समय पर (वर्ष में कम से कम एक बार) 
परियोजना की प्रगति की समीक्षा और मूल्यांकन करेगी, समिति परियोजना से संबंधित किसी 
भी मामले में संकाय प्रभारी (अनुसंधान और विकास) को सलाह दे सकेगी, परियोजना निगरानी 


समिति की संरचना इस प्रकार होगी: 

(क) फआईसी (अनुसंधान और विकास) अध्यक्ष: 
(ख) संबंधित केंद्र या क्षेत्र के संकाय प्रभारी या उनके द्वारा नामनिर्दिष्ट सदस्य; 
(ग) संकाय प्रभारी (प्रयोगशालाएँ) सदस्य; 
(घ) सुसंगत क्षेत्र का एक संकाय सदस्य; 
(ड) यदि अपेक्षित हो तो गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय वित्त पोषण 

के बाहर से सुसंगत क्षेत्र का एक विशेषज्ञ: अभिकरण का सदस्य या प्रतिनिधि; 
(व) परियोजना अन्वेषक; 


कुलपति, परियोजना निगरानी समिति के गठन या अनुमोदन के लिए सक्षम प्राधिकारी हैं। 


(27) असहमति या विवाद : (क) परामर्श परियोजना के किसी भी चरण में गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय के 
भीतर उत्पन्न होने वाले किसी भी मतभेद का समाधान संकाय प्रभारी (अनुसंधान और विकास) 
या कुलपति के परामर्श से किया जाएगा ताकि परियोजना की बाधाओं को शीघ्र दूर किया जा 
सके और सुचारू संचालन सुनिश्चित किया जा सके; 

(ख) परामर्श परियोजना के किसी भी चरण में अन्वेषकओं और वित्त पोषण अभिकरणओं के 
बीच किसी भी विवाद की स्थिति में, केवल अन्वेषक ही विवाद के निपटान के लिए उत्तरदायी 
होंगे; 

(ग) परियोजना से उत्पन्न किसी भी विवाद के लिए गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय उत्तरदायी नहीं 
होगा; 

(घ) सभी विधिक कार्रवाई केवल वडोदरा स्थित सिविल न्यायालयों के अधिकार क्षेत्र के अधीन 
होगी। 

(28) मध्यस्थम्‌ : (क) परामर्श परियोजना, या किसी अन्य खंड(ओं) या पक्षों के अधिकारों और दायित्वों 
की किसी भी विषय-वस्तु या उसमें विनिर्दिष्ट अन्य मामलों या परामर्श से उत्पन्न किसी भी चीज़ 
के संदर्भ में या शर्तों के संबंध में, परामर्श के दौरान हो या उसके पश्चात, पक्षों के बीच किसी भी 
समय कोई विवाद या मतभेद उत्पन्न होने की स्थिति में, ऐसे विवादों या मतभेदों को आपसी 
बातचीत द्वारा सुलझाने का प्रयास किया जाएगा। 

(ख) यदि ऐसी बातचीत निष्फल हो, तो विवाद का अंतिम निपटान मध्यस्थतम्‌ और सुलह 
अधिनियम, (1996 का 26) के माध्यम से किया जाएगा और माध्यस्थों को तर्कपूर्ण और स्पष्ट 
निर्णय देंगे। 

(29) सीमित देयता: 
यदि अन्वेषक(ओं) और वित्त पोषण अभिकरण के बीच कोई विधिक विवाद उत्पन्न होता है, 
जिससे अन्वेषकओं को वित्त पोषण अभिकरण द्वारा हुए नुकसान की भरपाई करने की किसी 
भी तरह से ज़िम्मेदारी मिलती है, तो ऐसी देयता अधिकतम देयता तक सीमित होगी, जिसकी 
गणना निम्नानुसार की जाएगी:

उत्तरदायी होगा। 

(30) समझौता ज्ञापन या समझौते के लिए मार्गदर्शक सिद्धांत 

(क) बौद्धिक संपदा अधिकार: गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय को यथासंभव अपने अधिकारों 
को अक्षुण्ण रखने का प्रयास करना चाहिए, गति शक्ति विश्वविद्यालय को भविष्य में 
किसी भी समय अनुसंधान से प्राप्त जानकारी का बिना किसी की अनुमति के 
उपयोग करने के अपने अधिकार की रक्षा करनी चाहिए, यदि आईपीआर का

अधिकार होगा और इसका समझौता ज्ञापन में स्पष्ट रूप से उल्लेख किया जाएगा;

डेवलपर, संकाय प्रभारी या परियोजना अन्वेषक: समझौता ज्ञापन में डेवलपर का

कार्यान्वयन के लिए ज़िम्मेदार है; 

डिलिवरेबल्स: समझौता ज्ञापन के पाठ में इस खंड की शब्दावली सबसे महत्वपूर्ण है, 
सटीकता सर्वोपरि है, समय-सीमा प्राप्त करने योग्य होंगे, डिलिवरेबल्स का स्पष्ट रूप 
से समझौते के अनुच्छेद या खंड या sa के रूप में उल्लेख किया जाएगा और 
कृत्य विवरण और प्राप्त किए जाने वाले लक्ष्यों की तारीख स्पष्ट रूप से लिख सकेगा 
और पूर्णता के लिए पूर्ण परियोजना प्रस्ताव संलग्न किया जा सकेगा;

अभिलिखित किया जाएगा; 

(४) समिति की सिफारिशों को कुलपति द्वारा अनुमोदित किया जाएगा;

नियमित खरीद आर्डर के विरुद्ध प्राप्त सामग्री का किया जाता है। 

(7) भुगतान: आपूर्तिकर्ताओं को भुगतान लेखा विभाग द्वारा चेक या नेशनल इलेक्ट्रॉनिक फंड्स ट्रांसफर 
या रियल टाइम ग्रॉस सेटलमेंट के माध्यम से किया जाएगा, सहमत नियमों और शर्तों के आधार पर, 
सहयोगी खातों के माध्यम से भुगतान की प्रक्रिया अपनाई जा सकती है। स्वीकृत सामग्री की 
डिलीवरी लेने के पश्चात्‌ भुगतान किया जाएगा और किसी भी मामले में फर्म को सामग्री की आपूर्ति 
की प्रत्याशा में भुगतान नहीं किया जाएगा। 


56. वित्तीय शक्तियों की शक्तियों के प्रत्यायोजन या वित्तीय शक्तियों के प्रत्यायोजन के सामान्य सिद्धांत 
अनुसूची.- (1) विश्वविद्यालय का कोई भी अधिकारी तब तक कोई व्यय नहीं करेगा या व्यय से संबंधित 
कोई दायित्व नहीं लेगा जब तक कि विश्वविद्यालय के सक्षम प्राधिकारी से इसके लिए 
अनुमोदन न ले लिया गया हो। 


(2) यदि निधि या अनुदान जारी करने से पहले व्यय हेतु स्वीकृति जारी करना आवश्यक हो

जाता है, तो स्वीकृति में यह विनिर्दिष्ट किया जाना चाहिए कि ऐसा व्यय निधि या अनुदान के 
वितरण के अधीन है। 

स्वीकृति की समाप्ति: किसी भी व्यय के लिए स्वीकृति, जब तक कि उसे विशेष रूप से 

नवीनीकृत न किया जाए, ऐसी स्वीकृति जारी होने की तारीख से 12 मास की अवधि के 

भीतर समाप्त हो जाएगी, परन्तु: 

() जब स्वीकृति की अवधि स्वीकृति में ही विहित की गई हो, तो वह ऐसी अवधि की 
समाप्ति पर समाप्त हो जाएगी; 

2) जब किसी स्वीकृति में यह विशिष्ट उपबंध हो कि व्यय निर्दिष्ट वित्तीय वर्ष के बजट 
उपबंध से पूरा किया जाएगा, तो स्वीकृति वित्तीय वर्ष के अंत में समाप्त हो जाएगी। 

जब तक अन्यथा विनिर्दिष्ट न हो, सभी प्रत्यायोेजन अवसर के आधार पर होते हैं। उच्च 

सक्षम प्राधिकारी का अनुमोदन प्राप्त करने के लिए, विभाजन से बचेगा और उसे 

हतोत्साहित किया जाएगा। 

प्रत्यायोजित शक्तियों को मूल रूप से शक्तियों को प्रत्यायोजित करने वाले प्राधिकारी के पूर्व 

अनुमोदन के बिना आगे प्रत्यायोजित नहीं किया जा सकेगा। 

इसके अधीन प्रत्यायोजित सभी शक्तियां सुसंगत बजट मद के अधीन बजट आवंटन के 

अधीन हैं तथा अधिनियम या संविधि में दर्शाए गए विहित प्रक्रिया और मार्गदर्शक सिद्धांतों 

या भारत सरकार या कार्यकारी परिषद द्वारा समय-समय पर जारी मार्गदर्शक सिद्धांतों के 
अनुपालन के पश्चात्‌ अनुदत्त की जाएंगी। 

इस पुस्तिका में दिए गए उपबंधों और मूल स्रोत जैसे सामान्य वित्तीय विनियमन आदि या 

भारत सरकार के प्रचलित नियमों या मार्गदर्शक सिद्धांतों या विधि के बीच अस्पष्टता या 

विरोधाभास की स्थिति में, मूल स्रोत या भारत सरकार के नियमों में निहित Gude ही मान्य 
होंगे। 

सभी क्रय प्रस्तावों पर सामान्य वित्तीय विनियमन या क्रय प्रक्रियाओं में विहित प्रक्रिया के 

अनुसार कार्रवाई की जाएगी, सभी संबंधित प्राधिकरण प्रशासनिक अनुमोदन के लिए 

प्रस्तावों को कुलपति या कुलसचिव को प्रस्तुत करेंगे। 

(9) कार्यों के सिवाय अन्य मामलों के लिए: व्यय के सभी मामलों में वित्त विभाग की सहमति 
आवश्यक होगी। 

(10) पदों के सृजन या व्यय करने के लिए एक औपचारिक स्वीकृति सभी संबंधितों को सूचित की 
जाएगी, जिसमें वित्तीय सहमति और बजट उपबंध की मौजूदगी का उल्लेख होगा। 

(11) इन प्रत्यायोजनों के अतिरिक्त, ऐसे मामलों में जहाँ पर्याप्त प्रतिस्पर्धा विधमान नहीं है या 
आकस्मिक क्रय किया जाना है, विहित प्रक्रिया में ढील देते हुए व्यय किया जाना है, वित्त 
विभाग की सहमति से कुलपति का अनुमोदन आवश्यक होगा। 

(12) वित्तीय शक्तियों के इन प्रत्यायोजनों के होते हुए, कुलपति उन अधिकारियों में से किसी की 
शक्तियों का प्रयोग कर सकते हैं, जिन्हें ये शक्तियां प्रत्यायोजित की गई हैं। 

(13) क्रय समिति का गठन रजिस्ट्रार या कुलपति द्वारा प्रत्यायोजित शक्तियों के अनुसार किया 
जाएगा। 

(क) वित्तीय शक्तियों का प्रत्यायोजन नियममें वित्तीय शक्तियाँ रखने वाले सभी 
अधिकारियों को पिछली तिमाही के दौरान किए गए व्यय के पश्चात वाले मास की 
पंद्रह तारीख तक कुलपति को तिमाही रिपोर्ट प्रस्तुत करनी होगी; 

(ख) कुलपति, यदि तो किसी प्राधिकारी की वित्तीय शक्तियों को रद्द या सीमित कर 
सकेंगे, यदि संबंधित प्राधिकारी इस वित्तीय शक्तियों के प्रत्यायोजन के अधीन उन्हें 
प्रत्यायोजित वित्तीय शक्तियों का पालन नहीं कर रहा है। 

ये प्रत्यायोजित वित्तीय शक्तियाँ पुनर्वलोकन के अध्याधीन हैं।

GATI SHAKTI VISHWAVIDYALAYA 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 18th June 2026 


F.No. GS V/REG/Ordinances/Notification/C1/2026/189 —In exercise of the powers 
conferred by sub-section (2) of section 28 of the Central Universities Act, 2009 (25 of 2009), 
the Vice-Chancellor of Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya with the prior approval of the Executive 
Council, hereby makes the following Ordinances, namely: 


1. Short title and (1) These Ordinances may be called the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
commencement.- Ordinances, 2026. 


(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the 


Official Gazette. 
2. Definitions.- (1) In these Ordinances, unless the context otherwise requires— 
(a) “academic council” means the Academic Council of the 
University; 


(b) “academic staff’ means such categories of staff as are designated 
as academic staff by these Ordinances; 

(०) “Act” means Central Universities Act 2009 (25 of 2009); 

(d) “affiliation and recognition board” means Affiliation and 
Recognition Board of the University; 

(e) “Board of studies” means the Board of Studies of the University; 

(f) “Campus” means the unit established or constituted by the 
University for making arrangements for instruction, research, 
education and training; 

(g) “finance officer” means the Finance Officer of the University; 

(h) “hostels” means hostels of the University; 

(i) “institution” means an institution, school, college or centre of 
studies maintained by or admitted to the privileges of the 
University for imparting education and training in its disciplines; 

(0) “professor of practice”, “chair professor’, “joint faculty”, 
“adjunct faculty”, “distinguished faculty”, “honorary professor” 
and visiting faculty” means the faculty appointed in accordance 
with the guidelines framed by the Executive Council; 

(k) “seniority list” means the list on seniority of any employee, 
maintained by the University; 

(0) “University” means Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya established 
under Section 3F of the Central Universities Act, 2009 (25 of 
2009); 


(2) The words and expressions used in these ordinances and not defined, 
but defined in the act and statues, shall have the same meaning espectively 
aligned to them in the Act and Statutes. 


(3) Should there be any difference in interpretation of these Ordinances 
amongst the English and Hindi versions due to translation error, the 


162 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


English version shall assume precedence. 


३... Application.- The Ordinances may provide for all or any of the following matters, 
namely: — 
(1) the admission of students to the University and their enrolment 
as such; 
(2) the courses of study to be laid down for all degrees, diplomas 
and certificates of the University; 
(3) the medium of instruction and examination; 


(4) the award of degrees, diplomas, certificates and other academic 
distinctions, the qualifications for the same and the means to be 
taken relating to the granting and obtaining of the same; 

(5) the fees to be charged for courses of study in the University and 
for admission to examinations, degrees and diplomas to the 
University; 

(6) the conditions for award of fellowships, scholarships, 
studentships, medals and prizes; 

(7) the conduct of examination, including the term of office and 
manner of appointment and the duties of examining bodies, 
examiners and moderators; 

(8) the conditions of residence of the students of the University; 


(9) the special arrangements, if any, which may be made for the 
residence and teaching of women students and the prescribing of 
special courses of studies for them; 

(10) the establishment of Centres of Studies, Boards of Studies, 
Specialised Laboratories and other Committees; 

(11) the manner of co-operation and collaboration with other 
Universities, Institutions and other agencies including learned 
bodies or associations; 

(12) the creation, composition and functions of any other body which 
is considered necessary for improving the academic life of the 
University; 

(13) the institution of fellowships, scholarships, studentships, medals 
and prizes; 

(14) the setting up of machinery for redressal of grievances of 
employees and students; and 

(15) all other matters which by this Act, or, the Statutes, are to be, or 
may be provided for by the Ordinances. 


4. Qualifications, (1) Qualifications: 

appointment, emoluments, — (4) For appointment as Vice-Chancellor of the University, the person 

terms and conditions of : f ; ; 

epics. of Vice=chabcelloke shall possess the highest level of competence, integrity, morals, 
and institutional commitment; 

(b) He shall be a distinguished academician, with a minimum of ten 

years of experience as Professor in a University system or ten 
years of research experience on an equivalent position in a 
reputed Academia or Industry or Research and Development 
organisation. 


(2) Appointment: The Vice-Chancellor shall be appointed as per the 
provisions laid out in the Act and Statutes. 


(3) Powers and Duties: The Vice-Chancellor shall have all powers 
and duties as laid out in the Act and Statutes. In addition: 


[भाग पा--_खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 163 


(a) The Vice-Chancellor shall be the principal executive and 
academic officer of the University and shall exercise general 
supervision and control over the affairs of the University and 
give effect to the decisions of all the authorities of the 
University; 

(b) The Vice-Chancellor, if he is of the opinion that any decision of 
any authority of the University is beyond the powers of the 
authority conferred by the provisions of the Act, the Statutes or 
the Ordinances of the University or that any decision taken is not 
in the interest of the University, may ask the authority concerned 
to review its decision within sixty days of such decision and if 
the authority refuses to review the decision either in whole or in 
part or no decision is taken by it within the said period of sixty 
days, the matter shall be referred to the Visitor whose decision 
thereon shall be final; 

(c) The Vice-Chancellor shall not act upon any decision of any 
authority, if he is of the opinion that: 

(i) 1115 ultra vires of the provisions of the Act, the Statutes, 
or the Ordinances of the University; 

(ii) such a decision shall not be in the best interests of the 
University. In either case, he may ask the authority 
concerned to review the decision, and if any difference 
persists, the matter shall be referred to the Visitor, whose 
decision shall be final; 

(d) As the Chairman of any authority, body or committee of the 
University, he shall be empowered to suspend a member from 
the meeting of the authority, body or committee for obstructing 
or stalling the proceedings or for indulging in behavior 
unbecoming of a member of such Committee; 

(e) The Vice-Chancellor shall have disciplinary powers with regard 
to employees of the University; 

(f) In an emergent situation, he shall take any action on behalf of 
any authority in which the power is vested and to report the 
action taken in the next meeting of the authority; 

(g) He shall be empowered to allocate responsibilities and to audit 
the performance of officers, faculty members and staff against 
the expected standards; 

(h) He shall exercise all administrative and financial powers 
considered necessary for smooth and efficient functioning of the 
University; 

(i) He shall pass such orders and take such measures that are 
necessary to implement any of the above powers and duties; 

(Gj) He shall be empowered to appoint or engage members of 
teaching and non-teaching employees possessing necessary 
qualifications for a period not exceeding one year to meet the 
urgent requirement of the University; 

(k) The Vice-Chancellor may delegate such powers and duties for 
day-to-day work to such person as deemed fit. 


(4) Emoluments: 


(1) The Vice-Chancellor shall be entitled to such emoluments, 


164 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


benefits and facilities and shall be subjected to other conditions 
of service as laid out in the Act and Statutes; 


(2) In addition: 


(a) The Vice-Chancellor shall be entitled to have water, power and 
rent free furnished residential accommodation; 


(b) The Vice-Chancellor shall be entitled to the facility of a free 
official car with driver, mobile phone, free telephone (with 
Subscriber Trunk Dialling (STD) and International Subscriber 
Dialling (ISD)) service at his residence; 

(c) The Vice-Chancellor shall be entitled to one cook and two 
attendants at his residence; 

(d) To ensure safety and security, the Vice-Chancellor shall be 
provided with security guards as necessary; 

(e) The Vice-Chancellor shall be eligible for Travel allowance or 
Composite Transfer Grant on joining and on completion of his 
term, and joining time pay as per the Government of India 
norms. 


(5) Leave: The Vice-Chancellor shall be entitled to leave benefits as 
laid out in the Act and Statutes. In addition: 

(a) The leave at the credit of the Vice-Chancellor at the close of the 
previous half-year shall be carried forward to the new a half 
year, subject to the condition that the leave, so carried forward 
plus the credit for that half year, does not exceed the maximum 
limit of three hundred days; 

(b) The Vice-Chancellor shall be entitled to the leave salary and 
leave salary encashment admissible as per the Government of 
India norms; 

(c) The Vice-Chancellor shall also be entitled to avail himself of 
Extra-Ordinary Leave as per the Government of India norms; 

(d) When the Vice-Chancellor wishes to avail himself any kind of 
leave more than one week in duration, he shall intimate the same 
to the Chancellor, provided that if the Vice-Chancellor goes 
abroad, an intimation in this regard shall also be sent to the 
Ministry of Railways along with the Chancellor, irrespective of 
the duration and this shall be reported to the Executive Council 
at its next meeting; 

(e) In the case of any absence of the Vice-Chancellor occasioned by 
any call by the Central or State Government, Public Service, or 
on deputation on behalf of the University for any public purpose, 
the period so spent shall be treated as on duty; 

(f) Where an employee of the University is appointed as the Vice- 
Chancellor, he shall be allowed to avail himself of any leave at 
his credit before his appointment as the Vice-Chancellor, and, in 
the event of his re-joining the old post, he shall be entitled to 
carry back the leave at his credit to the new post; 

(g) He may be allowed to contribute to the provident fund of which 
he is a member and the University shall contribute to the account 
of such person in that provident fund at the same rate at which 
the person had been contributing immediately before his

(1) Eligibility: 

(a) For appointment as Pro Vice-Chancellor of the University, 
the person shall possess the highest level of competence, 
integrity, morals and institutional commitment. 

(b) He shall be a distinguished academician, with a minimum of 
eight years of experience as Professor in a University system or 
eight years of research experience on an equivalent position in a 
reputed Academia or Industry or Research and Development 
organisation. 


(2) Emoluments: 

(a) The pay of the Pro Vice-Chancellor of the University, shall be 
fixed at academic level-14 in the pay matrix or academic level- 
15 in the pay matrix of 7" Central Pay Commission, as the case 
may be, with a special allowance as prescribed by the Executive 
Council (from time to time) and Pro Vice-Chancellor shall be 
eligible for Transfer allowance or Composite Transfer Grant on 
joining and on completion of his term, and joining time pay as 
per the Government of India norms; 


(b) Unless otherwise specified, when a person, employed in another 
organisation, is appointed as the Pro Vice-chancellor on 
deputation, the rules of deputation as admissible to an employee 
in the Government of India shall be applicable. 


(3) Leave: 

The Pro Vice-Chancellor shall be entitled to all kinds of leave 
applicable to whole time permanent teachers of the University. 

In addition: 

(a) If he is a Professor of the University discharging the additional 
duties of the Pro-Vice-Chancellor, or is a person not in the 
service of the University, but appointed to the post, he shall be 
entitled to leave on full pay for one eleventh of the period spent 
by him on active service as Pro Vice-Chancellor; 

(b) Where a person already in the service of the University is 
appointed as a Pro Vice-Chancellor, he shall be entitled to carry 
forward the leave at his credit on the date of such appointment. 


(1) The Registrar shall be appointed as per the provisions in the 
Statutes: 

Provided that where an employee of this University or 
any other Institution or Government and its organisations is 
appointed as Registrar while maintaining his lien, he shall 
continue to be governed by the retirement benefit schemes of the 
parent organisation to which he was entitled prior to his 
appointment as Registrar. 

(2) In case the Registrar is appointed on deputation from the 
Government or any other Organisation or institution, the terms

(4) Registrar is normally expected to stay within the campus and

term, and joining time pay as per the Government of India norms 
and the Leave Encashment shall be payable as per extant the 
Government of India norms. 

(8) The Registrar shall have the power to enter into agreements, sign 
documents and authenticate records on behalf of the University 
and shall exercise such powers and perform such duties, as may 
be prescribed by the Statutes as directed by the Vice-Chancellor.

be empowered to remove the name from the rolls and readmit a 
student in the school concerned; 

be empowered to withhold the Hall Ticket of student of the 
school for valid reasons, and 

exercise such other powers and perform such other functions, as 
may be delegated or assigned to him.

(1) The Affiliation and Recognition Board shall be responsible for 
admitting colleges and institutions to the privileges of the 
University. 

(2) The board shall form enabling Regulations in this regard, from 
time to time. 

(3) Procedure for Recognition of an Institution (i) such 
institutions as are desirous of seeking recognition from Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya shall submit a proposal detailing their 
courses or programs for which they want recognition for award 
of degree, diploma and certificate by the University and along 
with the proposal, they shall also send a profile of the Institute 
highlighting its broad aims and objects, domain expertise as the 
like physical and academic infrastructure, including availability 
of workshops or laboratories, manpower position besides the 
adequacy of financial resources at its disposal: 


Provided that such institutions may be located anywhere 
within the country or abroad: 


Provided further that such entities may be funded by the 
government or other agencies except that they shall be not-for- 
profit organisations and the source of its funding shall be through 
legal and government approved means; 


(ii) the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya shall consider the proposal to 
see, if, there is any, prima facie case to proceed further and, on 
its satisfaction, shall remit the same to the concerned School or 
Centre for their consideration and recommendations; 


(iii) based on the response of the School or Centre, a decision shall be 
taken by the Dean Academic Affairs and Vice-Chancellor to 
either regret the request or to place the matter for consideration 
of the Academic Council; 


(iv) the Academic Council, after deliberation, may approve the 
proposal or decide to appoint a three-member expert committee 
to visit the organisation and submit its recommendations to the 
Council for deciding the further course of action; 


(v) in cases where the proposals are accepted ‘in principle’, specific 
Ordinances. shall be prepared for consideration and 
recommendations of the Academic Council, which shall attain 
finality after the Ordinance is approved by the Executive Council 
and is duly notified; 


(vi) after an Institution or Organisation is given recognition by the 
University, the degree or diploma or certificate shall be awarded 
by Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya as per the academic Ordinance 
of such a degree, diploma, certificate as the like and the degree, 


172 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


diploma, certificate awarded shall contain the name of the 
organisation in whose collaboration the said course or Program 
was conducted. 


(4) Broad framework for Recognition of Institutions: (i) the 
academic or research Programs of the institution seeking 
recognition shall be in an area compatible with the academic and 
research programs of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya; it shall 
be of relevance to similar objectives already being pursued in the 
University so that reciprocal benefits might accrue and the 
institution might get linked with the academic pursuits of the 
University; 


(ii) the Institution shall have core academic staff and have a 
system by which people of eminence shall be associated 
with its academic programs and the academic level of the 
staff shall broadly be at par with the teaching or workshop 
training personnel in the University; 


(ii) the Institution shall have sufficient funds to support its 
academic activities and well-equipped departments, library, 
training facilities, etc.; 


(iv) the Institutions seeking recognition, from relatively less 
developed areas or regions of the country but having 
necessary potential to come up in some way to the desired 
level of the academic and allied programs of the University 
not usually covered by the universities in the region, shall be 
given adequate consideration within the framework of the 
criteria laid down by the University for according 
recognition to the institutions; 


(v) recognition granted shall normally not exceed five years and 
shall be subject to a mid-term review the manner and timing 
of which shall be as decided by the University; 


(vi) recognition may be extended after five years for subsequent 
terms of five years each; 


Provided that the continuing instructions and 
research schedule shall not be disturbed for the reason of 
expiry of the term of recognition alone: 


Provided further that normally no new registration 
for research or initiation of an instructional semester shall be 
initiated by the University during the last six months of the 
period of recognition; 


(vii) the request for re-recognition or renewal of recognition shall] 
be received at least six months in advance from the date of 
expiry of the period and shall be examined as per the 
procedure already detailed above; 


(viii) the University shall reserve the right to withdraw such 
recognition with immediate effect if any falsification of facts 
is found at a later stage or any fact comes to light that is

(ix) all legal disputes arising between the parties shall be subject

(5) Cooperation and Collaboration with other Universities or 
Institutions, etc.: 


(i) The Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya shall network and collaborate 
with Institutions of higher education, research Institutions, 
industry, ‘persons of eminence’ and or non-governmental 
organisations of national and international repute, subject to the 
applicable Regulations of the regulatory authorities, as issued 
from time to time; 

(ii) the objective of such networking and collaboration shall be to 
further teaching, research, extension, consultancy aimed at 
providing opportunities of enhanced learning, hands on 
experience, cross-cultural dialogue and exchange of ideas for the 
faculty, research scholars and students, and to consider offering 
joint degree programs, Indian as well as foreign, subject to the 
Regulation by regulatory authorities if any on the subject; 

(iii) the University may enter into exchange programs of teachers and 
students, sharing of course and instruction materials, sharing of 
credits, and holding of joint workshops, joints research projects 
funded by national and international agencies, and providing 
access to each other’s facilities, and to institute joint academic 
degrees in accordance with the standards recognised by the 
regulatory bodies of the respective countries; 

(iv) the University may collaborate for establishment of University’s 
centers abroad, may develop e-courses for interactive and 
integrated learning, sharing of library, laboratory and other 
learning resources or any other activity of mutual benefit, subject 
to the Regulation by regulatory authorities, if any on the subject; 

(v) the University may enter into contracts, including Memorandum 
of Understanding with other institutions of national and 
international repute for the purpose outlined hereinabove; and 

(vi) all Memorandum of Understandings are to be for a specified time 
of period and shall be reviewed periodically at appropriate level 
and any matter not specifically covered in the above shall be 
regulated as per the University rules as existing and amended 
from time to time;

14, Constitution, powers and (1) Each School shall have a School Board which shall consist of the 
nes of the school following members, namely: - 
: (a) Dean of the School who shall be the Chairperson; 

(b) Heads of the Departments in the School; 

(c) Directors of the Centres in the School; 

(d) All Professors in the School;

the School to be appointed by rotation; and 

(f) Four to six members to be nominated by the Vice- 
Chancellor from amongst the academic staff of the 
University, reputed experts from outside of the University 

(2) The Board shall perform as per guidelines set by the Executive 
Council from time to time, upon recommendation of the Vice- 
Chancellor. 

(3) Meetings of the School Board: 

(a) At least two ordinary meetings of the School Board shall be 
held in an Academic year; one in each Semester; 

(b) The Dean may convene special meetings of the Board at his 
own initiative or at the suggestion of the Vice-Chancellor or 
on a written requisition from at least one-fifth of the 
members of the Board; 

(4) The quorum for the meeting of the Board shall be one-third of its 
total members. 

(5) Notice for any meeting of the Board shall be issued at least seven 
days in advance of the date and the Dean may, however, convene 
emergency meeting of the Board at a short notice.

16. Duties and powers of the (1) The Head of Department shall be responsible for the overall 
headiot deparanent- academic quality of their respective school or department 
including publication, research work and training Programs. 
(2) The Head of the Department shall be responsible for preparation 
and review of the syllabus for various programs under their 
respective school or department in consultation with faculty and 
school board members. 
(3) The Head of the Department shall: 
(a) ensure proper allocation of class rooms and issue notices for 
changes, if any; 
(b) ensure that all class rooms are sufficiently equipped and the 
Head of the department shall intimate any defects to the 
Deputy Registrar or Assistant Registrar or employee of 
equivalent rank for necessary action; 
(c) ensure that adequate number of good quality laboratory 
equipment are available for experiments; 
(d) periodically review laboratory requirements and propose for 
necessary up-gradation in the facilities to meet the industry 
needs, if necessary. 
(4) The Head of the Department shall; - 
(a) ensure that students adhere to the Model Code of Conduct for 
Students, Dress code and Class attendance or leave rules; 

(b)to arrange for all documentation or data entry relating to 
examinations are done within the stipulated time frame and 
verify for its correctness; 

(c) ensure that students maintain discipline in the Campus; 

(d) encourage students to participate in co-curricular and extra- 

curricular activities; 

(e) facilitate students’ requests relating to — 

(i) Scholarships; 
(ii) Attestations; 
(iii) | Bonafide certificates; 
(iv) Examination applications; 
(v) Industrial visits; 
(vi) Any other student requests; and 

(vii) Maintain a record of students’ performance in 

examinations to identify poorly performing students and 
the Head of the department shall counsel such students 
and provide all necessary assistance for their academic 
improvement. 

(5) The Head of the Departments shall have following duties in 
faculty matters, namely; - 

(a) Motivate Faculty members to undertake research work, 
publications to improve Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya’s 


176 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


academic standing; 

(b) Prepare the work plan or time table for Faculty members 
well in advance before commencement of the semester; 

(c) Communicate the students’ feedback to the faculty members. 

(d) Ensure discipline among Faculty members; 

(e) Coordinate with Vice-Chancellor and Dean about any 
administrative requirements for engaging regular or contract 
or Visiting Faculty and other academic support staff; 

(f) Oversee the functioning of the training section; 

(g) Coordinate with other Heads of the Departments on all 
academic issues; 

(6) The Head of the department shall perform any other duties 
assigned by the Vice-Chancellor. 


17. Board of studies.- (1) The Board of Studies for each department shall have the 
following members, namely: - 

(a) head of the Department or Centre, who shall be the 
Chairperson and Convenor and in his absence, the senior 
most Professor of the department shall be the chairperson 
and convenor; 

(b) dean of the School concerned or his nominee; 

(c) all the Professors of the Department or Centre (ex-officio 
members); 

(d) one Associate Professor of the Department, by rotation; 

(e) one to Two members to be nominated by the Dean of the 
School from reputed experts from outside of the University. 

(2) The term of office of the members, other than the ex-officio 
members shall be three years. 

(3) The board of studies shall ordinarily meet at least twice a year 
and on such occasions as may be directed by the Vice- 
Chancellor or the Dean of the School. 

(4) The notice for a meeting of the Board of Studies, other than a 
special meeting, shall ordinarily be issued at least three days 
before the due scheduled date. 

(5) The quorum for the meetings of the Board of Studies shall be 
50% of the members of the Board of Studies. 


18. Medium of instruction.- (1) The medium of instructions in respect of all courses conducted in 
the Schools and in the colleges or Institutions admitted to the 
privileges of the University shall be in English. 

(2) The University shall make efforts to align with the National 
Education Policy 2020.

(2) Academic Calendar: 

(a) (4) The Academic Session shall normally commence in the first 

week of August every year and each Academic Session shall 
consist of two regular semesters and may have a Summer- 
Term, apart from vacations, and mid-semester breaks. 
(ii) Each regular semester shall consist of approximately 
twenty-weeks duration reflecting the credit requirements for the 
academic programs (including minimum fourteen weeks of 
classes, one week of mid-semester recess, and up to three 
weeks of mid-semester and end-semester examinations); 

(b) The exact dates for the important academic events scheduled 
during the Academic Session shall be specified in the 
Academic Calendar and the dates for the particular events shall 
be specified; 

(c) Registration, late Registration, commencement of classes, 
adding and dropping of courses, submission of documents, 
submission of fees, examinations, submission of grades; 
conversion of "I" Grades, vacations, mid-semester breaks; 

(d) The Academic Calendar shall be published on the University’s 
website and the any change therein during the session, shall 
require prior approval of the Academic Council. 


(3) Program Structure: 

(a) Every Program shall have a curriculum and course contents 
for the courses approved by the Academic Council. 
Curriculum consists of Foundation, Core and Elective 
courses further divided into tracks such as domain, 
multidisciplinary and professional development;

(4) Admission: 
The number of seats in each branch of the Program of Study for 
which admission is to be made in Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
as well as admission process shall be decided by the Academic 
Council. 


(5) Reservations 

(a) The University shall follow and adhere to the norms notified 
by the Government of India on reservation of seats for 
admissions and the University shall strive to be socially 
inclusive, and seek to implement affirmative actions; 

(b) The eligibility criteria, shall be set out in an information 
brochure to be made available to the applicants through the 
University website.

(7) Validity of admission and its cancellation: 

(a) Admission to any undergraduate Programme requires that the 
applicant fulfil all three of the following conditions, namely: - 

(i) be eligible; 
(ii) go through the laid down admission procedure; 
(iii) pay the prescribed fees as per deadline; 

(b) All admissions to Undergraduate and Postgraduate Programs 
shall be formally approved by the Admission Committee; 

(c) All students admitted provisionally or otherwise to any Program 
shall submit copies of their mark sheets, provisional certificates, 
of the qualifying examination and other documents by the last 
date specified for the purpose in the Academic Calendar. 

(d) The Admission Committee shall cancel the admission of any 
student who fails to submit the prescribed documents by the 
specified date or to meet other stipulated requirement and may 
also cancel admission at any later time if it is found that the 
student had supplied false information or suppressed relevant 
information while seeking admission; 

(e) In matters of admission to any Undergraduate or Postgraduate 
Programs, the decision of the Admission Committee shall be 
final. 


(1) Change of Branch rules shall be reviewed and revised by the 
Academic Council. 

(2) Change of Branch shall apply to the Undergraduate programs, 
and cannot be claimed as a matter of right. 

(3) Normally a student admitted to a particular branch of the 
undergraduate program shall continue studying in that branch till 
completion. 

(4) In special cases the University, at its discretion, may permit a 
student to change from one branch of studies to another after the 
first year, to fill vacant seats in any branch in relation to its 
sanctioned strength and enable students to pursue their preferred 
branch of study, such changes shall be permitted, in accordance 
with the provisions laid down hereinafter,- 

(a) Only those students shall be considered eligible for change 
of branch after the second Semester, who have, 
(i) completed all the credits required in the first year of 


180 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


their study in the first attempt; 
(ii) have not been subjected to any disciplinary action; 

(b) Applications for a change of branch must be made by 
intending eligible students in the prescribed form and such 
a form shall be submitted at the end of the first year of 
their study, and the completed forms must be submitted by 
the last date specified in the notification; 

(c) Students may enlist their choices of branch, in order of 
preference, to which they wish to change over and it shall 
not be permissible to alter the choices after the application 
has been submitted; 

(d) Change of branch shall be made strictly in order of merit 
of the applicants provided that for this purpose, the 
Cumulative Grade Point Average obtained at the end of 
the first year shall be considered and in case of a tie, the 
admission test rank of the applicants shall be considered. 

(5) The students who have scored 9.0 Cumulative Grade Point 
Average in the Ist year be allowed a change of branch with the 
condition that the overall strength of the branch do not exceed 
20% of the approved strength of the branch. 

(6) All changes of branch made in accordance with the above rules 
shall be effective from the start of the second year of the 
applicants concerned and no change of branch shall be permitted 
after this. 

(7) All changes of branch shall be final and binding on the 
applicants and no student shall be permitted, under any 
circumstances, to refuse the change of branch chosen. 


21. Registration, attendance (1) Registration.- 
ane temporary A student must be registered with the University in order to 
withdrawal of students.- : : ; 
pursue an academic program of study. Registration shall be 
ensured through fulfilment of eligibility criteria and pre- 
requisites and timely payment of prescribed fees in accordance 
with timelines announced by the University. 


(2) Validation of Registration.- 
Before the commencement of classes of each term, on a date 
specified in the Term Schedule, every student is required to be 
present on campus and validate his registration. 


(3) Registration and Fee Payment: 
(a) Every registered student must pay the stipulated fees in full 
before the specified deadlines. 
(b) In the event that a student does not make these payments, he 
shall be de-registered from all courses and his name shall be 
struck off from the rolls. 


(4) Late Registration: 

(a) For reasons beyond his control, if a student is not able to 
register or send an authorised representative with a medical 
certificate, he may apply to the Dean (Academics) for late 
registration. 

(b) Dean (Academics) may consider and approve late registration 


[भाग पा--_खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 181 


in genuine cases on payment of a late registration fee and late 
registration is permitted until a date specified in the term 
schedule, typically one week after the beginning of the 
semester. 


(5) Attendance Rules: 

(a) It is mandatory for the students to attend all classes and 
attendance records of all students for each course shall be 
maintained; 


(b) (0) The Program Coordinator shall reiterate the class policy on 
attendance with respect to grading at the beginning of the term 
and this shall be done keeping in mind the importance of 
classroom learning in the teaching-learning process. 


(ii) Once the class attendance policy has been made clear to all 
the students registered for the course, the Program Coordinator 
shall implement the same in totality; 

(c) For the purpose of attendance calculation, every scheduled class 
shall count as one unit irrespective of the number of contact 
hours; 


(d) (i) Attendance record shall be maintained in every scheduled 
lecture, tutorial, and practical class. 


(ii) Students are required to strictly adhere to and comply with 
any method or device employed by the Course Coordinator and 
Instructor for purpose of Attendance Recording. 


(iii) Failure to do so may call for disciplinary action and the 
Program Coordinator shall maintain and consolidate attendance 
record for the course (lectures, tutorials and practical classes 
together, as applicable); 


(e) A Program Coordinator may choose any one or more of the 
following as attendance policy, namely: - 


(i) the Program Coordinator shall assign 10% of the total marks 
to surprise quizzes; 

(ii) if attendance of the student is greater than 90%, result of the 
best two or three quizzes shall be considered, else average of 
all quizzes shall be considered; 

(iii) the Program Coordinator may allocate specific marks for 
participation in discussions in the class on a regular basis; 

(iv) ifastudent’s attendance is less than 75%, the student shall be 
awarded one grade less than the actual grade that he has 
earned. For example, a student who has got B grade but has 
attendance less than 75% shall be awarded C grade. 

(v) a student shall not get P grade for an audit course if his 
attendance is less than 75% and the Program Coordinator 


182 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


shall implement any other attendance policy provided the 
policy is approved by the Dean, Academics; 

(vi) if a student remains absent without sanctioned leave for more 
than 25% of the classes, then the course instructor may 
recommend deregistration of the student from the course to 
the Head of Department or Centre or School or the Vice- 
Chancellor and if accepted, the student shall not be graded 
for the respective course; 


(f) Other implications of attendance,- 

(i) if a student’s attendance is less than 75% in more than two 
courses without any valid reason in a term, he is liable to be 
issued warning and put under probation. 

(ii) If this is repeated, the matter is liable to be referred to The 
Vice-Chancellor for action which may even result in 
expulsion; 

(iii) if a student’s attendance is less than 75% in any course or 
Cumulative Grade Point Average is less than 7.0, then he is 
liable to be barred from holding any position of 
responsibility in the hostel or University in the next term. 


(6) Continuous absence and Registration Status.- 
If a student is absent from the University for more than four 
weeks without notifying the Dean (Academics) or Head of 
Department, his registration shall be terminated and name shall 
be removed from the University rolls. 

(7) Temporary Withdrawal: 
(a) Semester withdrawal and absence for a semester shall be 
granted under different conditions, namely: - 


(0) medical and personal grounds; 
(1) industrial internship; 
(ili) exchange or deputation to another academic institution 
in India or abroad; and 
(iv) disciplinary conditions on application; 


(b) The conditions as per the following shall be clearly specified 

in the application; 

(1) semester withdrawal reflects the condition, in which a 
student is forced to withdraw from all courses in the 
Semester for medical conditions and a student shall apply 
for semester withdrawal if he has missed considerable 
teaching days on these grounds and an application for 
semester withdrawal shall not be accepted after the 
commencement of major tests and a student is not permitted 
to request for term withdrawal with retrospective effect; 

(ii) Semester leave indicates the situation in which a student is 
permitted to take one or more Semesters off for industrial 
internship or any other assignment with prior approval and 
planning and the application is to be routed through his 
advisor or Programme coordinator and Head of the 
concerned Department or Centre or School; 

(iii) Vice-Chancellor is the final approving authority for such 
requests and all such applications must be processed before 
the beginning of the Semester in which the leave shall be 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 183 


taken and these terms shall not be counted towards the 
maximum permitted time period for completion of the 
degree; 

(iv) when a student registers at another academic institution in 
India or abroad with the expectation of credit transfer or 
research work through a _ pre-approved arrangement 
including a Memorandum of Understanding, the student 
shall be considered as being on a Semester Exchange and 
the period shall be counted towards the total period 
permitted for the degree; 

(v) when a student is suspended for one or more Semesters on 
disciplinary grounds, the student status shall be called 
deregistered or disciplinary withdrawal period. 


22. Fee structure.- (1) For each academic session, the University shall prescribe and 
announce the fees structure and timelines for payment of fees 
and the fee component shall include Admission Fees, Caution 
Deposit (Refundable), Registration Fees, Tuition Fees, Campus 
Facilities Charges, Hostel and Mess Fees, Electricity Charges, 
Student Benevolent Fund, Study Materials or Case Study 
charges, Sports Facilities Fees, Examination Fees, Convocation 
Fees, Documents and Certificates Fees, Transcript Fees, 
Supplementary Exam Fees, Re-admission Fees, etc. 

(2) The students shall necessarily comply with the timelines for the 
fees payment. 

(3) The University reserves the right to revise the fee from time to 
time, with the approval of the Executive Council. 


(4) If a student chooses to withdraw from the Program of study in 
which he is enrolled, the University shall follow the refund 
policy as announced by the regulatory authorities every year,- 

(a) caution deposit shall be refunded in all cases at the end of 
the Program, after adjusting any pending dues; 

(b) hostel and Mess fees (lodging and boarding) shall be 
refunded on pro-rata basis and the part of month shall be 
calculated as full month; 

(c) Campus facilities charges shall be refunded on pro-rata basis 
and the part of month shall be calculated as full month; 

(d) An application for refund along with original fee receipt and 
admission offer letter to be submitted; 

(e) fees shall be refunded within thirty working days from the 
date of receipt the application.

(10) When a student gets the grade ‘U’ in any subject during a term, 
the Grade Point Average and the cumulative grade point 
average from that term onwards shall be tentatively calculated, 
taking only ‘zero point’ for each such ‘U’ grade and after the 
‘U’ grade has or have been substituted by better grades during 
a subsequent term, the grade point average and the cumulative 
grade point average of all the terms, starting from the earliest 
term in which the ‘U’ grade has been updated, shall be 
recomputed and recorded to take this change of grade into 
account. 

(11) In certain courses, particularly in the case of non-credit 
courses, a grade of only pass or fail may apply and in such 
circumstances, a student shall receive a grade ‘P’ for passing 
the course or a grade ‘U’ for failing the course. In case a grade 
of only pass or fail applies to a credit course, P shall stand for 
10 grade points per credit for pass and 0 grade points for fail. 

(12) Conversion of cumulative grade point average into percentage 
marks: In case of a specific query by students or employers 
regarding conversion of cumulative grade point average into 
percentage marks, the following formula shall be adopted for 
notional conversion of cumulative grade point average into 
percentage marks. 

(13) Formula: % Marks = Cumulative Grade Point Average x 9.5 

(14) Grade Card: 

The grade card issued at the end of the semester to each student shall 
contain the following: 


(i) the name of the course and the credits for each course 
registered in that term; 
(ii) the letter grade and grade point obtained in each course; 
(iii) the total number of credits earned by the student up to the 
end of that term in each of the course categories; 
(iv) the Cumulative Grade Point Average of all the courses taken 
from the first term. 


(15) Promotion and Discontinuation of Study: 
(1)A_ student shall fulfil the following conditions for 
registration,- 

(a) Cleared half of the registered credits of the preceding 
Semesters (after supplementary examinations). 

(b) If the above condition is not fulfilled, student shall have 
to repeat that Semester by registering for the subjects 
with grade U or I. 

(c) If after completion of the 4" Semester, the student fails to 
clear 2/3 of the credits registered in the first two 
Semesters, the student shall be counselled to pursue 
different academic pathways. 

(2)The student must register the backlog courses first giving 


188 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


25. 


Examinations.- 


priority to the oldest backlogs and the students shall register 
by default for backlog courses being offered in a particular 
Semester. 

(3) The student shall be allowed to register for any course only 
after he has cleared all pre-requisites for that course. 

(4) For students having more than one backlog in a Semester the 
registered credit in that Semester inclusive of backlog subjects 
must not exceed the prescribe and the credits of internship and 
field trips shall be excluded while calculating credit limit and 
the final year students may be allowed to take prescribed 
credits and, in addition, two backlog subjects in a Semester 
provided he has a cumulative grade point average of 5.00 and 
above. 

(5) The candidate earns the degree on completing the prescribed 
course work and clearing the minimum required credits with a 
minimum cumulative grade point average of 5.00.

The Audit Courses are the ones the students may choose to

not be counted for semester grade point average or cumulative

The Program Coordinator may permit a student to register for an

The word "AU" shall be written alongside the course name in the 
Grade Card and the audit course shall not carry any credits and 
grade points and an Audit Course outcome shall be shown in the 
grade card as "P" for passed candidates. 


In assessing the student's performance in subjects, the system 
of continuous assessment is adopted by the University and in 
conformity with this practice, faculty members are empowered 
to adopt the modes of assessment that they deem the most 
suitable for testing students’ grasp on the subject and 
measuring their performance. 

Modes of Assessment (Theory or Laboratory or Project or 
Seminar): Evaluation for the course may include class tests or 
quizzes, written reports, oral presentations, viva voce, projects 
submissions, case studies, individual or group assignments, 
mid-semester, end-semester examinations, and _ other 
assessment tools and the Academic Council may prescribe any 
or all components as mandatory for a student, including 
minimum pass percentage for the end semester examination. 
The weightage assigned to different components of assessment 
shall be announced by the concerned faculty member in the 
beginning of the Semester, preferably in the course outline. 

A student must fulfil all the requirements of continuous 
assessment process as prescribed by the faculty or co- 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 189 


ordination committee of the subject and if due to any 

compelling reason (such as his illness, calamity in the family, 

etc.) a student fails to meet any of the requirements on the 
scheduled date and time, the teacher or co-ordination 
committee in consultation with the concerned Head of the 

Department may take such steps (including conduction of 

compensatory tests or examinations) as are deemed fit. 

(5) For the benefit of and as a process of learning by the students, 
the scripts after correction of all class tests, assignments, mid- 
Semester examinations, end-semester examinations shall be 
shown to the students within fifteen days from the last date of 
tests or mid-semester or end-semester examination. Students 
shall point out discrepancies in the evaluation by the instructor, 
if any, on the spot and no claims whatsoever shall be 
entertained afterwards. 

(6) Minimum 30 percent marks in the end-term examination is 
essential to qualify for passing the course. 

(7) The final grades for a subject must be submitted after the end- 
semester examination by the concerned faculty member or 
Instructor to the office of Controller of Examinations. 

(8) A student may be debarred from appearing in the end Semester 
examination due to the following reasons: 

(a) If he has ceased to be a registered student (for example has 
not cleared University and hostel dues up to that Semester, 
disciplinary matter); 

(b) If his attendance in the Lecture or Tutorial or Studio or 
Practical classes is inadequate during the Semester as per 
University rules. 

(9) Supplementary or Make-up Examination: 

(1) A student who earns an ‘U’ or ‘IT in any core course shall 
have to appear for a supplementary or make-up exam and 
must secure a pass grade in that course and make-up exam 
shall be offered only in case of genuine reasons such as 
medical with due evidence. 

(2) A student who has not appeared in the end-term or final 
evaluation of a particular course shall be awarded ‘U’ or 17 
grade, as applicable, in that course, irrespective of the marks 
obtained by him in the mid-term examination and other 
components of the Continuous Evaluation Scheme. 

(3) The supplementary or makeup examination shall normally 
be offered before or during the subsequent term, a student 
was deregistered in the previous semester shall have to 
register for that course again in the following semester(s). 

(4) A student is eligible for only one supplementary or makeup 
examination in a course to clear the backlog, in case a 
student fails in the Supplementary or makeup examination, 
he has to register for that course again (including payment 
of the prescribed fees) in a regular term or during vacation 
term and repeat the course by attending the classes and 
exams. 

(5) The end term exam alone shall alone be substituted by the 
supplementary or makeup examination. The sessional marks 


190 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


(i.e. quiz marks etc.) earned by the student during the term 
shall be taken into account for calculating the final marks. 

(6) University at its discretion may conduct remedial or 
refresher classes for students failing in a course. 

(7) The student who passes the course after supplementary 
exam in a course in which he secured ‘U’ or ‘I’ grade, shall 
be awarded one grade lower than the grade he shall have 
obtained with those marks in regular exam, unless the grade 
is E. 

(10) Any exception to the Supplementary Exam policy may be 
referred to the Vice-Chancellor for approval. 


26. Ensuring fair (1) The University stands for absolute and impeccable integrity in 
examinaHons: all the examinations (including all components of assessments of 

a course) and shall ensure the credibility and academic sanctity. 

(2) Cases adopting unfair means in an examination shall be dealt 
with by the committee on prevention of examination 
malpractices. 

(3) A candidate shall not use unfair means in connection with any 
examination. 

(4) The following shall constitute and deemed to be adoption of 
unfair means or practices during examinations, namely: - 

(a) in possession of incriminating materials related or unrelated 
to the subject of the examination concerned; 

(b) paper leaking; 

(c) unauthorised access (or attempts thereof) to the University 
systems, with an intention to gain access to the examination 
papers, answer-scripts; 

(d) influencing an examiner (or instructor) to gain undue 
favours; 

(e) copying in any form (for example either from the possessed 
material or other examinees or internet); 

(f) inter-changing of answer scripts; 

(g) change of seat for copying; 

(h) trying to benefit other candidates for unfair practices; 

(i) consulting fellow examinees; 

(j) exchange of answer sheets or relevant materials; 

(k) writing some other candidate’s register number in the main 
answer paper; 

(1) insertion of pre-written answer sheets (Main sheets or 
Additional Sheets); 

(m) threatening the invigilator or insubordinate behavior as 
reported by the Chief Superintendent and or the Hall 
Superintendent; 

(n) consulting the invigilator for answering the questions in the 
examination; 

(0) impersonation; 

(p) mass copying; and 

(q) use of social media and other technology platforms or 
advances during the exams or any evaluation components 
(viz., projects or assignments, etc). 

(5) The Academic Council may periodically review 16 


[AMT 1--खण्ड 4] 


भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 19] 


examination process with an intention to define and uphold the 
highest values and transparency. 

(6) An “Examination Disciplinary Committee” shall be set up by 
the Controller of Examinations. 

(7) When an unfair practice is observed, the matter shall be 
immediately reported to the Controller of Examination along 
with the related documents, evidences, etc. 

(a) the Examination Disciplinary Committee shall conduct the 
investigation and make necessary recommendations for the 
approvals by the competent authority; 

(b) if the Controller of Examinations is satisfied that there has 
been a mass-scale copying or use of unfair means on a mass- 
scale, he may report the matter to the competent authority to 
cancel the examination of all the candidates concerned and 
order re- examination.

Every student is required to observe discipline and decorous 
behavior both inside and outside the campus and not to indulge 
in any activity which shall bring down the reputation of the 
University. 

The following shall constitute gross violation of the code of 
conduct and are liable to disciplinary actions: 


The University has a zero-tolerance policy towards ragging and 

is strictly prohibited inside and outside the University campus. 

ragging is a criminal and non-available offence as per the law 
and the University shall follow the guidelines on ragging issued 
by the Hon’ble Supreme Court of India and University Grants 

Commission. 

The University has a zero-tolerance policy against sexual 

harassment, which is widely condemned as a form of human 

rights violation, an infringement on life and liberty and a grave 
form of gender-based discrimination and sexual harassment of 
any level is a punishable offence. 

(a) Sexual harassment of any kind which shall also include: 
unwelcome sexual proposition or advancements, sexually 
graphic comments of a body, unwelcome touching, patting, 
pinching or leering of parts of the body or persistent offensive 
or unwelcome sexual jokes and comments; 

(b) eve-teasing or disrespectful behavior or any misbehavior with 


[PART II—SEc.4] 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 193 


a girl student, woman staff member or visitor. 


(5) Offences against persons with disability: 
(a) intentionally insults or intimidates with intent to humiliate 
a person with disability in any place within public view; 
(b) assaults or uses force to any person with disability with 
intent to dishonor him or outrage the modesty of a woman with 
disability; 
(c) having the actual charge or control over a person with 
disability voluntarily or knowingly denies food or fluids to him; 
(d) being in a position to dominate of a child or woman with 
disability and uses that position to exploit her sexually; 
(e) voluntarily injures, damages or interferes with the use of 
any limb or sense or any supporting device of a person with 
disability 
(f) | these offences shall be punishable as per the Rights of a 
Persons with Disabilities Act, 2016 (. 49 of 2016). 
(6) Furnishing false statement of any kind in the form of application 
for admission or for award of scholarship, etc. 
(7) Act of verbal abuse or physical assault. 
(8) Threatening fellow students, staff, faculty and authorities of the 
universities, including any third party workers deployed in the 
University or hostels. 


(9) Adoption of unfair means in the examinations. 

(10) Displaying lack of courtesy and decorum; resorting to indecent 
behavior anywhere within or outside the campus. 

(11) Damaging or stealthily removing any property or belongings of 
the University, or fellow students. 

(12) Possession, consumption or distribution of alcoholic drinks or 
any kind of banned drugs or smoking inside the campus as well 
as in public areas. 

(13) Possession of firearms or any other lethal weapon. 

(14) Tampering with any fire extinguisher or fire alarm system, or 
any activity compromising security and safety within the 
University or hostels premises. 

(15) Gambling of any kind. 

(16) Littering in the campus. 

(17) Organising or participating in any group activity in company 
with others in or outside the campus without prior permission of 
the Dean of Students’ Affairs or Warden. 

(18) Mutilation, theft or unauthorised possession of library books. 

(19) Resorting to noisy and unseemly behavior, disturbing studies of 
fellow students, disturbing peaceful and tranquil atmosphere of 
the University or hostels. 

(20) Misuse of Internet or e-mail facilities or tempering or hacking 
with servers anywhere in the University, including access to 
pornography and banned sites. 

(21) Interacting with media (any form of media, including social 
media) without express approval of the competent authority of 
the University, on matters related to the University affairs. 

(22) Forming, or attempting to form, any association or union that is 
unrecognised by the University. 


194 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


(23) Not intimating his absence to the Warden of the hostel before 
availing any leave. 

(24) Arousing communal, caste or regional feeling or creating 
disharmony among students or employees. 

(25) Any act of moral turpitude. 

(26) Damaging or defacing or unauthorised shifting of any property 
of the University or the property of any employee of the 
University. 

(27) Breaking open locked rooms, cupboards, safes, lockers and so 
on, and unauthorised locking of open rooms of the University. 

(28) Causing disturbance to and hindering the smooth functioning of 
classrooms, libraries, laboratories, workshops, canteens, mess, 
hostels or office. 

(29) Use of abusive, defamatory, derogatory or intimidatory language 
against any student or employee of the University or visitor to 
the University. 

(30) All acts of physical violence including pelting of stones and 
other objects. 

(31) All forms of coercion and intimidation such as wrongful 
confinements, gheraos, laying siege, sit-ins, blocking entry and 
exit, prevention of normal movement of traffic, or any variation 
of the same, which disrupt the normal academic and 
administrative functioning of the University and which deter the 
officers, faculty and other employees of the University from 
discharging their duty, and which disturb their right to privacy 
and free movement. 

(32) Not sending of an individual representation to higher authorities 
of University through proper channel. 

(33) Making joint or anonymous representation to the University 
Authorities. 

(34) Deliberate boycott of classes, practicals and field visits as part of 
protest. 

(35) All forms of demonstration which have the effect of bringing 
down the public image of the University including shouting of 
slogans, display of condemnatory placards, burning of effigies, 
taking out protest marches, resorting to hunger strikes, etc. 

(36) Talking to the media or publishing or posting of content on the 
Internet including social media and You Tube with the intention 
to slander or bring disrepute to the University, or any student or 
section of students, or any officer, faculty or other employee of 
the University. 

(37) Bringing any political or other outside influence in respect of 
academic or disciplinary matters pertaining to the University. 

(38) Furnishing false certificates or false information in any manner 
to the University. 

(39) Committing forgery, tampering with the Identity Card or 
University records, impersonation, misusing University property 
(movable or immovable), documents and records, tearing of 
pages, defacing, burning or in any way destroying the books, 
journals, magasines and any material of library or unauthorised 
photocopying or possession of library books, journals, magasines 
or any other material.

(53) Disciplinary measures shall commensurate with the gravity of 
the offence, the University may take any action including but not 
limiting to: reprimanding, financial penalty, academic 
suspension or deregistration for semester, debarment from an 
examination, rustication for a specified period or even outright 
expulsion from the University or hostels. 

(54) The University shall evolve necessary mechanisms, including set 
up of Students Disciplinary Committee, to ensure proper 
discipline amongst students, recommendation of all such 
committees shall be considered by the Dean of Students Welfare. 

(55) All cases involving ragging shall be referred to Anti-Ragging 
Committee and those involving sexual harassment shall be 
referred to Internal Complaints Committee and all grievances of 
the nature defined under University Grants Commission 
(grievance redressal) Regulations, 2018, shall be referred to 
Students Grievance Redressal Committee. 


(1) Ragging in any form is strictly prohibited within the premises of 
the University and outside the University Campus including the 
Hostels as well as on public transport. 

(2) Complaints against ragging shall be dealt in accordance with the 
‘University Grants Commission Regulations on Curbing the 
Menace of Ragging in Higher Educational Institutions, 2009’. 


(1) It shall be open to the Dean of the college or institution to admit 
a student who has put in part attendance in another college 


196 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


within the University area and who seeks admission in the college 

or institution during the course of an academic year subject to the 

following conditions: 

(a) The subjects and the medium of instructions offered in both 
the colleges institutions are the same; 

(b) There must be a vacancy in the college or institution in the 
course of study concerned; 

(c) The prescribed fees for such combination of attendance shall 
be collected from the students; 

(d) A no objection certificate from the college or institution 
concerned shall be produced. 

Note: Combination of attendance shall not be granted: 

(a) If there is a change either in the language under Foundation 
course, or, in the optional subject under core course, and 

(b) Ifthe sanctioned strength is exceeded by such admission. 

(2) Students transferred from other Universities and seeking 
admission in the University may be permitted to be admitted to 
the corresponding branch of the concerned course provided that,- 
(a) Equivalence of the course concerned is approved by the 

University; 

(b) They shall produce documentary evidence from the Head of 
the Institution in which they have last studied; 

(c) A certificate stating that they have earned necessary 
attendance and progress as prescribed by the University 
concerned till the date of their leaving that institution; 

(d) They shall have passed all the examinations prescribed by 
the Parent University for the duration of the course of study 
already put in and shall have to produce documentary 
evidence to that effect along with the application for 
admission; 

(e) They shall pay the prescribed fees for such migration to the 
University; 

(f) They shall undergo the remaining course of study and pass 
the examinations prescribed thereof in the University and 
satisfactory fulfill such other requirements as prescribed by 
the University; and 

(g) They shall be eligible for classification but not for ranking in 
the University examination concerned.
