(1) Short Title, Extent and Commencement 


These are called the conditions of Service of Teachers and other 
Academic Staff of Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
(a) These ordinances shall apply to all teachers of the University 
of the University; 
(b) These ordinances shall also apply to academic staff of this 
University; 


Explanation: The term academic staff unless contrary to the context, 
shall include every employee of the University who is required to 
take part in teaching and or research in University Departments, 
Centers, Schools and other institutions maintained or affiliated to 
the University; 

(c) These ordinances shall be deemed to have come into force on the 
date of its approval by the Executive Council; 

Provided that the scales of pay and allowances shall 
come into effect on such dates as the Government of India have 
notified or the University shall notify in relation to such items; 

Provided further that nothing in these ordinances shall be 
deemed to adversely affect any conditions of service of any 
teacher already in service. 

(2) Pay Scales: 

The pay scales of different categories of teachers shall be as per 
the scales approved or adopted by the Executive Council or 
Government of India from time to time. 


(3) Recruitment and qualifications: 


(a) All appointments to teaching posts shall be either by direct 
recruitment on the basis of merit through all India advertisement 
by duly constituted Selection Committee or by deputation or by 
promotions as provided herein: 


Provided that a representative of the Scheduled Castes or 
Scheduled Tribes, women and physically handicapped persons 
shall be included in the Selection Committee whenever persons 
from any of these categories appear for interview. 


(b) The minimum qualifications for the post of Assistant 
Professors, Associate Professors and Professor and _ other 
equivalent posts in Library, Physical Education, shall be those 
prescribed by the University from time to time and every order 
or clarification issued by Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya in this 
regard shall be deemed to be part of these Ordinances, as the 
case may be, and shall take effect from the date prescribed in 
such order. 


[PART IHI—SEc.4] 


[AMT 1--खण्ड 4] 


भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 199 


(4) Incentives for research degrees: Additional increments shall be 
provided to the faculty on direct recruitment as prescribed by 
regulatory authorities (if applicable), from time to time. 

(5) Probation and confirmation: (a) Every teacher shall be 
appointed on probation for a period of twelve months which may 
be extended by the Executive Council wherever necessary. 

Provided that the case of each teacher shall be considered 

for confirmation by a duly constituted review committee or by 
the Vice-Chancellor and shall be reported to the Executive 
Council closer to the conclusion of the probation period and the 
teacher shall be informed of the decision not later than fifteen 
days prior to the expiration of the period of probation. 
(b) Where a teacher appointed on probation is found, during the 
period of probation, not suitable for holding that post or has not 
completed the period of probation whether extended or not, 
satisfactorily, the Executive Council may (i) if the appointment 
is by promotion, revert the incumbent to the previous post held 
by him; and (ii) if the appointment is by direct recruitment, 
terminate the teacher’s services under the University without 
notice. 

(6) Increment: Every teacher shall be entitled to draw his increment 
in his scale of pay unless it is withheld or postponed by a 
resolution of the Executive Council on a reference by the Vice- 
Chancellor and the teacher has been provided with an adequate 
opportunity of making his representation as to why such an 
action shall not be taken. 

(7) Leave: Every teacher shall be eligible for leave as stated in 
Ordinance-35 (Leave rules of Teaching Faculty). 

(8) Retirement: All teachers shall retire at the end of the month in 
which they complete the age of sixty-five years; 

Provided that the University may permit the teacher to 
continue to serve after retirement as aforesaid till the end of the 
academic year to ensure that the teaching work is not dislocated. 

(9) Re-employment of retired persons as teachers and part-time 
teachers: Notwithstanding the provisions of clause (8) above, the 
University may engage any person who has superannuated on 
contract to serve the University in teaching and other academic 
activities subject to such guidelines as the Executive Council 
may from time to time issue in this regard and that pay shall be 
fixed according to the Government of India instructions on the 
pay fixation of pensioners; 

Provided that no person who has attained the age of 
seventy years be appointed under this clause. 

(10) Duties of the Teacher: 

(a) No teacher shall without previous sanction of the Executive 
Council engage directly or indirectly in any employment, trade 
or business to which any emolument or honorarium or other 
pecuniary benefit is attached. 

Provided that nothing in this shall apply to any work 
undertaken in connection with examination of academic bodies 
or literary or scientific work or publication or radio talk or 
extension lecturers or other academic work undertaken with the 


200 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


permission of the Vice-Chancellor. 

(b) Every teacher shall be bound to act in conformity with the 
Statutes, Ordinances, Regulations and Rules of the University for 
the time being in force as well as a code of professional ethics as 
may be formulated by the University. 

(11) Resignation: (a) all permanent teacher may resign his position 
after giving three months’ notice in writing to the University or 
three months’ salary in lieu of the notice. 

(b) all temporary teacher may resign his post after giving one 
months’ notice in writing to the University or one months’ salary 
in lieu of the notice. 

Provided that the Executive Council may waive the 
notice period. 

(12) Contract: All teachers shall be appointed on a written contract 
(Acceptance of offer of appointment) and one copy shall be 
lodged with the Registrar, subject to nothing in this clause shall 
limit the Executive Council from entering into special contract 
providing greater benefits to the teacher. 

(13) Part-time teachers: Part-time teacher may be appointed by the 
University when exceptional circumstances require such 
appointments: 

Provided that qualification, emoluments prescribed 
for full-time teachers shall apply to such part-time teachers. 

(14) Variation in terms and conditions of service: All teacher of 
the University shall be bound by the Statutes, Ordinances and 
Regulations for the time being in force in the University; 

Provided that no change in the terms and conditions 
of service of a teacher shall be made after his appointment, in 
regard to designation, scale of pay, increment, provident fund, 
retirement benefits, age of retirement, probation, confirmation, 
leave salary and removal from service, etc., so as to adversely 
affect him, but this does not in any way restrain the University 
from incorporating the rules and regulations in the Statutes or 
Ordinances of the University based on the communication from 
the Government of India from time to time. 


34. Policy on compensation (1) The salary and allowances shall be as the pay structure approved 
for faculty and: group-A or adopted by Executive Council or Government of India. 
officers. - ५ मु 
(2) Relocation Assistance: 
(a) The relocation assistance include travel for self and 


dependent family members and freight cost towards transfer of 
personal goods, joining time, etc., as per Government of India 
rules for those joining the University on technical resignation 
from Government of India or State Government or public sector 
undertakings or autonomous bodies of Government of India or 
State and as per the limits as indicated below, for those joining 
the University other than on technical resignation or on public 
interest. 

(b) For those from non-governmental establishments, 
reimbursement of relocation expenses including one-time travel 
(as per entitled mode of travel) for self and dependent family 
from current place to residence at Vadodara, and transfer of 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 201 


personal effects (latter subject to a maximum of one lakh rupees 
for India, and two lakhs for overseas relocation) shall be allowed. 

(3) Professional Development Assistance: Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya endeavours to encourage faculty members to 
strive for excellence and work towards self-development and 
professional development assistance is made available to the 
faculty to meet the expenses for participation in national and 
international conferences, payment of membership fee of 
professional bodies and contingent expenses and the amount of 
rupees three lakhs for a period of three years for attending 
conferences, membership to professional bodies, etc. and there 
shall not be further accumulation beyond the rupees three lakhs. 
(The present block is FY 2023-26.) 

(4) Initiation Grant: Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya has been 
striving to become an institution of excellence in education and 
research in the transportation sector, initiation grant shall be 
made available to faculty on joining for initial research set-up at 
the University, this shall be subject to approval of the Vice- 
Chancellor, a grant of rupees five lakhs on joining to be used for 
initial research setup. 


35. Leave rules of teaching (1) Leave of any kind shall not be availed as a matter of right and it 
mpculye may be refused or revoked by the sanctioning authority in the 
interest of the University and leave shall always be got 
sanctioned in advance, except in case of urgency. 
(2) Subject to (1), the University teachers shall be eligible for the 
following kinds of leave: 


(a) Duty Leave: Duty Leave of the maximum of thirty days (this 
excludes the absence for Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya related 
works which the Vice-Chancellor shall approve within the 
delegated powers) in an academic year may be granted for the 
following: 

(i) attending Conferences, congresses, symposia and seminars 
on behalf of the University and with the permission of the 
University. 

(ii) delivering lectures in institutions and Universities at the 
invitation of such institutions or Universities received by the 
University, and accepted by the Vice- Chancellor. 

(iii)working in another Indian or foreign University, any other 
agency, institution or organisation, when so deputed by the 
University. 

(iv)participating in a delegation or working on a committee 
appointed by the Central Government, State Government, the 
University Grants Commission, a sister University or any other 
academic body; 

(v) the duration of leave shall be such as may be considered 
necessary by the sanctioning authority on each occasion; 

(vi)the leave may be granted on full pay, provided that if the 
teacher receives a fellowship or honorarium or any other 
financial assistance beyond the amount needed for normal 
expenses, he may be sanctioned duty leave on reduced pay and 


202 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


allowances; 

(vii) duty leave may be combined with earned leave, half pay 
leave or extraordinary leave; 

(viii) duty leave shall be given also for attending meetings in 
the Universities Grants Commission, Department of Science and 
Technology, etc, where a teacher is invited to share expertise 
with academic bodies, government or Non-Government 
Organisation. 

(ix)for performing any other duty for the University; 


(b) Sabbatical Leave: 

(i) permanent, whole time teachers of the University who have 
completed six years of service may be granted sabbatical leave to 
undertake study or research or other academic pursuit solely for 
the object of increasing their proficiency and usefulness to the 
University and higher education system. 

(ii) (A) the duration of leave shall not exceed one year at a time 
and two times in the entire career of a teacher: 


Provided that sabbatical leave shall not be granted 
until the expiry of six years from the date of the teacher’s return 
from previous sabbatical leave. 

(B) a teacher shall, during the period of sabbatical leave, be 

paid full pay and allowances (subject to the prescribed conditions 
being fulfilled) at the rates applicable to him immediately prior to 
his proceeding on sabbatical leave. 
(ili) a teacher on sabbatical leave shall not take up, during the 
period of that leave, any regular appointment under another 
organisation in India or abroad and he may, however, be allowed 
to accept a fellowship or a research scholarship or ad hoc 
teaching and research assignment with honorarium or any other 
form of assistance, other than regular employment in an 
institution of advanced studies: 

Provided that in such cases the Executive Council 

may, if it so desires, sanction sabbatical leave on reduced pay 
and allowances. 
(iv) during the period of sabbatical leave, the teacher shall be 
allowed to draw the increment on the due date and the period of 
leave shall also count as service for purposes of New Pension 
Scheme, provided that the teacher re-joins the University on the 
expiry of his leave, as such, the New Pension Scheme 
contribution shall be made only after return from sabbatical 
leave; 


(c) Casual Leave: 
(i) total casual leave granted to a teacher shall not exceed eight 
days in a calendar year; and 


(ii) casual leave shall not be combined with any other kind of 
leave except special casual leave, casual leave may be combined 
with holidays including Sundays and such holidays or Sundays 
falling within the period of casual leave shall not be counted as 
casual leave: 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 203 


(9) Special Casual Leave: 
(i) special casual leave, not exceeding fifteen days in an 
academic year, may be granted to a teacher; 
(A) to attend invited Programs, project related 
presentations, conduct examination of a University or Public 
Service Commission or board of examination or other 
similar bodies or institutions; and 
(B) to inspect academic institutions attached to a statutory 
board, etc; 
(ii) in computing the fifteen 14395 leave admissible, the days of 
actual journey, if any, to and from the places where activities 
specified above, take place, shall be excluded; 
(iii) in addition, special casual leaves to the extent mentioned 
below, may also be granted; 
(a) to undergo sterilisation operation (vasectomy or 
salpingectomy) under family welfare Programme and leave 
in this case shall be restricted to six working days; and 
(b) to a female teacher who undergoes non-puerperal 
sterilisation, leave in this case shall be restricted to fourteen 
days. 
(iv) special casual leave shall not be accumulated, nor shall it be 
combined with any other kind of leave except casual leave and it 
may be granted in combination with holidays or vacation by the 
sanctioning authority on each occasion; 


(e) Earned Leave: 

(i) vacation leave (eligible vacation staff only) during specified 
vacation period of the university that is sixty days in a year; 

(ii) earned leave admissible to a teacher shall be: 
Fifty percent of the unused vacation period including vacation; 

(iii) earned leave at the credit of a teacher shall not accumulate 
beyond three hundred days, the maximum earned leave that may 
be sanctioned at a time shall not exceed sixty days and earned 
leave exceeding sixty days may, however, be sanctioned in the 
case of training, or leave with medical certificate, or when the 
entire leave, or a portion thereof, is spent outside India; 


(f) Half-pay Leave : 

Half-pay leave admissible to a permanent teacher shall be 
twenty days for each completed year of service and such leave 
may be granted on the basis of medical certificate from a 
registered medical practitioner, for private affairs or for 
academic purposes. 

Explanation: A "completed year of service" means continuous 
service of specified duration under the University and includes 
periods of absence from duty as well as leave including 
extraordinary leave; 


(g) Commuted Leave 


Commuted leave, not exceeding half the amount of half pay 
leave due, may be granted on the basis of medical certificate 
from a registered medical practitioner to a permanent teacher 


204 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


subject to the following conditions, namely: - 
(i) commuted leave during the entire service shall be 
limited to a maximum of two hundred forty days; 
(1) when commuted leave is granted, twice the amount of 
such leave shall be debited against the half-pay leave due; 
and 
(iii) the total duration of earned leave and commuted leave 
taken in conjunction shall not exceed two hundred forty 
days at a time: 

Provided that no commuted leave shall be 
granted under these Ordinances unless the authority 
competent to sanction leave has reason to believe that the 
teacher shall return to duty on its expiry. 


(h) Extraordinary Leave: 
(i) a permanent teacher may be granted extraordinary leave 
when: 
(a) No other leave is admissible; or 
(b) Other leave is admissible and the teacher applies in 
writing for the grant of extraordinary leave: 
(ii) extraordinary leave shall always be without pay and 
allowances. Extraordinary leave shall not count for increment 
except in the following cases: 
(a) Leave taken on the basis of medical certificates; 
(b) Cases where the Vice-Chancellor is satisfied that the 
leave was taken due to causes beyond the control of the 
teacher, such as inability to join or re-join duty due to civil 
commotion or a natural calamity, provided the teacher has 
no other kind of leave to his credit; 
(c) Leave taken for pursuing higher studies; and, 
(d) Leave granted to accept an invitation to a teaching post 
or fellowship or research- cum- teaching post or on 
assignment for technical or academic work of importance; 
(ili) extraordinary leave may be combined with any other leave 
except casual leave and special casual leave: 

Provided that the total period of continuous absence 
from duty on leave (including periods of vacation when such 
vacation is taken in conjunction with leave) shall not exceed 
three years except in cases where leave is taken on medical 
certificate and the total period of absence from duty shall in no 
case exceed five years in the full working life of the individual; 
(iv) the authority empowered to grant leave may commute 
retrospectively periods of absence without leave into 
extraordinary leave; 


(i) Leave Not Due: 
(1) leave not due, may, at the discretion of the Vice-Chancellor, 
be granted to a permanent teacher for a period not exceeding 
three hundred sixty days during the entire period of service, out 
of which not more than ninety days at a time and one hundred 
eighty days in all may be otherwise than on medical certificate 
and such leave shall be debited against the half- pay leave earned 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 205 


by him subsequently; 

(ii) ‘Leave not due' shall not be granted, unless the Vice- 
Chancellor is satisfied that as far as shall reasonably be foreseen, 
the teacher shall return to duty on the expiry of the leave and 
earn the leave granted; 

(iii) a teacher to whom ‘leave not due' is granted shall not be 
permitted to tender his resignation from service so long as the 
debit balance in his leave account is not wiped off by active 
service, or he refunds the amount paid to him as pay and 
allowances for the period not so earned: 

Provided that in a case where retirement is 
unavoidable on account of reason of ill health, incapacitating the 
teacher for further service, refund of leave salary for the period 
of leave still to be earned may be waived by the Executive 
Council: 


Provided further that the Executive Council may 
waive off, in any other exceptional, for reasons to be recorded in 
writing, the refund of leave salary for the period of leave still to 
be earned; 


(j) Maternity Leave (as per Government of India Rules): 

(i) maternity leave on full pay may be granted to a woman 
teacher for a period not exceeding one hundred eighty days, to be 
availed of twice in the entire career, maternity leave may also be 
granted in case of miscarriage including abortion, subject to the 
conditions that the total leave granted in respect of this to a 
woman teacher in her career is not more than forty five days, and 
the application for leave is supported by a medical certificate; 

(ii) maternity leave may be combined with earned leave, half 
pay leave or extraordinary leave but any leave applied for in 
continuation of maternity leave may be granted if the request is 
supported by a medical certificate; 


(k) Child Care Leave (as per Government of India Rules): 
Women teachers having minor children may be granted leave up 
to two years for taking care of their minor children and such 
leave for a maximum period of two years (seven hundred thirty 
days) may be granted to the women teachers during entire 
service period in lines with Central Government women 
employees rules; 


(I) Paternity Leave (as per Government of India Rules): 
Paternity leave of fifteen days may be granted to male teachers 
during the confinement of their wives, and such leave shall be 
granted only up to two children; 


(m) Adoption leave (as per Government of India Rules); 
Adoption leave may be provided as per the rules of the Central 
Government; 


(n) Vacation: 


(i) vacation may be taken in combination with any kind of 


206 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


leave except casual and special casual leave, provided that 
vacation shall not be both prefixed and suffixed to leave; 

(ii) except in special circumstances, vacation and earned leave 
taken together shall not extend beyond one semester; 

(iii) when a vacation falls between two periods of leave so as to 
result in a continuous absence from duty during the entire period, 
such vacation shall be treated as part of the leave; 

(iv) for the vacation period, a teacher shall be entitled to the 
same pay as when on duty and a teacher shall, be entitled only to 
half of such pay if he has given notice of resignation and the 
period of such notice expires during vacation or within one 
month from the last pay thereof; 


(o) Teacher Appointed on Probation: (i) a teacher appointed as a 
probationer against a substantive vacancy and with definite 
terms of probation shall during the period of probation be 
granted leave which shall be admissible to him if he has held this 
post substantively otherwise than on probation; 


(ii) if a person in the permanent service of the University is 
appointed ‘on probation’ to a higher post, during probation, he 
shall not be deprived of the benefit of leave rules applicable to 
his permanent post; 


(p) Teacher Appointed on Contract Basis: 


Teachers appointed on contract basis shall be entitled to leave as 
under: 

(i) earned leave or half pay leave or casual leave as admissible 
to whole time temporary teachers of the University; 

(ii) in the case of contract appointments for one year of less, 
unless on medical grounds, no extraordinary leave shall be 
granted; 

(ili) in the case of contract appointments for more than one year 
but less than five years, extraordinary leave as admissible to 
temporary teachers’ subject to the condition that the total period 
of extraordinary leave during the entire contract period shall not 
exceed ninety days; 

(iv) where the contract appointment is for five years or more, the 
extraordinary leave shall be as that admissible to the whole time 
temporary teachers; 


(q) General Conditions Applicable to All Categories of Teachers: 


(i) Right to leave: 

(a) No leave shall be claimed as matter of right, leave of any 
kind may be varied, refused or revoked by the competent 
authority empowered to grant it without assigning any 
reason, if that authority considers such action to be in the 
interest of the University; 

(b) No leave shall be granted to a teacher whom a 
competent authority has decided to dismiss, remove or 
compulsorily retire from service, nor shall any leave be 
granted to a teacher who is under suspension; 


[AMT aves 4] 


भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 207 


(ii) Maximum period of absence from duty on leave: 

(a) No teacher shall be granted leave of any kind for a 
continuous period exceeding five years; 

(b) Where a teacher does not resume duty after remaining 
on leave for a continuous period of five years or where a 
teacher after the expiry of his leave remains absent from 
duty, other than on foreign service or on account of 
suspension, for any period which together with the period of 
leave granted to his exceeds five years, he shall unless the 
Executive Council in view of the exceptional circumstances 
of the case otherwise determines, be removed from service 
after following prescribed procedure; 


(iii) Application for Leave: 
Leave shall always be applied for in advance and _ the 
sanction of the competent authority obtained before it is 
availed of except in cases of emergency and for satisfactory 
reasons. 

Note: Faculty member shall not leave station till the order 

sanctioning leave has been issued. 

(iv) Commencement and termination of leave: 
(a) Leave ordinarily begins from the date on which leave as 
such is actually availed of and ends on the day the teacher 
resumes his duty; 
(b) Sunday and other recognised holidays may be prefixed 
and or suffixed to the leave with the permission of the 
authority competent to sanction the leave; 


(v) Re-joining of duty before the expiry of the leave: 

(a) A teacher may return to duty before the expiry of the 
leave granted to him, with the permission of the competent 
authority; 

(b) Notwithstanding anything contained in (a) above, a 
teacher on leave preparatory to retirement shall be precluded 
from withdrawing his request for permission to retire and 
from returning to duty, save with the consent of the 
Executive- Council; 


(vi) Leave on medical grounds to be supported by medical 
certificates 
A teacher who applies for leave on medical grounds shall 
support his application with a medical certificate from a 
registered medical practitioner empanelled by the University 
and leave or extension of leave on medical certificate shall not 
be granted beyond the date on which a teacher is pronounced 
by a Medical Board constituted by the University for the 
purpose to be permanently incapacitated for further service; 
(vii)Re-joining duty on return from leave on medical 
ground.- 
No teacher who has been granted leave (other than casual 
leave) on medical grounds shall be allowed to return to duty 
without producing a certificate of fitness from the registered 
medical officer empanelled by the University; 
(viii) Employment during leave: 


208 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


(A) a teacher on leave shall not, without the written 
permission of the University, engage directly or indirectly in 
any trade or business whatsoever or in any private tuition or 
other work to which any emolument or honorarium is 
attached: 

Provided that this prohibition shall not apply to 
work undertaken in connection with the examination of a 
University, Public Service Commission, Board of Education 
or similar Bodies or Institutions or to any literary work or 
publication or radio or extension lectures, or with the 
permission of the Vice-Chancellor to any other academic 
work; 
(B) The leave salary of a teacher who is permitted to take up 
any employment during leave shall be subject to such 
restrictions as the Executive Council may prescribe; 

(ix) Absence without leave or overstay on leave: 
A teacher who absents himself without leave or remains 
absent without leave after the expiry of the leave granted to 
him, shall be entitled to no leave allowance or salary for the 
period of such absence, such period shall be debited against 
his leave account as leave is extended by the authority 
empowered to grant the leave and wilful absence from duty 
may be treated as misconduct; 
(x) Conversion of one kind of leave to another: 

(a) At the request of the teacher concerned, the University 
may convert retrospectively any kind of leave including 
extraordinary leave into a leave of different kind which was 
admissible to him at the time leave was originally taken, but 
he shall not claim such conversion as a matter of right; 
(b) If one kind of leave is converted into another, the 
amount of leave salary and the allowances admissible shall 
be recalculated and arrears of leave salary and allowances 
paid or the amount overdrawn recovered, as the case may 
be; 


(xi) Increment during leave: 
If increment of pay falls during any leave other than casual 
leave, special casual leave, duty leave, or sabbatical leave, 
the effect of increase of pay shall be given from the date the 
teacher resumes duty without prejudice to the normal date of 
his increment, except in those cases where the leave does 
not count for increment; 

(xii) Leave year: 
For the purpose of these Ordinances, unless otherwise 
specified, the term ‘year’ shall mean a calendar year; 


(xii) authorities Empowered to Sanction Leave — As per the 
delegated powers.

Provided that where leave involves visit abroad, the 
competent authority shall be the Vice-Chancellor; 


(xix) Leave Salary: 
(a) A teacher granted casual leave or special casual 
leave is not treated as absent fromduty and his pay is not 
interrupted; 
(b) A teacher on earned leave is entitled to leave 
salary equivalent to the pay drawn immediately before 
proceeding on leave; 
(c) A teacher on commuted leave is entitled to leave 
salary equal to the salary admissible; 
(d) A teacher on extraordinary leave shall not be entitled 
to any leave salary; 
(e) A teacher on maternity leave or paternity leave or child 
care leave and quarantine leave is entitled to draw the same 
pay as he may be drawing at the time of proceeding on 
leave; 
(f) Payment of all admissible allowances during leave 


210 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


shall be governed by the provisions of the rules regarding 
the payment of those allowances; 

(g) During the period of re-employment, the leave salary 
shall be based on the pay drawn by him exclusive of the 
pension and pension equivalent of other retirement 
benefits; 


(xx) Cash Payment In lieu of Leave: 


(a) On Superannuation.- 

Where a teacher superannuates on attaining the normal age 
prescribed for superannuation under the terms and 
conditions governing his service, the authority competent to 
grant leave shall suo motu authorise encashment of earned 
leave, if any, at the credit of the teacher on the date of his 
superannuation, subject to a maximum of three hundred 
days and this limit shall be as applicable to Government of 
India employees; 

(b) Superannuation while under suspension.- 

A teacher, who superannuates while under suspension, shall 
become eligible for the benefit of cash equivalent of earned 
leave at his credit on the date of his superannuation on 
conclusion of the proceedings against him, if the competent 
authority decides to re-instate him in service and holds that 
the suspension was wholly unjustified; 

(c) Retirement before superannuation.- 

A teacher who retires or is retired from service may be 
granted suo moto, by the authority competent to grant leave, 
cash equivalent of leave salary in respect of earned leave at 
his credit subject to maximum number of days including 
days in respect of the half pay leave at his credit as specified 
by the Government of India or regulatory authorities from 
time to time; 

Provided that this period does not exceed the 
period between the date on which he so retires or is retired 
from service and the date on which he shall have retired in 
the normal course after attaining the age prescribed for 
superannuation under the terms and conditions governing 
his service, the cash equivalent shall be equal to the leave 
salary as admissible for earned leave and equal to the leave 
salary as admissible for half pay leave plus dearness 
allowance admissible on that leave salary for the first three 
hundred days, at the rates in force on the date the University 
employee so retires or is retired from service, the pension 
and pension equivalent of other retirement benefits and ad 
hoc relief or graded relief on pension shall be deducted from 
the leave salary paid for the period of half pay leave, if any, 
for which the cash equivalent is payable and this limit shall 
stand revised as per Government of India orders issued from 
time to time; 

Settlement or no house rent allowance shall be payable; 

Provided that if leave salary for the half pay 
leave component falls short of pension and other pensionary 
benefits, cash equivalent of half pay leave shall not be 
granted: 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 211 


Provided further that a teacher who is retired by 
University by giving him pay and allowances in lieu of 
notice, cash equivalent of leave salary shall be allowed only 
for the period of leave excluding that period for which pay 
and allowances in lieu of notice have been allowed. 
(d) Resignation or Termination: 
(i) resignation: if a teacher (confirmed employee) 
resigns or quits service, he may be granted suo moto by 
the authority competent to grant leave cash equivalent in 
respect of earned leave at his credit on the date of 
cessation of service, to the extent of half of such leave at 
his credit, subject to a maximum of one hundred fifty 
days; 


(ii) termination: Where the services of a teacher are 
terminated by notice or by payment of pay and 
allowances, in lieu of notice, or otherwise in accordance 
with the terms and conditions of his appointment, he may 
be granted, suo moto by the authority competent to grant 
leave, cash equivalent in respect of earned leave at his 
credit on the date on which he ceases to be in service 
subject to maximum of three hundred days and this limit 
shall stand revised as per Government of India orders 
issued from time to time; 

(e) Dismissal or Removal.- 
When a teacher is dismissed or removed from service 
following disciplinary proceedings he shall not be 
eligible for leave encashment. 

(f)Leave encashment when permanently incapacitated: 
(1) a teacher who is declared by a duly constituted 
Medical Board to be completely and permanently 
incapacitated for further service may be granted, suo 
motu, by the authority competent to grant leave, cash 
equivalent of leave salary in respect of earned leave due 
and admissible on the date of his invalidation from 
service subject to leave salary for a maximum three 
hundred days; 
(1i)the cash equivalent thus payable shall be equal to the 
leave salary as calculated under the rule and this limit 
shall stand revised as per Government of India orders 
issued from time to time; 

(g) Leave encashment of a teacher who dies while in 

harness.- 

In case a teacher dies in harness, the cash equivalent of the 

leave salary that the deceased teacher shall have got, had he 

gone on earned leave, but for the death, due and admissible 

on the date immediately following the date of death subject 

to a maximum of leave salary for three hundred days shall 

be paid to his family and this limit shall stand revised as per 

Government of India orders issued from time to time; 

(h) Calculation of cash equivalent of leave.- 

The cash equivalent of leave shall be calculated and payable 

in one lump sum as a one-time settlement as per 

Government of India rules;

36. 


Eligibility or experience 
requirements for faculty 
positions and 

selection process. - 


followed under this Ordinance.- 


The University may make rules and procedures for effecting the 
provisions of this Ordinance. 


The following shall be the minimum requirements for faculty 
positions at the University: 


(1) Professor: Academic pay level-14 in the pay matrix (Rs 1,44,200- 
2,18,200) of 7" Central Pay Commission; 
Age-limit - 50 years (preferable); 
Eligibility (Minimum Qualification or Experience): 


(a) Doctor of Philosophy with first class or equivalent at the 
preceding degree in appropriate branch with consistent and very 
good academic record; 

(b) Published work of high quality, actively engaged in research 
with evidence of published work with, a minimum of fifteen 
research publications in scopus indexed journals; 

(c) Post - Doctor of Philosophy teaching experience of minimum of 
ten years in an academic institution or University or college of 
which, four years as Associate Professor or equivalent or 
comparable research experience at equivalent level at the 
University or national level institutions with evidence of having 
successfully guided doctoral candidate; 

OR 

Post-Doctor of Philosophy experience of minimum of ten years 
in a research organisation or industry, with significant 
contribution to the knowledge in the concerned or allied or 
relevant discipline, supported by documentary evidence; 

(d) The candidate should have demonstrated research ability with an 
excellent record of publications in reputed journals and 
conferences and must have supervised Doctor of Philosophy 
students, the candidate should also have demonstrated excellent 
teaching abilities at higher educational institutions, it is desirable 
that the candidate has carried out laboratory development or 
course development or technology development or innovative 
work leading to patents and has generated sufficient funding for 
research and the criteria of supervision of Doctor of Philosophy 
students and teaching at higher educational institutions may be 
relaxed for candidates who have joined from industry; 


(2) Associate Professor: Academic pay level-13A in the pay matrix 
(Rs. 1,31,400-2,17,100) of 7" Central Pay Commission; 
Age-limit: 45 years (preferable); 
Eligibility (Minimum Qualification or Experience): 


(a) Doctor of Philosophy with first class or equivalent at the 
preceding degree in an appropriate branch with consistent and 
very good academic record; 

(b) Published work of high quality, actively engaged in research 
with evidence of published work with, a minimum of 10 research 
publications in Scopus indexed journals; 

(c) A minimum of six years of post - Doctor of Philosophy 
teaching or research experience in an academic or research 
position equivalent to that of Assistant Professor in an academic

(4) Selection Process shall involve the following steps: 


Step-1 | Shortlisting committee gives its recommendation on the 
suitability candidates for the respective faculty position. 
Step-2 | The candidates recommended by the shortlisting 
committee are called for a seminar and interaction. 
Step-3 | Seminar and Interaction. 

Step-4 | Recommendation letters are sought from the referees of 
these candidates who are identified after the seminar 
and interaction. 

Step-5 | A final shortlist of candidates is made based on the 
application, faculty input, seminar, interaction and 
referee comments. 

Step-6 | The shortlisted candidates are called for a personal 
interview. 

Step-7 | The Selection Committee conducts the Interview. 


Step-8 | The selected candidates are offered the position, as 
advertised, on the approval of the competent authority. 


(5) Following shall be the general conditions for the applicants: 


(a) If a class or division is not awarded, minimum of 60% marks in 
aggregate shall be considered equivalent to first class or division, 
if a Grade Point System is adopted the Cumulative Grade Point 
Average shall be converted into equivalent marks as below. 


Grade Point Equivalent Percentage 
6.25 55. % 
6.75 60 % 
7.25 65 % 
7.75 710 % 
8.25 75 % 


(b) Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya reserves the right to charge 
application fee for the various positions as amended from time to 
time; 

(c) Applicants who are employed in government, semi- 
government organisations or institutions or autonomous bodies 
shall send their application through proper channel or a no 
objection certificate from the employer shall be produced by the 
applicant at the time of the interview; 

(d) All supporting documents provided and the application form 
must bear full name and signature of the candidate on each page 
at the bottom; 

(e) Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya reserves the right to summarily 
reject the incomplete applications and also, to reject any or all 
applications without assigning any reason; 

(f) The number of positions to be filled as indicated in the 
advertisement may change at the time of selection and the 
university reserves the right to consider good candidates over and 
above the number indicated in the advertisement as per the 
requirement at the time of the selection interviews; 

(g) 041 Shakti Vishwavidyalaya reserves the right to waive or 
relax any conditions in deserving cases; 

(h) Selection shall be based on interview and only the shortlisted

224 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


(a) To develop a useful and viable collaboration between 
institutions and industry and enhancing quality of education 
भाव skills by involvement of academicians, scholars, 
practitioners, policymakers and skilled professionals in 
teaching, training, research and related services on regular 
basis; 

(b) To attract distinguished individuals who have excelled in their 
field of specialisation like science and technology, industry, 
commerce, social research, media, literature, fine arts, civil 
services and public life into the academic arena, to enrich the 
overall learning processes by bringing external perspectives to 
regular teaching, such interactions are expected to foster 
trans-disciplinary approach and synergise the outside ‘real 
world’ experience with the inside intellectual pursuits in the 
university; 

(c) To promote the interaction of skilled professionals with the 
learners and facilitate the imparting of industry relevant 
standards in skills, acceptable nationally, which shall fulfil the 
need for skilled workforce and also to undertake Research and 
Development in the areas related to skill education and 
development, entrepreneurship and employability, etc; 

(d) To enable higher educational institutions to access the 
eminent teachers and researchers who have completed their 
formal association with the university or college, to 
participate in teaching, to collaborate and to stimulate 
research activities for quality research at Doctor of Philosophy 
levels; and to play mentoring and inspirational role; 

(e) To recognise the skills of professionals in their respective 
areas of excellence irrespective of their academic 
qualifications to impart training to the learners of skill based 
vocational courses in Universities and colleges. 


(2) Target Groups: 


(a) Teaching and research organisations supported by bodies like 
Indian Council of Agricultural Research, Indian Council of 
Social Science Research, Council of Scientific and Industrial 
Research, Indian Council of Medical Research, Defence 
Research and Development Organisation, Indian Institutes of 
Technology, Indian Institutes of Management, National 
Institutes of Technology, Indian Institutes of Science 
Education and Research, Central and State Universities, etc; 

(b) Railway Public Sector Undertakings, Central or State public 
sector undertakings, business corporations, Non-government 
Organisations and professional associations. Professionals, 
experts, officials and managers having experience of working 
in; 

(c) Civil servants (Indian Administrative Service or Indian Police 
Service or officials from Central and Provincial Services) and 
professionals and officials from professional councils and 
statutory bodies, both serving and retired; 

(d) Industry Experts and skilled professionals working in 
organised and unorganised sectors known for their hands-on 
skilling techniques and expertise; 

(०) Non-Resident Indians and Persons of Indian Origin working

(3) Selection Criteria: 
Candidate for adjunct faculty shall satisfy the following norms, 
namely: - 


(a) Shall have the minimum qualifications as prescribed in the 
regulations framed by regulatory authorities or respective 
statutory councils from time to time; OR 

(b) A person of eminence with or without a postgraduate or 
Doctor of Philosophy qualifications, they are also expected to 
have an understanding of industry requirements with a 
potential for Assessment and Certification for skills; 


In addition to the above, it is expected that the adjunct faculty 
in both the above streams shall be an accomplished scholar in 
his area of specialisation and his association shall add value to 
the academic programs he is associated with. 

Adjunct Faculty shall be appointed by the competent authority 
based on the recommendation of the Selection Committee 
comprising of the following,- 


(i) chairperson, Faculty Standing Committee. Dean 
(Academic or Research) in case of university or senior 
most faculty in case of college; 

(ii) head of the concerned Department; 

(iii) at least one external expert (Nominated by the Vice- 
Chancellor). 

(c) If the selection committee recommends the case, the same 
shall be forwarded to the competent authority for 
consideration and necessary approval, the strength of adjunct 
faculty may not exceed 25 % the sanctioned strength of 
faculty at any time and the compensation structure may be 
reviewed from time to time. 

(d) Roles and Responsibilities: 


(i) Teaching: The empaneled adjunct faculty is expected 
to undertake following assignments: 

e Conventional Higher Education Courses: Adjunct 
faculty shall be expected to teach courses directly 
related to his specific expertise and professional 
experience or the areas of his specialization, he may 
also contribute to the institution’s activities like 
counselling of students, developing new courses and 
pedagogical improvements. 

e Skill based Vocational Courses: The core courses 
pertaining to specialised skills or trades may be 
imparted by the adjunct faculty from industry, 
Sector Skill Councils approved trainers or other 
persons with appropriate skill proficiency and such 
faculty, imparting education and training to learners 
in skill based courses, shall have relevant National 
Skills Qualifications Framework qualifications,

journals), he may participate by advising faculty on 
their research projects, serving as a liaison between the 
institutions and industry or government entities to 
identify research or funding opportunities or by 
working with faculty to identify research projects that 
shall benefit private industry or government entities; 

(iv) Services: Adjunct faculty is also expected to actively 
participate in service-related activities, such as sitting 
on departmental committees, serving as advisors to 
faculty or undergraduate and post graduate students, 
helping students network, and active collaboration with 
the industry or employer providing internship and job 
opportunities; 

(v) Adjunct faculty shall be provided travel support as per 
the university norms, from his institution or place of 
stay and back, maximum twenty-four times per 
academic year and the university shall take care of the 
accommodation, food and local travel; 

(vi) Adjunct faculty shall be provided an honorarium and 
other perks, as approved by the Executive Council. 


Engagements of Faculty on contract basis shall be made by the 
Executive Council against sanctioned posts (unfilled regular or 
contract). 

Educational qualifications for Faculty (on contract) shall be same 
as that of regular posts in the concerned discipline. 

The maximum entry age for engagement of Faculty on contract 
is sixty-two years. 

The reservation and relaxation in qualifications shall apply as per 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 227 


Government of India rules which are in force. 

(5) The Selection Committee norms for engagement of Faculty on 
contract basis shall be same as those of regular posts under 
Statute. 

(6) The selection process may consist of personal interview and 
making a demo lecture before the Selection Committee, in case 
of large number of applicants, a screening test shall be conducted 
in Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya to shortlist candidates and the 
candidates who pass the screening test shall be called for 
personal interview and the shortlisting of applications and 
decision to conduct a screening test shall be recommended by a 
scrutiny committee, constituted with the approval of Vice- 
Chancellor. 

(7) Faculty member engaged under this Ordinance shall enter into a 
contract with Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya. 

(8) The performance appraisal of the faculty members shall be based 
on the Annual Performance Report as per the format prescribed 
by Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya. 

(9) The duration of the contract engagements shall be initially for a 
period of one year and the contract shall be extended for a max 
of two more instances (one year at a time) upon the 
recommendations of a committee constituted by the Vice 
Chancellor and the extension is subject to Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya’s requirements. 

(10) At the end of each three-year tenure, a contract Faculty shall be 
eligible upon his application for re- engagement for additional 
tenure (renewable annually), provided a review of the 
performance by the selection committee as indicated in sub- 
clause 4 above finds the candidate suitable and a fresh contract 
shall be entered into by the candidate and Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya and the re-engagement is subject to Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya’s requirements and other applicable norms. 

(11) The Faculty member engaged on contract shall be paid a 
consolidated salary which shall be fixed by the Selection 
Committee, by taking into consideration of justifications (that 
must be recorded) like qualification, experience, the last drawn 
emoluments, etc. and they shall be eligible for annual increment 
@ 5%, further, the consolidated remuneration paid to contract 
Faculty member shall not exceed the emoluments at the max of 
the scale of the regular post against which appointed. 

(12) The contract Faculty member shall during official engagement or 
travel be entitled to same allowance as that of regular Faculty or 
at the corresponding level. 

(13) Applicable statutory deductions towards Income tax, etc. shall be 
made from the consolidated remuneration. 

(14) Dearness Allowance or City Compensatory Allowance or House 
Rent Allowance or Leave Travel Concession or any other relief 
or allowances or benefits are not admissible. 

(15) The total casual leave admissible shall not exceed 20 days 
(without any deduction of consolidated remuneration) per 
annum. 

(16) Faculties engaged on contract basis shall be assigned, in addition 


228 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


to the specific task for which appointed, any other duties by the 
Dean (Academics) or Deans of University Functions or Vice- 
Chancellor. 

(17) The engagement shall be discontinued with one-month notice by 
either side without assigning any reason, also, the appointee shall 
not have any claim whatsoever for regular appointment on any 
position on the basis of contract appointment. 

(18) Engagement of Visiting Faculty or Guest Faculty on hourly basis 
shall not come under the purview of these rules. 


41. Career progression of Preamble: 
teachers. - 


The Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, by nature, is a Central University, a 
pioneer in the domain of transport and logistics education in India and 
the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya promotes excellence in quality and 
prefers to maintain good quality professionals in the faculty cadre. 
The cadre structure is different from Indian Institutes of Technology 
cadre structure but in quality, there is no compromise. Since, the 
Assistant Professor level induction is at academic level-10 in the pay 
matrix (Rs.57700-182400), Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya has to 
provide promotional avenues so as to keep the faculty motivated and 
avoid attrition. As Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya is recruiting faculty 
who are pass-outs from Indian Institutes of Technology or Indian 
Institutes of Management or the premier National Institutes of 
Technology, meritocracy shall prevail in promotion and not the time- 
bound or in-situ promotion. Such a policy shall motivate the 
performers. 


(1) Career Progression at Assistant Professor Level: The 
movement from level-10 to level-11 and level-11 to level-12 in 
the pay matrix of the 7” Central Pay Commission shall be 
through selection and not in-situ. 

(2) The selection committee shall have at least one external member 
in the level of Professor or above and this scheme shall be 
termed career progression for faculty. 

(3) The minimum residency period at Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
for movement of faculty from Academic pay level-10 to 
Academic pay level-11 shall be one year and on successful 
completion of probation period; and for movement from 
Academic pay level-11 to Academic pay level-12, two years 
from the date of joining on Academic pay level-11. 

(4) Movement to the level of Associate Professor and Professor shall 
only be through selection, after the required residency as in 
Indian Institutes of Technology. 

(5) For the position of Associate Professor, the candidate shall have 
at least a minimum of six years (post Doctor of Philosophy) 
teaching, research or industrial experience out of which at least 
three years shall be at the level of Assistant Professor in 
academic level-12 in the pay matrix (Rs. 79800-211500) in a 
reputed Institute or University or equivalent. 

(6) For the position of Professor, at least a minimum of ten years 
(post Doctor of Philosophy) teaching, research or industrial 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 229 


experience out of which at least four years shall be at the level of 
Associate Professor or at an equivalent level. 


42. _ Policy for lien to faculty (1) The lien is not a right but a privilege; 

and stat petabels?s (2) The lien shall be limited to only Group-‘A’ positions and no lien 

for employees below Group- ‘A’. 

(3) The lien shall be maximum for a period of one year. 

(4) For the role of head of institute or university position of 
government or autonomous institutes funded by government, lien 
period shall be as per government of India norms. 

(5) Probationers shall not be eligible for lien. 

(6) In all cases, the lien shall be for a maximum 10% of the 
respective teaching or non- teaching positions. 

(7) There shall not be any leave accrual during the period of lien and 
there is no leave salary or pension contribution during lien. 

(8) During the lien period, the host establishment shall have the 
liability for payment of National Pension System contribution, in 
cases where the employee is moving for Academic or Scientific 
pursuits, the period shall be a non-contributory period for 
National Pension System. 

(9) After a lien is availed, there shall be a cooling down period of 
five years to avail the next lien. 


43. Manner ofappointment (1) (a) The employees of the University, other than teachers and other 
end enol uientset nen: academic staff shall be grouped in to Group ‘A’, ‘B’, and ‘C’ 
teaching staff. - : ; wa 

categories and such other categories as classified by the 
Government of India from time to time. 

(b) The age, qualifications and methods of recruitment for 
appointment to various posts in the University shall be such as 
may be prescribed hereunder or in the Cadre Recruitment Rules 
adopted by Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, with the approval of 
the Executive Council. 

(c) The emoluments of these employees shall be as prescribed by the 
Government of India or regulatory authorities from time to time. 


(2) Cadre Recruitment Rules for Non-Teaching Posts: 
(a) These rules shall be called Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Non- 
teaching and other Academic Posts Recruitment Rules, 2026. 
(b) These Rules shall come into force with effect from the date of 
issue of notification by the University. 
(c) Definition: In these rules, unless the context otherwise requires: 

(i) ‘age’ shall mean the upper age-limit as specified in the 
Schedule 1. Notified date shall mean the date specified in the 
Notification. 

(ii) ‘Appointing Authority’ in relation to any post in the 
University shall imply the authority competent to make 
appointment to that post under the Act or Statute or 
Ordinances or Cadre Recruitment Rules or Regulations of the 
University as amended from time to time. 

(iii) ‘Cadre’ shall mean the strength of service or a part of service 
sanctioned as a separate unit; 

(iv) ‘Competent Authority’ shall mean the authority competent to 


230 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


exercise different powers and functions under these rules; 

(v) ‘Departmental candidate’ shall mean the employee working on 
regular basis in the “Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya” against a 
substantive post in the University but does not include an 
employee working on ad-hoc, daily wage, contract, or 
temporary basis; 

(vi) ‘direct recruitment’ shall means the recruitment made other 
than by promotion, deputation or absorption; 

(vii) ‘Departmental Promotion Committee and Departmental 
Confirmation Committee’ shall mean departmental Promotion 
Committee and Departmental Confirmation Committee 
respectively; 

(viii) ‘Government’ shall mean the Government of India; 

(ix) ‘non-teaching employee’ shall mean an employee of the 
University other than the teachers of the University; 

(x) ‘other academic staff’ shall mean the other academic staff as 
defined in the “University Grants Commission Regulations on 
Minimum Qualifications for Appointment of Teachers and 
Other Academic Staff in Universities and Colleges and Other 
Measures for the Maintenance of Standards in Higher 
Education, 2018’, which includes the cadres of Librarians, 
Directors of Physical Education and Sports; 

(xi) ‘On Probation’ with relation to a person shall mean a person 
appointed to any post on probation as specified in these rules; 

(xii) ‘regular service’ shall mean the service rendered by an 
employee in the cadre on regular basis and shall not include 
the service on contract or daily wage or temporary or ad- hoc 
basis; 

(xiii) ‘SC’ shall mean Scheduled Caste, ‘ST’ shall mean Scheduled 
Tribe, ‘OBC’ shall mean Other Backward Class, ‘PwBD’ 
shall mean Persons with Benchmark Disability, ‘EWS’ shall 
mean Economically Weaker Sections. 

(xiv) 'Schedule' shall mean the Schedule appended to these rules; 

(xv) ‘Selection Committee’ shall mean a composition of members 
of Selection Committee as specified in the Act, Statutes, 
Ordinances, regulatory authority Regulations, Guidelines or 
the Cadre Recruitment Rules of the University; 

(xvi) (a) ‘service or experience’ wherever prescribed under these 
rules for direct recruitment shall mean a condition for 
appointment to any post shall include the period which the 
person has worked on such lower post, (b) ‘Service or 
Experience’ wherever prescribed under these rules for 
promotion shall mean a condition for promotion to any higher 
post, shall include the period which the person has 
continuously worked on such lower post on regular basis; 

(xvii) ‘Substantive appointment’ shall mean appointment on a 
substantive post on regular basis including on probation 
followed by confirmation; 

(xviii) All other words and expressions, used herein, but not defined 
in these rules, unless the context otherwise requires, shall 
have the same meaning as they have been assigned in the 
“Central Universities Act, 2009”, Statutes, Ordinances and 
Regulations made thereunder or in the relevant rules framed 
by the Government of India. 


[AMT 1--खण्ड 4] 


भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 231 


(xix) ‘UGC’ shall mean the University Grants Commission 
established under the University Grants Commission Act, 
1956; 


(3) Extent of Application: These rules shall apply to all appointments of 

non-teaching posts including other academic staff on regular or 
tenure or deputation or promotion basis by the University as the 
case may be. 
Instructions issued by the Government of India or regulatory 
authority regarding appointment, conditions of service and 
admissible emoluments, etc. of the non-teaching employees and 
other academic staff from time to time shall apply mutatis 
mutandis with due approval of the Executive Council. 


(4) Classification of Posts: 
(a) Group ‘A’ (pay level-10 in the pay matrix and above); 
(b) Group ‘B’ (pay level 6 to 9 in the pay matrix); 
(c) Group ‘C’ including Multi-Tasking Staff (MTS) (pay level 1 to 
5). 


(5) Schedule: The posts with classification, pay-matrix, details of 
qualifications or experience, method of recruitment, age-limit, etc, 
for various posts and any other information relevant to these posts 
are specified under Schedule- I. 


(6) Appointing Authority: The Appointing Authority in respect of 
various posts in the University shall be mentioned in the following

(7) Method of Recruitment: 
The recruitment to various posts shall be made by the Appointing 
Authority by the following methods, namely: - 
(a) Direct recruitment; 
(b) Promotion; 
(c) | Deputation or absorption; 
(d) Tenure appointment; 
The Executive Council shall decide the proportion of the above
