the pay matrix and above 


(2) | Post carrying the pay level from 6 | Group ‘B’ 
to 9 in the pay matrix 


(3) | Post carrying the pay in Level from | Group ‘C’ 
1 to 5 in the pay matrix 


(21) | Anemployee shall, on his appointment to a post on a time-scale 
of pay, draw pay at the minimum of the time-scale unless the 
Appointing Authority decides that he shall draw pay at any 
higher stage. 

Provided that, when such appointment is made by 
promotion- 

(a) The pay of the employee shall first be increased by one 
increment in the lower scale, and then fixed in the higher 
scale at the stage next above, 
the employee shall, however, have the option to be exercised 
in writing within a period of three months of his promotion, 
either to have his pay fixed in the higher scale of pay from 
the date of promotion or from the date on which his next 
annual increment falls due and this option, once exercised 
shall be final; 

(b) If he had previously served in the same post or in any other 
post under the University on the same or identical time-scale 
of pay, and was drawing pay higher than the pay admissible 
to him under clause (i) he shall draw such higher pay and the 
period of his duty in such post on such pay shall also count 
for purpose of increment in the higher post: 

(०) (i) Fixation of pay of re-employed pensioners shall be done 
as per the Government of India norms, 


(ii) in case where the minimum pay of the post in which the 
officer is re-employed is more than the last pay drawn, the 
officer concerned may be allowed the minimum of the 
prescribed scale of the post less pension and pension 
equivalent of other retirement benefits; 


(iii) Once initial pay of re-employed pensioner has been 
fixed in the manner indicated above he may be allowed to 
draw normal increments in the time scale of the pot to 
which he is appointed provided that the pay and gross 


[भाग पा--_खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 289 


pension or Pension equivalent of the retirement benefit 
taken together does not at any time exceed the limit 
prescribed by the Government of India for the purpose; 


(iv) Notwithstanding anything contained in the foregoing 
paragraphs the Vice-Chancellor, in special circumstances, 
shall have the power to fix the pay of the re- employed 
pensioner at a higher stage and permit him to draw the 
normal increments in the time-scale of the post to which he 
is appointed; 

(22) (a) An increment shall ordinarily be drawn as a matter of course 
unless it is withheld by the Competent Authority if the 
conduct of the employee has not been good or his work has 
not been satisfactory; 

(b) When an efficiency bar is prescribed in the time-scale, the 
increment next above that bar shall not be given to an 
employee without specific sanction of the Vice- 
Chancellor; 

(23) (a) All duty in a post on a time-scale of pay counts for increments 

in that time-scale; 
(b) Service in another equivalent or higher post, Foreign Service 
and joining time shall count for increments; 
(c) All leave except extraordinary leave taken without medical 
certificate shall also count for increments; 
(d) The extraordinary leave sanctioned for the following purposes 
shall automatically count as qualifying service for pension and 
increments without any further sanctions:- 
(i) extraordinary leave granted due to inability of a 
University employee to join or rejoin duty on account of 
civil commotion; 
(ii) extraordinary leave granted to a University employee 
for prosecuting higher technical and scientific studies. 


(24) (a) An employee under suspension shall, during the period of 
suspension, draw subsistence allowance equivalent to half the 
rate of pay which is admissible to him immediately before the 
commencement of the suspension and in addition the dearness 
allowance as admissible on the basis of that pay and such 
compensatory allowances admissible from time to time on the 
basis of pay which he was in receipt on the date of suspension, 
subject to fulfillment of other conditions laid down for the drawl 
of such allowances : 

Provided that where the period of suspension 
exceeds three months, the authority which made or is deemed to 
have made the order of suspension shall be competent to vary the 
amount of subsistence allowance for any period subsequent to the 
period of the first three months as follows: 

(i) the amount of subsistence allowance may be increased by a 
suitable amount, not exceeding fifty percent of the 
subsistence allowance admissible during the period of the 
first three months, if, in the opinion of the said authority, the 
period of suspension has been prolonged for reasons, to be 
recorded in writing, not directly attributable to the 
employee; 


290 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


(ii) the amount of subsistence allowance may be reduced by a 
suitable amount, not exceeding 50% of the subsistence 
allowance admissible during the period of the first three 
months, if in the opinion of the authority, the period of 
suspension has been prolonged for reasons to be recorded in 
writing, directly attributable to the employee; 

(iii) the rate of the dearness allowance shall be based on the 
increased or, as the case may be the decreased amount of 
subsistence allowance admissible under sub-clause (i) and 
(ii) above; 

(b) No payment under sub-rule (i) shall be made unless the employee 
furnishes a declaration that he is not engaged in any other 
employment, business, profession or vocation during the period 
of suspension, 

Provided that in the case of an employee 
dismissed or terminated from service or compulsorily retired 
from service who is deemed to have been placed or to continue 
to be under suspension from the date of such dismissal or 
termination of service or compulsory retirement and who fails 
to produce such a declaration for any period or periods during 
which he is deemed to be placed or to continue to be under 
suspension, he shall be entitled to the amount by which his 
earnings during such period or periods as the case may be, fall 
short of the amount of subsistence allowance and other 
allowances that shall otherwise be admissible to him; where the 
subsistence allowance and other allowances admissible to him 
are equal to or less than the amount earned by him, nothing in 
this proviso shall apply to him. 

(c) The permissible deductions from the subsistence allowance shall 
be of the following two categories: - 

(1) compulsory deductions; 
(A) income-tax and super-tax (Provided the employee’s 
yearly 
income calculated with reference to subsistence allowance is 
taxable); 
(B) house rent and allied charges, i.e. electricity, water, 
furniture, etc.; 
(C) repayment of loans and advances other than from 
provident fund taken from University at such rates as the 
Registrar may decide; 

(ii) | optional deductions: 

The deductions falling under this category shall not be made 

except with the employee’s written consent: 

(A) premium due on Life Insurance Policies; 
(B) amount due to Co-operative Stores and Co-operative 
Credit Societies; 
(C) refund of advance taken from Provident Fund. 
(iii) The deduction of the following nature shall not be made from 
the subsistence allowance: 
(A) subscription to Provident Fund; 
(B) recovery of loss to University in which an employee is 
responsible. 


[भाग पा--_खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 291 


(25) The University may sanction to an employee, in any special 
circumstances, such special pay, personal pay, honorarium or fee 
on such conditions as may be prescribed by regulations. 

(26) (a) An employee shall be entitled to draw the pay of the post to 
which he is appointed from the date on which he assumed charge 
of the post, if joining on the forenoon of that day; otherwise, 
from the next day; 

(b) Unless the Vice-Chancellor, in view णए special 
circumstances, otherwise orders, pay in respect of any month 
shall become payable on the last working day of the month to 
which it relates; except for the month of March which shall be 
disbursed only on the first working day of April: 

(c) Unless the Vice-Chancellor otherwise directs an employee 
resigning from service of the University without giving the 
prescribed notice shall not be allowed to draw pay due but not 
drawn. 

(27) (a) Anemployee appointed to hold full additional charge of the 
duties of a higher post shall receive pay of the higher post; 

(b) An employee placed in charge of the full duties of a post of 
status equivalent to his own basic post shall receive allowances 
at the rate of 10% of the presumptive pay of the additional post; 
(c) No allowance shall be admissible when an employee holding 
one post is placed in-charge of the current duties of a post of 
equivalent status of his own basic post, the employee concerned 
shall receive pay in his basic post only; 


(d) An employee holding one post when placed in charge of the 
current duties of a lower post shall not receive any allowance for 
the additional work. 


Note: (i) The additional pay or allowance shall not be admissible if the 
period of additional charge is thirty days or less. 
(ii) The additional pay or allowance shall not be admissible for 
any period exceeding six months at a time. 

(28) |The employees of the University shall be eligible to draw 
Dearness Allowance, House Rent Allowance, City Compensatory 
Allowance, Travelling Allowance and other allowances as 
sanctioned by the University according to the rules in force from 
time to time and subject to the conditions prescribed for the 
drawl of the allowances. 

(29) Unless there is anything repugnant in the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya Act, Statutes, Ordinances, any amendments 
to fundamental rules and supplementary rules shall be deemed 
to be the amendments to the relevant provisions of these rules or 
any order or any administrative instructions already issued by the 
Central Government shall be deemed to be the orders or 
administrative instructions under these rules with effect from the 
date of such amendments or orders brought into force by the 
Central Government. 


45, _ Leave rules of non- (1) Leave of any kind shall not be availed as a matter of right, it 
seaching staff, > shall be refused or revoked by the sanctioning authority in the 
interest of the University, leave shall always be got sanctioned 


292 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


in advance, except in case of urgency. 
(2) Subject to (1) above, the non-teaching staff of the University 
shall be eligible for the following kinds of leave. namely: - 


(a) Duty Leave.- 
For attending duties related to the Universities or duties 
assigned by the sanctioning authority of the University for 
matters of interest to the University: 


(b) Casual Leave 
(i) total casual leave granted shall not exceed 8 days in a 
calendar year; 
(ii) casual leave shall not be combined with any other 
kind of leave except special casual leave, however, such 
casual leave may be combined with holidays including 
Sundays, holidays or Sundays falling within the period 
of casual leave shall not be counted as casual leave; 


(c) Special Casual Leave: 
(A) Special casual leave, not exceeding fifteen days in an 
academic year, may be granted- 


(i) to attend invited Programs, Public Service 
Commission or board of examination or other 
similar bodies or institutions; 

(ii) 10 visit other academic institutions to be in the 
selection board, etc; 

(iii) 160 undergo sterilisation operation (vasectomy or 
salpingectomy) under family welfare Programme, 
leave in this case shall be restricted to six 
working days; and 

(iv) to a female non-teaching staff who undergoes 
non-puerperal sterilisation, leave in this case shall 
be restricted to fourteen days; 

(B) Special casual leave shall not be accumulated, nor shall 
it be combined with any other kind of leave except casual 
leave, it may be granted in combination with holidays or 
vacation by the sanctioning authority on each occasion. 


(d) Earned Leave.- 
As per the Government of India Rules; 
(e) Half-pay Leave.- 
As per the Government of India Rules; 
(f) Commuted Leave 
Commuted leave, not exceeding half the amount of half 
pay leave due, may be granted on the basis of medical 
certificate from a registered medical practitioner to a 
permanent non-teaching staff subject to the following 
conditions, namely: - 
(i) commuted leave during the entire service shall be 
limited to a maximum of two hundred forty days; 
(ii) when commuted leave is granted, twice the amount 
of such leave shall be debited against the half-pay 
leave due; and 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 293 


(iii) the total duration of earned leave and commuted 
leave taken in conjunction shall not exceed two 
hundred forty days at a time, 

Provided that no commuted leave shall 
be granted under these rules unless the Authority 
Competent to sanction leave has reason to believe that the 
non-teaching staff shall return to duty on its expiry. 


(g) Extraordinary Leave: 

(i) a permanent Non-Teaching staff may be granted 
extraordinary leave when no other leave is admissible 
or other leave is admissible and the non-teaching staff 
applies in writing for the grant of extraordinary leave; 

(ii) extraordinary leave shall always be without pay and 
allowances, extraordinary leave shall not count for 
increment except in the following cases: 

(iii) leave taken on the basis of medical certificates; 

(iv) cases where the Vice-Chancellor is satisfied that the 
leave was taken due to causes beyond the control of 
the non-teaching staff, such as inability to join or re- 
join duty due to civil commotion or a natural 
calamity, 

provided the non-teaching staff has no 
other kind of leave to his credit; 

(v) leave taken for pursuing higher studies; 

(vi) leave granted to accept an invitation to a teaching 
post or fellowship or research- cum- teaching post or 
on assignment for technical or academic work of 
importance; 

(vii) extraordinary leave may be combined with any other 
leave except casual leave and special casual leave, 
provided that the total period of continuous absence 
from duty on leave (including periods of vacation 
when such vacation is taken in conjunction with 
leave) shall not exceed three years except in cases 
where leave is taken on medical certificate, the total 
period of absence from duty shall in no case exceed 
five years in the full working life of the individual. 

(viii) the authority empowered to grant leave may 
commute retrospectively periods of absence without 
leave into extraordinary leave; 


(h) Leave Not Due; 

(i) leave not due, may, at the discretion of the Vice- 
Chancellor, be granted to a permanent non-teaching 
staff for a period not exceeding three hundred sixty 
days during the entire period of service, out of which 
not more than ninety days at a time and one hundred 
eighty days in all may be otherwise than on medical 
certificate, such leave shall be debited against the 
half-pay leave earned by him subsequently; 

(ii) | ‘Leave not due' shall not be granted, unless the Vice- 
Chancellor is satisfied that as far as shall reasonably 


294 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


be foreseen, the non-teaching staff shall return to 
duty on the expiry of the leave and earn the leave 
granted; 

(ii) A non-teaching staff to whom ‘leave not due’ is 
granted shall not be permitted to tender his 
resignation from service so long as the debit balance 
in his leave account is not wiped off by active 
service, or he refunds the amount paid to him as pay 
and allowances for the period not so earned, in a 
case where retirement is unavoidable on account of 
reason of ill health, incapacitating the non-teaching 
staff for further service, refund of leave salary for 
the period of leave still to be earned may be waived 
by the Executive Council; 

Provided that the Executive Council 


may waive off, in any other exceptional, for reasons to be 
recorded in writing, the refund of leave 
salary for the period of leave still to be earned. 


(i) Maternity Leave (as per Government of India Rules): 

(i) maternity leave on full pay may be granted to a 
woman non-teaching staff for a period not exceeding 
one hundred eighty days, to be availed of twice in 
the entire career, maternity leave may also be 
granted in case of miscarriage including abortion, 
subject to the condition that the total leave granted 
in respect of this to a woman non-teaching staff in 
her career is not more than forty-five days, and the 
application for leave is supported by a medical 
certificate; 

(ii) maternity leave may be combined with earned leave, 
half pay leave or extraordinary leave but any leave 
applied for in continuation of maternity leave may 
be granted if the request is supported by a medical 
certificate; 


(j) Child Care Leave (As per Government of India Rules) : 
Women non-teaching staff having minor children may be 
granted leave up to two years for taking care of their minor 
children, child care leave for a maximum period of two 
years (730 days) may be granted to the women non- 
teaching staff during entire service period in lines with 
Central Government women employees, this leave may be 
as per rules applicable to the women employees of the 
Government of India; 

(k) Paternity Leave (As per Government of India Rules) 
Paternity leave of 15 days may be granted to male non- 
teaching staff during the confinement of their wives, and 
such leave shall be granted only up to two children; 

(1) Adoption leave (as per Government of India Rules): 
Adoption leave may be provided as per the rules of the 
Government of India; 

(m) Study Leave: Study leave to a non-teaching member of 
staff shall be in accordance with the Government of India 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 295 


Rules; 
(n) Non-Teaching Staff appointed on Probation: 


A non-teaching staff appointed as a probationer against a 
substantive vacancy and with definite terms of probation 
shall during the period of probation be granted leave which 
shall be admissible to him if he has held this post 
substantively otherwise than on probation, if a person in 
the permanent service of the University is appointed ‘on 
probation’ to a higher post, during probation, he shall not 
be deprived of the benefit of leave rules applicable to his 
permanent post; 

(०) Non-Teaching Staff Appointed on Contract Basis: 
Non-teaching staff appointed on contract basis shall be 
entitled to leave as under: 

(i) earned leave or half pay leave or casual leave as 
admissible to whole time temporary non-teaching 
staff of the University; 

(ii) in the case of contract appointments for one year of 
less, unless on medical grounds, no extraordinary 
leave shall be granted; 

(iii) in the case of contract appointments for more than one 
year but less than five years, extraordinary leave as 
admissible to temporary non-teaching staff subject to 
the condition that the total period of extraordinary 
leave during the entire contract period shall not exceed 
ninety days, where the contract appointment is for five 
years or more, the extraordinary leave shall be as that 
admissible to the whole time temporary non-teaching 
staff; 

(p) General Conditions Applicable to All Categories of Non- 
Teaching staff: 

(a) No leave shall be claimed as matter of right, leave of 
any kind may be varied, refused or revoked by the 
Competent Authority empowered to grant it without 
assigning any reason, if that authority considers such 
action to be in the interest of the University; 

(b) No leave shall be granted to a non-teaching staff 
whom a Competent Authority has decided to dismiss, 
remove or compulsorily retire from service, nor shall 
any leave be granted to a non-teaching staff who is 
under suspension; 

(q) Maximum period of absence from duty on leave: 

(4) No non-teaching staff shall be granted leave of any 
kind for a continuous period exceeding five years; 

(ii) where a non-teaching staff does not resume duty after 
remaining on leave for a continuous period of five 
years or where a non-teaching staff after the expiry of 
his leave remains absent from duty, other than on 
foreign service or on account of suspension, for any 
period which together with the period of leave granted 
to his exceeds five years, he shall unless the 
Executive Council in view of the exceptional 


296 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


circumstances of the case otherwise determines, be 
removed from service after following prescribed 
procedure; 

(r) Application for Leave: 

Leave shall always be applied for in advance and the 

sanction of the Competent Authority obtained before it is 

availed of except in cases of emergency and for 
satisfactory reasons. 

Note: Faculty non-teaching staff shall not leave station till the 
order sanctioning leave has been issued. 

(s) Commencement and termination of leave: 

(i) leave ordinarily begins from the date on which leave 
as such is actually availed of and ends on the day the 
Non-Teaching staff resumes his duty; 

(ii) Sunday and other recognised holidays may be 
prefixed or suffixed to the leave with the permission 
of the Authority Competent to sanction the leave; 

(t) Re-joining of duty before the expiry of the leave: 

(i) a non-teaching staff may return to duty before the 
expiry of the leave granted to him, with the 
permission of the Competent Authority; 

(ii) notwithstanding anything contained in (a) above, a 
non-teaching staff on leave preparatory to retirement 
shall be precluded from withdrawing his request for 
permission to retire and from returning to duty, save 
with the consent of the Executive Council; 

(u) Leave on medical grounds to be supported by medical 
certificates: 

A non-teaching staff who applies for leave on medical 

grounds shall support his application with a medical 

certificate from a Registered Medical Practitioner 
empanelled by the University, leave or extension of leave 
on medical certificate shall not be granted beyond the date 
on which a non-teaching staff is pronounced by a Medical 

Board constituted by the University for the purpose to be 

permanently incapacitated for further service;

ground: No non-teaching staff who has been granted leave 
(other than casual leave) on medical grounds shall be

University;

A non-teaching staff on leave shall not, without the written

in any trade or business whatsoever or in any private

honorarium is attached: 

Provided that this prohibition shall not 
apply to work undertaken in connection with the

any literary work or publication or radio or extension 
lectures, or with the permission of the Vice-Chancellor to

Absence without leave or overstay on leave: 
A non-teaching staff who absents himself without leave or 
remains absent without leave after the expiry of the leave 
granted to him, shall be entitled to no leave allowance or 
salary for the period of such absence, such period shall be 
debited against his leave account as leave is extended by 
the authority empowered to grant the leave, wilful absence 
from duty may be treated as misconduct. 

Conversion of one kind of leave to another 

(i) at the request of the non-teaching staff concerned, the 
University may convert retrospectively any kind of 
leave including extraordinary leave into a leave of 
different kind which was admissible to him at the time 
leave was originally taken, but he shall not claim such 
conversion as a matter of right; 

(ii) if one kind of leave is converted into another, the 
amount of leave salary and the allowances admissible 
shall be recalculated and arrears of leave salary and 
allowances paid or the amount overdrawn recovered, 
as the case may be. 

Increment during leave: 
If increment of pay falls during any leave other than casual 
leave, special casual leave, duty leave, or sabbatical leave, 
the effect of increase of pay shall be given from the date 
the non-teaching staff resumes duty without prejudice to 
the normal date of his increment, except in those cases 
where the leave does not count for increment;

For the purpose of these Ordinances, unless otherwise 
specified, the term ‘year’ shall mean a calendar year:

who retires or is retired from service may be granted suo 
motu, by the Competent Authority to grant leave, cash 
equivalent of leave salary in respect of earned leave at his 
credit subject to maximum number of days including days 
in respect of the half pay leave at his credit as specified by 
the Government of India or regulatory authorities from 
time to time. 

Note: Provided this period does not exceed the period between 
the date on which he so retires or is retired from service 
and the date on which he shall have retired in the normal 
course after attaining the age prescribed for superannuation 
under the terms and conditions governing his service, the 
cash equivalent shall be equal to the leave salary as 
admissible for earned leave and equal to the leave salary as 
admissible for half pay leave plus dearness allowance 
admissible on that leave salary for the first three hundred 
days, at the rates in force on the date the University 
employee so retires or is retired from service. The pension 
and pension equivalent of other retirement benefits and ad 
hoc relief or graded relief on pension shall be deducted 
from the leave salary paid for the period of half pay leave, 
if any, for which the cash equivalent is payable. The 
amount so calculated shall be paid in one lump-sum as a 
onetime settlement. This limit shall stand revised as per 
Government of India norms;

(v) Resignation or Termination: 

(a) Resignation: If a non-teaching staff (confirmed

suo moto by the Competent Authority to grant leave 
cash equivalent in respect of earned leave at his credit 
on the date of cessation of service, to the extent of half 
of such leave at his credit, subject to a maximum of 
one hundred fifty days; 

(b) Termination with respect to services of a non-teaching 
staff are terminated by notice or by payment of pay 
and allowances, in lieu of notice, or otherwise in 
accordance with the terms and conditions of his 
appointment, he may be granted, suo moto by the 
Competent Authority to grant leave, cash equivalent in 
respect of earned leave at his credit on the date on 
which he ceases to be in service subject to maximum 
of three hundred days and this limit shall stand revised 
as per Government of India norms. 

(vi) Dismissal or Removal: 

(a) When a non-teaching staff is dismissed or removed 
from service following disciplinary proceedings he 
shall not be eligible for leave encashment. 

(b) Leave encashment when permanently incapacitated. - 
A non-teaching staff who is declared by a duly 
constituted Medical Board to be completely and 
permanently incapacitated for further service may be 
granted, suo motu, by the Competent Authority to 
grant leave, cash equivalent of leave salary in 
respect of earned leave due and admissible on the 
date of his invalidation from service subject to leave 
salary for a maximum three hundred days, this limit 
shall stand revised as per Government of India 
Norms; 

The amount payable shall be equal to the leave 
salary as calculated under the relevant rules; 

(c) Leave encashment of a non-teaching staff who dies 
while in harness, in case a non-teaching staff dies in 
harness, the cash equivalent of the leave salary that 
the deceased non-teaching staff shall have got, had 
he gone on earned leave, but for the death, due and 
admissible on the date immediately following the 
date of death subject to a maximum of leave salary 
for three hundred days shall be paid to his family 
and this limit shall stand revised as per Government 
of India Norms;

unless, 

(a) where the number of Visitor’s nominee and the persons 
nominated by the Executive Council is four in all, at least three 
of them attend the meeting; and 

(b) where the number of Visitor’s nominee and the persons 
nominated by the Executive Council is three in all, at least two 
of them attend the meeting. 

(4) The Chairperson of the Selection Committee shall be entitled to 
vote at the meeting and shall have, and exercise a casting vote in 
the case of an equality of votes. 

(5) The meeting of Selection Committee shall be held at 
University’s office or at such other place as the Vice-Chancellor, 
or in his absence Pro Vice-Chancellor, may decide. 

(6) The Selection Committee shall consider and submit to the 
Executive Council recommendations as to the appointment 
referred to it. 

(7) The Chairperson of the Selection Committee shall have the 
power to lay down the procedure in respect of any matter not 
covered by this Ordinance. 

(8) The University shall have the right to restrict the number of 
candidates to be called for interview, based on _ the 
recommendations of the Screening Committee constituted as per 
the Regulations for this purpose, to a reasonable number on the 
basis of qualifications and experience higher than the minimum 
prescribed. 

It shall be open to the Executive Council to offer appointment to 

suitable persons, however, the Vice-Chancellor may offer 

appointment with the condition that such cases may be got 
ratified in the next Executive Council Meeting as expressly 
approved by the Executive Council. 

(10) The rules and procedures prescribed by the Government of India 
in respect of the reserved categories shall be followed.

50... Anti-plagiarism policy.- (1) The University shall endeavor to maintain the highest levels of 
academic integrity to encourage original work amongst its students, 
faculty, staff and employees at all levels and the University shall take 
measures to eliminate plagiarism. 

Explanation.- For the purpose of this Ordinances the expression 
‘Plagiarism’ shall mean the passing off of another person’s work as 
one’s own work without acknowledgement of the source, and 
involves copying of phrases, sentences, paragraphs or longer extracts 
from published or unpublished work including from the Internet. 

(2) Modes of Plagiarism: 
The following are considered plagiarism, namely:- 
(a) Turning in someone else's work as your own; 
(b) Copying words or ideas from someone else without giving 
credit; 
(c) Failing to put a quotation in quotation marks; 
(d) Giving incorrect information about the source of a quotation; 
(e) Changing words but copying the sentence structure of a source 
without giving credit; 
(f) Copying so many words or ideas from a source that it makes 
up the majority of your work, whether you give credit or not 
(i.e. not "fair use") 
(3) ‘No-Plagiarism Declaration’ by Students: 
Every student who is required to submit a thesis or dissertation or 
project report or research paper or assignment as part of the 
requirement for award of a Post-Graduate or Doctoral degree shall be 
required to submit a signed statement at the beginning of the Post 
Graduate or Doctor of Philosophy Programme that he is aware of the 
University’s Anti-Plagiarism policy and shall abide by it and he shall 
submit a “No-Plagiarism Declaration” with every thesis or dissertation 
or project report or research paper or assignment submitted by him. 
(4) Responsibility for detecting Plagiarism committed by Students: 
It shall be the primary responsibility of the supervisor or guide or 
teacher to detect the plagiarism, if any, committed by the student 
under his charge, the supervisor or guide or teacher shall run the thesis 
or dissertation or research paper or assignment through the prescribed 
anti-plagiarism detection tool to ensure that it reflects genuine research 
and original work, and he shall furnish a certificate to this effect, a 
supervisor or guide or teacher who fails to put in due diligence to 
detect plagiarism shall be liable for disciplinary action. 
(5) Plagiarism by Employees: 
Teachers and other employees who submit articles or research papers 
or consultancy reports or project reports or who present papers or 
deliver orations in seminars and conferences shall be required to 
uphold the highest ethical standards and not resort to plagiarism and f 
any plagiarism comes to light, they shall be liable for disciplinary 
action. 
(6) Procedure for handling allegations of Plagiarism: 
(i) whenever an allegation of plagiarism is brought to the 


[AMT 1--खण्ड 4] 


भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 305 


University’s notice either by the supervisor or guide or teacher 
or by any other source against a student or an Ex-student or an 
employee, the Vice Chancellor may refer the same to the Board 
of Research Studies for an inquiry; 


(ii) the Board of research studies may conduct an inquiry on its own 


(ii) 


or constitute a Standing sub-committee to inquire into allegations 
of plagiarism and the Board or sub-committee shall observe the 
principle of natural justice and afford the student or Ex-student 
or employee against whom the allegation of plagiarism has been 
received a reasonable opportunity to show cause against the 
allegation; 

the inquiry shall be completed expeditiously especially where a 
student is on the verge of completing his program and the Board 
or sub-committee shall submit its report to the Vice Chancellor 
with (i) a clear-cut finding as to whether plagiarism has been 
committed or not, and (ii) if committed, an opinion on the gravity 
of the offence (expressed as a percentage of total number of 
sentences); 


(iv) the Vice Chancellor is at liberty to accept the findings of the 


Board or sub-committee in full or to deviate from the same for 
reasons to be recorded in writing. 


(v) depending on the gravity of the offence, the Vice Chancellor in 
respect of students, and the Competent Authority in respect of 
employees, may award punishment as per the norms prescribed 
below in the table:

[भाग [खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 307 


(2) Office of research and development: 


Office of Research and Development is set up to provide specialised 
administrative and managerial support for the operation of 
Sponsored Research Projects, Consultancy Projects, Executive and 
Continuing Education and other Research and Development related 
activities of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, the Office of Research 
and Development facilitates interaction with national and 
international agencies supporting research and development, it also 
promotes academia-industry interactions and collaboration with 
larges Research and Development laboratories, and liaises between 
Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya and funding agencies and it supports 
faculty members in filing of patents and documenting Intellectual 
Property Rights generated through research activity of the Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya. 


(3) Functions: 
(a) Project Management 
The Research and Development Office supports following project 
management activities: 

(i) Submitting the Proposals:- The proposals seeking funding 
for undertaking sponsored, consultancy or outreach activities 
are routed through the office and the request of the Project 
Investigator is duly forwarded to the Faculty-In-Charge or 
Research and Development to ensure that all administrative 
matters are examined; 

(ii) Registering the Project:- When a project is sanctioned by a 
funding agency or industry, a new project registration form 
is filled by the faculty member and submitted along with the 
sanction letter, agreement, Memorandum of Understanding 
and proposal, on registration, a unique project account 
number is assigned and budget heads are earmarked based 
on the approval of funding agency along with the respective 
amounts sanctioned and the project is made operational on 
receipt of the grant in the form of cheque or bank transfer; 

(ii) Operating the Project: The Project Investigator 
and Co- Project Investigator have the authority to expend 
from the project account, subject to the approval, as per the 
delegation of the financial powers of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya, the expenditure shall not exceed the 
sanctioned amount for the respective budget heads, re- 
appropriation of funds between various budgets heads is 
permitted only with the prior approval of the funding agency 
and when such an approval is available from the funding 
agency, the Project Investigator may request for the re- 
appropriation of funds along with the approval of the 
funding agency; 

(iv) Submission of Progress Reports and other deliverables:- 
The Project Investigator is responsible to the funding agency 
for the deliverables of the project and timely submission of 
various reports; 

(v) Accounts and Auditing of Funds:- A statement of accounts 
shall be made available to the Project Investigator on request 


308 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


and the accounts of the Office of Research and Development 
are audited annually; 

(vi) Utilisation Certificate: The utilisation certificate of a 
registered project is issued at the request of the Project 
Investigator after auditing of all expenditures incurred in the 
project, the utilization certificate of individual project is 
prepared based on the audited statements, this may be sent to 
the funding agency by the Project Investigator. The invoice 
seeking grant-in-aid or funds shall be issued by the Office on 
request of Project Investigator and the Project Investigator is 
responsible for sending the invoice or grant-in-aid bills to the 
funding agency as per the requirement of project; 


(vii) Project Extension:- The duration of the project may extend 


on submission of letter stating the same from the funding 
agency and the Project Investigator or Co-Project Investigator 
shall coordinate with the funding agency; 


(viii) Project Closure and Completion Report: The Project 


Investigator is responsible for submitting the project 
completion report to the funding agency, the office shall close 
the project account on submission of acceptance certificate 
from the funding agency for closing the project, at the time of 
closing the project, unutilised fund, if any, shall be returned to 
the funding agency, in consultancy projects, the balance 
amount can be utilised according to approved norms; 


(b) Stores and Purchase Office: 


(० 


(i) | All purchases under a project need to be made as per the 
norms of Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, items purchased 
using the project funds are property of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya and shall be used solely for project 
purposes during the term of the Project, on closure of the 
project, they are the property of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya, unless there is a specific provision or 
requirement of returning the same to the funding agency; 

(ii) consequently, each such item shall be recorded in the Assets 
Register of the Office of Stores and Purchase, the equipment 
may be used for other activities after the term of the project, 
with the approval of the Vice Chancellor; 

(iii) in cases where the funding is by a private agency, the Vice 
Chancellor may approve appropriate procurement mode 
with the consent of the funding agency (not limited to the 
General Finance Rules provisions);

(i) checking the financial aspects of the proposal prepared by 
the Project Investigator to ensure compliance with Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya polices; 

(ii) assigning the Project Number and maintenance of the 
project files; 

(iii) preparation of receipt or payment vouchers; 
(iv) disbursement of compensation of project staff members;

Establishment Office: 
This office undertakes two primary tasks, namely appointment of 
project staff members, maintenance of their personal file, leave 
records, House Rent Allowances, contract agreements, etc., in 
particular, the activities undertaken by the office are: 

(i) Seeking approval of Draft Advertisement and Selection 


Committee proposed by Project Investigator 

(A) Project Investigator shall send a draft advertisement 
along with a proposed Selection Committee to the 
office for approval against the vacant post; and 

(B) After approval, the advertisement shall be published 
by the office on the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
website; 


(ii) Screening applications 


(A) The Project Investigator shall fix the meeting for 
screening of the applications received, after screening, 
the Project Investigator shall submit to the office the 
report along with the proposed schedule of the 
interview; and 

(B) The office shall issue the call letter to the applicants 
called for the interview; 


(iii) Interview of the applicants 


(A) The Project Investigator shall arrange the Selection 
Committee meeting on the scheduled date and time, 
and conduct the interview; and 

(B) After interview, the Project Investigator shall submit 
to the Establishment office the Report of the Selection 
Committee along with applications of recommended 
candidates; 


(iv) Appointment of the project Staff Member 


(A) After scrutiny of the report of the Selection 
Committee, the office shall submit the 
recommendation to the Vice Chancellor for approval; 
and 

(B) After Vice Chancellor’s approval, the offer letter shall 
be issued by the office; 


(v) Joining of Project Staff Member and Signing of Contract 


Agreement 
(A) Selected candidate shall report to the Project 


310 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


Investigator for joining the duties, the Project 
Investigator shall submit the joining report to the 
Establishment Office; 
(B) After acceptance of the joining report, a unique 
Project Personnel File Number is assigned and a 
personal file shall be opened at the Office; and 
(C) Contract agreement shall be signed by the Project 
Staff Member and copy of the same is shared with 
Finance. 
(3) FACULTY-IN-CHARGE (RESEARCH AND 
DEVELOPMENT): 
A faculty member belonging to the disciplines of Engineering or 
Science shall be appointed by the Vice Chancellor as Faculty in 
Charge (Research and Development), the Faculty in Charge 
(Research and Development) shall steer the activities of the Office 
of Research and Development and he shall be replaced by the Dean 
(Research and Development) once appointed. 


(4) RESEARCH AND DEVELOPMENT COMMITTEE (DAC): 


The Research and Development Committee shall be proposed by 
Faculty in Charge (Research and Development) and approved by the 
Vice Chancellor, it consists of: Faculty in Charge (Research and 
Development); Faculty in Charge of each Department or Domain; and 
Faculty in Charge (Laboratories). Research and Development 
Committee has the mandate to: 

(i) encourage faculty members to write research proposals to 
national and international funding agencies; 

(ii) propose policies related to conduct of Research and 
Development Projects at the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya; 

(iii) coordinate the signing of Memoranda of Understating and Non- 
Disclosure Agreements with others organisations, Universities 
and Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalayas; 

(iv) streamline the functions of the Office of Research and 
Development and guide it from time to time; 

(v) ensure that all finances are handled as per the norms of funding 
agency, Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya and Government of India, 
and the utilisation certificates are submitted in a timely manner 
to the funding agencies; 

(vi) ensure all purchase and finance related items are routed through 
the office of Stores and Purchase and Finance office; and 

(vii) interface with the Project Investigator to do the needed 
communication with the funding agencies. 


(5) Project investigator: 

(1) Each project shall have a Project Investigator, who is a faculty 
member in the regular service of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya or on contract service, the Vice Chancellor 
may appoint a Group ‘A’ officer as a Project Investigator of 
projects that are primarily meant to steer specific activities of the 
Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya. 

(2) The Project Investigator shall be solely responsible for: 

(a) Formulating project proposals, which may include; 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 311 


(i) planning the scope of the work to be done; 

(ii) estimating cost involved in achieving the objectives of 
the project; and 

(ili) identifying Co-Investigators, if required, who are faculty 
members of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya; 

(b) Coordinating and executing the work; 

(c) Handling all communications with the funding agency; 

(d) Submitting intermediate and final reports to funding agencies 
according to the project proposal; 

(e) Ensuring that all reports bear the name of the Project 
Investigator and his signatures, and the name of the other 
Investigators who participated in the project; 

(f) Signing the Memorandum of Understanding or agreements 
with the funding agency, if required, after the approval of 
the Vice Chancellor; and 

(g) Making recommendations to the Vice Chancellor regarding 
honoraria to be paid to faculty members, staff members and 
students, if applicable. 

(3) (a) While making an estimate of the funds required for a project, 
the following budgets heads shall be taken into account: 
(i) salaries of Project Staff Members and Doctor of 
Philosophy students; 
(ii) equipment; 
(iii) consumables; 
(iv) contingency; 
(v) travel (domestic and international); 
(vi) others (if any); and 
(vii) overhead. 


(4) While estimating the budget required under compensation head, 
the Project Investigator has to identify the positions required 
depending on the nature of research, the proposal once approved 
by the funding agency for a specific category of staff members, 
their emoluments cannot be changed or altered, in case the 
funding agency has provided the consolidated fund under 
compensation head, then positions and emoluments as per the 
Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya’s norms shall be applicable for 
Research and Development staff member appointment. 

(5) Incase any equipment is to be returned to the funding agency or 
organisation after completion of the project, the same shall be 
clearly brought out explicitly in the project proposal, supported 
by written communication from the funding agency to this effect. 

(6) The Project Investigator can co-opt other faculty members of 
the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya as Investigators. The Vice 
Chancellor may permit, at the request of the Project Investigator, 
a Group ‘A’ Officer of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya as Co- 
Project Investigator of a project. 

(7) The Project Investigator shall prepare research project 
proposal in conformity with: 

(i) designation and emoluments rates for project. staff 
members, commensurate with the qualification and 


312 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


experience specified by the funding agency or the Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya; 

(ii) provision for Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Overhead 
Charges as per the norms of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya; 

(iii) other guidelines issued by the funding agency; 

(iv) applicable rules, regulations and Statutes of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya. 

(v) the Project Investigator shall be responsible to get project 
work completed satisfactorily within the sanctioned grant; 

(vi) the Project Investigator shall ensure that the head-wise 
expenditure does not exceed the budgetary allocation as 
sanctioned by funding agency; 

(vii) in case a Project Investigator leaves the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya or retires or proceeds on leave or is 
unavailable for some reason, the co- Project Investigator 
may be appointed by the Vice Chancellor as the Project 
Investigator or the Vice Chancellor, on _ the 
recommendation of the Faculty-in-Charge (Research and 
Development), on the request of Project Investigator, OR 
on the request of the Faculty-in-Charge of the concerned 
Department or domain, shall appoint a new Faculty-in- 
Charge, who shall assume the powers and responsibilities 
of the Faculty-in-Charge, the new Faculty-in-Charge shall 
need to furnish an undertaking for completion of the 
project with the remaining funds within the duration 
specified by the funding agency; 

(viii) project file shall be closed with the submission of final 
project report and final settlement of accounts. 

(8) Types of projects: 

(1) The Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya encourages faculty members 
to seek external funds from Government and non- government 
agencies to pursue Research and Development activities in areas 
of their competence, the Office of Research and Development 
administers these projects in keeping with the guidelines agreed 
upon with the funding agencies, the office may administer the 
following types of Research and Development projects, namely:- 

(a) sponsored Research Projects; 
(b) consultancy Projects; 

(c) outreach Projects; and 

(d) routine Testing Projects. 

(2) In addition, it administers other projects based on funding from 

other funding agencies in the form of 
(a) Seed Grant; 
(b) Professional Development Account; and 
(c) Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Projects. 

(3) In addition to the above, the Executive and Continuing Education 
Programs externally funded are construed to be a project. 


(9) SPONSORED RESEARCH PROJECTS: 
(1) Individual or a group of faculty members of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya can write research proposal to seek 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 313 


competitive grants from extra-mural research funding agencies 
in India or abroad. Faculty members may approach various 
national (e.g., Department of Science and Technology, 
Department of Biotechnology, Council of Scientific and 
Industrial Research, Board of Research in Nuclear Sciences, 
Aeronautics Research and Development Board, Defence 
Research and Development Organisation, Indian Space Research 
Organisation, etc.,) or international (e.g., bilateral government 
agencies, such as Indo-Japan and Indo-Russian agencies, etc.,) 
funding agencies and industries for a sponsored research project 
and the Project Investigator shall adhere to the funding agency 
norms alongside complying with the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya terms and conditions. 

(2) In specific instances, the Research and Development Office also 
administers the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Collaborative 
Projects, which are projects seeking fund simultaneously from 
the funding agency and Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, the project 
proposal shall bifurcate clearly the money requested from Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya and the funding agency and the Project 
Investigator shall obtain due approval of the Vice Chancellor 
before indicating such internal financial support to external 
agencies; with due financial concurrence. 

(3) Sponsored research project proposals may be submitted as per the 
format of the respective funding agencies, generally, these 
formats are available on the websites of the agencies and the 
following points shall be considered while writing the proposal: 

(4) The Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Overhead (typically 20% to 
30%) shall be decided by the Executive Council from time to 
time, any departure from the above shall be duly notified to and 
approval sought from the Vice Chancellor before sending such a 
proposal, in special cases on the recommendation of the 
Research and Development committee, the Vice Chancellor can 
give partial waiver of the Overhead and the Calculation of Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Overhead shall be as per table.

(5) The government funding agencies (like Department of Science 
and Technology, Department of Biotechnology, Council of 
Scientific and Industrial Research, Board of Research in Nuclear 
Sciences, Aeronautics Research and Development Board, 
Defence Research and Development Organisation and Indian 
Space Research Organisation) have their own overhead norms, 
Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya overhead shall be charged as per 


314 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


the norms of government funding agency where such a provision 
is available. 

(6) Endorsement certificate shall be printed on Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya letterhead, and shall be signed by Project 
Investigator, Faculty-In-Charge (Research and Development) 
and the Vice Chancellor and the Vice Chancellor may delegate 
the Registrar to sign the endorsement certificate, on his behalf. 

(7) Both soft and hard copies of the proposal shall be submitted to 
office of Research and Development for records, before sending 
the same to the funding agencies. 

(8) The research project proposal must contain a clear statement of 
yearly expenditure under different heads, this proposed 
scheduled expenditure and manpower mentioned in proposal 
shall be adhered to by the Project Investigator, any deviation 
from the stated head needs prior approval of sponsoring agency, 
in case, where the funding agency revises the budget under the 
different heads, the budget approved by the funding agency shall 
be binding. 

(9) The Intellectual Property and Patents generated during the 
sponsored project shall be shared by the funding agency and Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, the proposal shall clarify this, in the 
absence of this, guidelines developed by the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya shall be binding on both the parties. 

(10) When a project is approved by the funding agency, a new project 
account shall be opened with the Office of Research and 
Development for its operation and monitoring. 

(11) Utilisation certificate shall be submitted by Project Investigator 
on time to the sponsoring agency. 

(12) After completion of the project, final technical report shall be 
submitted to the funding agency, with a copy to the Research and 
Development office for record. 

(13) After submission of the final technical report, a completion 
certificate shall be obtained from the funding agency for 
successful completion of the project based on which, the project 
account shall be closed after settlement of all the committed 
expenditures. 

(14) After final notification of closure from the sponsoring agency, 
assets generated from the project, shall be audited by the Office 
of Store and Purchase, the asset transfer, if any, shall be 
facilitated by the office of Research and Development. 

(15) No honorarium or consultation fee can be charged from the 
sponsored research projects unless it was clearly mentioned in 
project proposal, approved and sanctioned by the funding 
agency, and agreed upon by Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya. 

(16) Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya neither charges any interest from 
the funding agencies in case of any delay in the reimbursement 
of expenditure or transfer of the funds pledged by the funding 
agency towards the execution of the project, nor pays any 
interest, if earned, on the grant received.

(10) Consultancy projects: 


The total agreed charges of a Consultancy Project shall consist of 
the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Overhead and other expenses the 
other expenses shall cover the following costs related to the project, 
the Goods and Services Tax shall be applicable as per government 
rules; in addition: 

(a) Permanent equipment to be procured or fabricated for the 
equipment or models; 

(b) Remuneration or consultancy fee of the Faculty and Staff 
Members; 

(c) Consumable materials; 

(d) Travel expenses in connection with the project work; 

(e) Computational or other charges the Project Investigator may 
have to pay to the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya or any other 
outside agency in the course of the execution of the work; 

(f) Charges to be paid for the use of specific equipment in the 
departments or central facilities; 

(g) Contingency expenses to cover cost of supplies, preparation 
of report, typing, word processing, drawing, drafting, 
stationery, reproduction, literature (books, journals, 
membership fee for professional societies), postage, courier, 
communication expenses, cost of insurance of personnel 
(medical insurance, etc.,) or equipment being used for the 
project; 

(h) Expenses for work to be carried out on payment basis, 
remuneration to student assistants; 

(i) Insurance on equipment and manpower during travel; and 

(0) Any other costs considered appropriate. 

Faculty-in-Charge shall make sure availability of required 
funds under the respective head prior to approving the 
expenditure under the project. 


Faculty members may offer technical or managerial consultancy 
services to governments, industries and private individuals, based on 
his competence, acting as Project Investigator of the project, these 
projects shall provide challenges technically and befit the 
professional competence of the faculty member; else the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya shall not accept to undertake the project. Further, 
the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya shall not be liable in any way for 
the negative fallout of any consultancy service offered by the Project 
Investigator. And, the Project Investigator is advised to write clear 
agreements with the funding agencies to limit their own liability. 


316 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


(i) the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Overhead (typically 10 to 
30%) shall be decided by the Executive Council from time to 
time, after deducting the Goods and Services Tax, the Goods 
and Services Tax on Consultancy Projects shall be as per the 
rates prevalent and decided by the Government of India; 

(ii) the total fee of the project shall be paid by the funding agency 
100 the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya before the 
commencement of the work, no payment made by the funding 
agency for consultancy projects shall be routed other than 
through the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, this means, that 
whenever payments are received, they shall be received by the 
Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya; the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya shall do the needful to comply with the 
statutory laws and then release the honorarium to the faculty 
and staff members as applicable and the expenditure and 
disbursements shall be made as per the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya norms; 

(iii) once a Consultancy Project is approved by the funding agency 
and the funds are received at the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya, a new project account shall be opened in 
the Office of Research and Development, each proposal must 
contain a clear statement of the honorarium or consultancy fee 
to be paid to the hired consultants, if any and if this is not 
written in the proposal, no consultant can be hired; 

(iv) a completion certificate shall be obtained from the funding 
agency after successful completion of the project, based on 
this, 

(A) Project Investigators and others involved shall be paid 
the honorarium or consultancy fee, the total honorarium 
paid to the Faculty and Staff Members of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya shall not exceed 65% of the 
consultancy fee charged to the agency minus the Goods 
and Services Tax; and 

(B) Project shall be closed; 

(v) after the completion of the project, a final report shall be 
submitted to the funding agency with a copy to Office of 
Research and Development for record; with a copy to 
Finance; 

(vi) a Non-Disclosure Agreement, if required, shall be signed 
apriori between the Project Investigator and funding agency, 
also, the Intellectual Property and Patents generated during 
the project shall be shared by the funding agency and Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, the proposals shall clarify this and in 
the absence of such a clarification, guidelines developed by 
the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya shall be binding on both the 
parties; 

(vii) the fund received may be used to cover the expenses related 
the site visit, meetings outside the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya and participation in conferences within 
India or abroad, there is no restriction from the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya on travel by air or taxi on grounds of 
entitlement, ctual boarding and lodging expenses shall be paid 


[भाग [खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 317 


on production of payment receipt, as per the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya norms or as per the funding agency’s norms 
(if any), This shall be within the budget provisions given in 
the respective heads; 

(viii) The time spent by the Project Investigator on all consultancy 
projects together shall be limited to one day per week, on an 
average, absence of Project Investigator on grounds of 
consultancy project from the campus shall be limited to thirty 
days a year, and not to exceed seven working days during the 
semester, sufficient caution needs to be exercised to ensure 
that consultancy projects do not interfere with the normal 
duties of the faculty members, no alternate arrangement shall 
be made by Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya to compensate the 
class or lecture or lab load of the faculty;

(11) Routine testing projects: 

Faculty members may undertake routine testing tasks, which are 
required by the Industries or utility companies or other institutions 
for the performance evaluation of their specific product or projects, 
the routine testing projects involve utilisation of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya laboratory facilities, Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
does not allow to undertaking any certification work, unless certain 
facilities of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya are recognised by 
appropriate authority of the country for this purpose, as and when 
the testing equipment is ready to be extended for testing by outside 
agencies, depending on the price of the equipment, maintenance cost 
and the labour involved, appropriate testing charges may be 
proposed by the Faculty-in-Charge (Research and Development) 
involving the faculty concerned. 

The following points shall be adhered to: 

(a) Only non-specialised equipment shall be used in testing 
projects; 

(b) The testing may be done at standard fee estimated by the 
Faculty Member, who shall supervise the testing work. Goods 
and Services Tax at the applicable rates shall be received on 
this source and paid to the Government of India; 

(C) After deducting Goods and Services Tax, the balance amount 
shall be distributed as per the Table 5: 


[AMT 1--खण्ड 4]

Outreach projects: 


(a) Faculty members can organise Conferences, Seminars, 
Workshop or Short-term Courses with funds from outside 
agencies, after seeking due approval of the Vice Chancellor. 


(b) The Office of Research and Development shall facilitate the use 
of such funds received from external agencies. 


(c) Faculty members shall open an account in the Office of Research 
and Development, clearly indicating the purpose of the event 
being organised. 


(d) If the funds are raised from the multiple funding agencies (e.g., 
governmental and non-governmental agencies), then sub-project 
needs to be created, under the umbrella of the same project 
account, clearly stating the expenditure heads in each sub-project. 


(९) The Faculty member concerned shall prepare a detailed proposal 
with various budget heads for expenditure (indicating the faculty 
honoraria or boarding or lodging or transport, etc), and submitted 
to the Vice Chancellor through the Research and Development 
Office and Finance. 

The Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Overhead (typically 10% to 
30%) shall be decided by the Executive Council from time to time, 
after deducting the Goods and Services Tax, and to be raised from 
external funding agencies, after the event completed, utilization 
certificate duly issued by office of Research and Development 
shall be submitted especially to the governmental funding 
agencies, after deducting Goods and Services Tax, the balance 
amount shall be distributed as Table 6.

(14) Seed Grant Projects: 


The Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya supports newly joined young 
Faculty Members for initiating his/her research at Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya, with a sum of Rs. 10 Lakhs for Faculty Members 
with background in Science and Technology, and with Rs. 5 Lakhs 
for Faculty Members with background in Management. 

(a) The new Faculty Member shall submit a Seed Grant Proposal 
to Office of Research and Development; 

(b) The Faculty Member shall present the same to a Committee 
consisting of: 

(i) Vice Chancellor; 

(ii) Dean (Academics) 

(iii) Dean (Research & Development) or Faculty-in-Charge 
(Research and Development). 

(c) All Faculty Members, especially those in the domain of the 
proposal, are invited to the presentation; 

(d) If the proposal is accepted in principle, suggestions made by 
the Seed Grant Proposal Review Committee shall be 
incorporated and the revised proposal submitted by the 
Faculty Member to the office of Research and Development, 
on receiving the revised proposal, Faculty-in-Charge 
(Research and Development) may recommend to the Vice 
Chancellor for approval of the Seed Grant; and 

(e) After approval of the Vice Chancellor, a Seed Grant Project 
account shall be opened by the Office of Research and 
Development, clearly stating the budget utilisation under 
different heads such as equipment, consumables and other 
necessary sections, the expenditure towards consumable and 
contingency shall not exceed 25% of the amount granted, no 
furniture items shall be purchased from the seed grant; the 
same shall be provided by the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya. 


(15) Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Projects: 
Some of activities of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, such as 
management of hostels, automation, laboratory development, etc. 
are financially regulated Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Projects, in 
administering such projects, the following points shall be 
considered: 

(a) The Project Investigator of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
Project appointed by the Vice Chancellor, shall seek the 
budget for the financial year from the Grant-in-aid; 

(b) After the budget is approved, a new project account shall be 
opened in the office of Research and Development; 

(c) Any purchase or procurement made under the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya Project shall be in accordance with the Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya norms of expenditure applicable to 
the Grant-in-aid; 

(d) The wages of the hired manpower in Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya Projects shall follow Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya norms; 

(e) The Office of Research and Development shall provide to the 
Vice Chancellor quarterly expenditure statement of all Gati 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 321 


Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Projects duly signed by respective 
Project Investigators; and 

(f) The Project Investigator of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
Project shall seek approval for additional budget for next 
session, before the available fund is exhausted. 


(16) Professional Development Account: 

(a) A Professional Development Account is created for each 
Faculty Member at the time of his joining the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya, incomes in Professional Development 
Account are those arising from the academic and professional 
activities of the Faculty Member, the incomes that can be 
credited to this account include: 

(i) part of Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Overheads from 
Sponsored Research Projects; 

(ii) amount transferred from Consultancy Projects, if any; 

(iii) any other balance income on the project, with the 
approval of Vice Chancellor; 

(b) The amount credited in the Professional Development Account 
can be utilised only for the designated purposes as outlined in 
the extant policy on Professional Development Account; 

(c) The Professional Development Account cannot be operated by 
the Faculty Member, when he is on long leave, short leave, 
leave on “Foreign Service Terms” or on extra-ordinary leave 
and he may be allowed to use the Professional Development 
Account amount, while on sabbatical leave (once the scheme is 
in place). 


(17) Utilization of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya overhead and 
research and development overhead: 

(a) The Overheads under “Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
Development Fund” shall be utilised as per the needs of the Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, and with the approval of the Vice 
Chancellor. 

(b) The “Research and Development Overhead” shall be utilised for 
both recurring and non- recurring expenditure, including 
compensation of the project staff, honorarium to regular staff 
members, travel and other expenditures approval by the Vice 
Chancellor. 


(18) Project Appointments: 
The appointment of qualified temporary Project Staff Members is an 
important project management activity of the Office of Research and 
Development, this section elaborates the possible appointments, 
procedure for making the appointment and salaries payable therein. 
(a) Type of Appointments: The hiring of manpower under 
sponsored Research Projects, Consultancy Projects and other 

Projects are of 4 categories, namely: - 

(i) Ad-hoc Appointment: Hiring of manpower on ad-hoc 
basis for a maximum period of three months by means of 
awarding a short term ad-hoc appointment against the 
vacant post or as one-time activity; 


322 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


(ii) Project Appointment: Hiring of qualified candidates by 
giving wide publicity through press, internet, etc., for a 
period beyond three months and extendable till maximum 
of one year, any appointment beyond an estimated period 
of one year can be made on the recommendation of Project 
Investigator for a maximum period of five years or till the 
duration of the project, whichever is earlier, approval of 
Competent Authority for engagement to be taken; 

(iii) Assistantship: Registered students of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya can be employed on projects on full-time 
basis during summer and winter vacations or on part-time 
basis during the semester as Student Research Associate or 
Senior Student Research Associate against the vacant post; 

(iv) Engagement of skilled, semi-skilled or unskilled 
personnel through Manpower Outsourcing Agency, the 
appointment of qualified temporary Project Staff Members 
is an important project management activity of the Office 
of Research and Development, this section elaborates the 
possible appointments, procedure for making the 
appointment and salaries payable therein. 

(2) Procedure of Appointment: 

(i) Ad-hoc Appointments. - The Project Investigator OR Co- 
Project Investigator can hire the services of qualified 
candidates on Ad-hoc basis (Informal selection) against 
vacant posts for a specific work pertaining to the project 
up to a maximum period of three months, the request for 
such appointments can be submitted in the Office of 
Research and Development in prescribed format along 
with curriculum vitae with all the supporting documents, 
like mark-sheets and experience certificates; 


(ii) Project Appointments.- The Project Investigator OR Co- 
Project Investigator may forward a request in prescribed 
form for selecting persons for such appointments against 
the vacant positions, also, the Project Investigator shall 
submit the qualifications and experience prescribed for the 
positions, the proposed qualification and experience can be 
higher but not lower than the norms laid down by the Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya or Funding Agency, a Selection 
Committee shall be constituted, with the approval of the 
Vice Chancellor; 

(iii) any appointment for less than one year can be through an 
advertisement in the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya website 
and for engagement for one year or above shall be through 
a press advertisement to be published in at least one 
English and one vernacular daily (with one insertion in 
Employment News) and the detailed advertisement hosted 
on the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya website stating the 
required educational qualification, experience, etc., the 
essential qualification mentioned by the funding agency 
shall be strictly followed, the Project Investigator can add 
additional qualification depending upon the requirement of 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 323 


the project and the advertisement shall mention clearly that 
the post is purely temporary and for specified limited 
period, strictly co-terminus with the project. 
(3) The Project Investigator has two options for holding these 
selections: 

(a) Walk-in Test OR Interview: The Project Investigator can 
exercise this option, if only a limited number of candidates 
are expected to apply and is for a period of maximum one 
year, under this option, on-the-spot written or skill test 
may also be conducted to narrow down the number of 
candidates; 

(b) Shortlisting followed by Written Test or Interview: 
This option can be adopted, if the engagement for one year 
or the above with a call to the shortlisted candidate. 

(4) The composition of standing Selection Committee shall be as 
follows, namely: - 

(a) For Consultants: 

(i) | Vice Chancellor or his nominee — Chairperson; 

(ii) | two Faculty Members from the domain concerned- 
Member; 

(iii) | one Faculty Member from the domain other than 
that of the — Member; 

(iv) Project Investigator — Convener; 

(b) For Research and Technical Staff Members: 

(i) Faculty-In-Charge of the concerned Center- 
Chairperson; 

(ii) One Faculty Member from the Center - Member; 

(iii) One Faculty Member from a Center other than that 
the Project Investigator - Member; 

(iv) Project Investigator - Convener; 

(c) For Administrative Staff Members: 

(i) Faculty-in-Charge (Research and Development)- 
Chairperson; 
(ii) | One Faculty Member or a Group A Officer — 
Member; 
(iii) Project Investigator — Convener. 
(5) The following are the additional notes on the above: 

(a) For the post which has been sanctioned by the funding 
agency, but not covered above, Vice Chancellor shall 
approve a Standing Selection Committee, णा the 
recommendation of the Faculty-in-Charge, all the members 
must be present during the interview, in case of exigency, 
the Convener may seek to replace the member unable to 
attend with another, with the approval of the Vice 
Chancellor; 

(b) The Chairperson of the Selection Committee shall ensure 
that any candidate who does not fulfill the advertised criteria 
is not interviewed OR considered for selection, the 
candidates appeared or appearing for final exams shall not 
be considered qualified, if their result is still awaited as on 
the last date of receipt of application. The candidates not 


324 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


fulfilling the minimum advertised qualification cannot be 
considered even for the lower post, if such lower position 
has not been advertised; 

(c) The Chairperson of the Selection Committee shall ensure 
that, all the candidates, who come to attend the interview, 
are interviewed properly by the Selection Committee; 

(d) The Members of the Selection Committee shall recuse 
themselves from the process of selection, where their own 
close relatives are appearing in the test OR interview, in 
case, it is so, the concerned Committee Member shall opt 
out, and another person be appointed in his place as 
Member, with the approval of the Vice Chancellor; 

(e) The scheduled Selection Committee meetings cannot be 
cancelled or rescheduled at short notice; this shall avoid 
inconvenience to candidates appearing in the interview, 
especially from outside, in case of absence of Chairperson or 
Project Investigator due to exigency, his nominee may be 
appointed with the approval of the Vice Chancellor, to 
ensure smooth conduct of interviews as scheduled; 

(f) The report of the Selection Committee duly recommended 
by the Faculty-in-Charge with applications of candidates 
selected and also considered for appointment under the 
project, shall be placed before the Vice Chancellor for 
approval; 

(g) After, obtaining approval of the Vice Chancellor, the offer 
of appointment can be issued to the selected candidates by 
the office of Registrar.

The compensation structure may be reviewed from time to 
time.
