(20) Others Terms and Conditions. (a) (i) All appointments shall be on 
contract for one year or less, in case the project is terminated 
earlier. 

(ii) Further, the maximum duration shall be the term of the 
project (co-terminus with the project) i.e., the appointment is 
against the temporary project post and shall not continue in any 
circumstances beyond the duration of the said project. 

(11) Any subsequent contract in the project or another project 
shall be fresh appointment. 

(iv) The appointment in the project does not give project staff 
members any claim whatsoever for appointment or regularisation 
against any regular post of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya; 

(b) All Project Staff Members need to sign a contract agreement with 
Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya on 7100 Non-judicial Stamp Paper 
at the time of the joining, compensation shall be released after the 
signing of the contract agreement, in case, any Project Staff 
Members does not sign the contract agreement within fifteen 
days from the date of joining, the appointment shall stand 
cancelled. 

(c) (0) compensation of the selected candidate shall be as indicated in 
the table above, 

(1) but, at the time of selection, the Selection Committee can 
recommend a higher start by offering a maximum of five 
increments in case of experienced candidates, the higher start 
from the initial level of the scale can be calculated duly counting 
the number of years of experience, offering one increment each 
for each year of service, over and above the minimum number of 
years required towards applicable experience, 

(iii) in case the Committee recommends fixation at higher stage 
within the scale, the same may only be given with proper 
justification, recommendation for higher fixation shall be 
entertained, only if the fund is available, 

(iv) finally, the recommendation of the Selection Committee 
shall be reviewed by the Vice Chancellor before approval; 

(d) The pay-fixation of staff retired from Government service (if 
any) shall be fixed as per Government of India norms; 

(e) Intellectual Property Rights: 

(i) Staff Members appointed in a project shall be required to 
work under the supervision of the Project Investigator of the 


330 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


project, the outcome of any research under the project is the 
property of the project and is bound by the prevailing 
Intellectual Property Rights Policy of the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya and sponsors of the project and any result 
can only be published with prior written approval of the 
Project Investigator with due acknowledgement of the 
sponsors of the project. 

(ii) on leaving the service, all documents relating to the project 
are required to be returned to the Project Investigator; 

(f) At the time of the extension of the service of the Project Staff 
Members, the Project Investigator can recommend 5% 
enhancement in the compensation after completion of one-year 
service or the funding agency norms shall be applicable from 
time to time, extension of the service is admissible only when the 
project is valid and the sufficient fund is available; 

(g) Extension of service can be given up to a maximum of one year 
at a time, the maximum length of service for a project staff 
cannot exceed five years or as per the funding agency norms 
whichever is minimum; 

(h) Incase of migration of a project staff from one project to another 
without the need for undergoing fresh selection process, valid 
reasons and justification shall be submitted by the Project 
Investigator for such migration, in such cases, the Personal File 
Number of the Project Staff Members shall remain the same and 
the total cumulative engagement shall be not more than five 
years; 

(i) If a person is already working or has worked earlier in any 
specific project of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, the previous 
compensation can be taken as the basis for fixing the 
consolidated pay for the fresh position being appointed for; 

G) का case of person who retired from the Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya service, the academic qualifications can be 
relaxed commensurate with the experience and the designation 
with proper mapping to the designations and experience levels as 
mentioned in the above tables; 

(k) (0) in case a Project Investigator considers upgrading a Project 
staff within the same project, he after reviewing performance of 
the staff can recommend for up-gradation without going through 
Selection Committee; 

(ii) but, in case of up-gradation of the staff to work in some other 
project, the case shall be put up through the Selection Committee 
for consideration; 

(iii) the up-gradation shall be justifiable in terms of additional 
qualifications and experience, acquired commensurate with the 
position for which being considered; 

(1) | Consolidated compensation offered for the project post is all 
inclusive; 

(m) Medical Facilities: Routine outpatient department medical 
facility at Gatti Shakti Vishwavidyalaya (for consultancy and 
diagnostics only) can be availed; 

(n) Termination of Service: During the period of project 
appointment, Project Staff Members are liable to be terminated 


[भाग [खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 331 


with or without one month’s notice or on payment of one-month 
compensation in lieu thereof, likewise, Project Staff Members too 
can resign by giving one-month notice or compensation in lieu of 
the notice period; 

(0) Leave Entitlement Per year: Applicable as equal to the 
contractual staff of the University which varies from time to time 
and 
No leave encashment or carry forward is permissible on the 
termination or renewal of the contract. 

(21) Assistantship to Students. The registered students of the Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya may be employed on projects on part-time 
basis as Student Research Associate or Senior Student Research 
Associate with the following hourly payment norms as given in the 


table:

(a) The payment structure may be reviewed from time to time and 
the following terms and conditions are admissible, namely: 


(i) the minimum Cumulative Grade Point Average is 6.5 for 
employing Bachelor of Technology Students in part-time 
project work; 

(ii) the employment of students be recommended by the Dean 
(Acad) and Head of the respective Center in the prescribed 
format; 

(iii) non-Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya students to be engaged 
on the projects on the part-time basis, shall produce a no 
objection certificate from the current Head of their 
University along with a bonefide student certificate; 

(iv) applicable Income Tax is deducted, wherever necessary, 
for the payment made for the project employment or 
assignments; 

(v) any project assignment of the student shall not affect the 
academic or administrative status of a student, that is, a 
student employed on a project shall, for purpose of leave, 
accommodation, house rent, etc. (except payment 
received) be governed by his student status and during the 
part-time assignments, he shall continue getting the regular 
scholarships that may be admissible to him; 


(b) Engagement of Skilled, Semi-skilled and unskilled personnel 
through Manpower Outsourcing Agency.- 
To carry out miscellaneous tasks required for the projects, 
skilled, semi-skilled or unskilled manpower on hourly basis can 


332 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


be engaged through Outsourcing Manpower Agency, subject to 
the minimum wages as approved by the Government of India 
norms, in such cases, the following procedure shall be followed: 

(i) The Project Investigator shall take the approval of 
Faculty-In-Charge (Research and Development) for 
engaging skilled, semi-skilled or unskilled personnel; 

(ii) after getting the approval, the Project Investigator shall 
request to the Establishment for providing the required 
personnel through the Manpower Outsourcing Agency; 
and 

(iii) | the Project Investigator shall verify the attendance of the 
engaged personnel for making monthly payments of 
remuneration. 

Note: No person can be engaged on projects under more than one 
funding source, i.e., simultaneously a Sponsored Project and 
Consultancy Project. 


(22) Travel from Project: (a) The travel shall be regulated based on the 
Travelling Allowance Rules of Government of India unless and 
otherwise specified by the funding agency and this requires the 
approval of The Competent Authority, record of such expenses shall 
be recorded under the specified budget heads of the Project 
Accounts. payment of charges to travel agents for ticket purchase 
assistance, visa assistance, Insurance, etc. shall be admissible from 
project funds. 

(b) But, if funding agency has specified any specific condition for 
travel under the project, that shall be followed normally. 


(23) Temporary Project Advance: (a) To carry out miscellaneous 
expenditures required for the projects, the Project Investigator can 
use the funds from the project and shall take a temporary advance 
for the same and advance can be taken from various budget heads, 
like Contingency, Consumables and Travel; 

(b) The Project Investigator shall submit the request in the 
prescribed format, not more than 4 advances shall be sanctioned 
from one project at a time, and that too only one per person; 

(c) All the advances taken to be settled within fifteen days from the 
date of the completion of the activity and purpose for which the 
advance was taken; 

(d) Statement of expenditure shall be submitted by the Project 
Investigator in the prescribed format with all the original bills duly 
verified, the PI shall ensure that all the bills are properly entered in 
the relevant project register. 

(e) Non-consumables item must be entered in the assets register of 
the Office of Stores and Purchase, else bills shall not be passed for 
the payment. 


(24) Project Procurement: 

(a) (i) All items purchased under a project are property of the Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya (or of the funding agency if specified), 
though they are to be used primarily for the Project, they are to 
be purchased using the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya approved 
procedures, after the project terminates or completes, they remain 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 333 


with the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, unless there is a specific 
provision or requirement of returning the same to the funding 
agency. 

(ii) Consequently, each item purchased shall be recorded in the 
Assets Register and all permanent assets shall depreciate as per 
the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya norms; 

(b) The Project Investigator shall verify the original bill and cash 
memos, and submit in the prescribed format to the Finance 
Office for payment or reimbursement, Project Investigator shall 
ensure that all the bills are properly entered in stock register, non- 
consumables items must be entered in the Assets Register 
maintained by the Office of Stores and Purchase, else the bills 
shall not be passed for the payment; 

(c) For the purchase high-end or costly equipment, a Technical 
Purchase Committee has been constituted to assess the technical 
aspects of the equipment and their installation, the composition 
of the Technical Purchase Committee as: 

(i) faculty in Charge (Laboratory) Chairperson; 
(ii) one Faculty Member in relevant field © Member; 
of the purchase 
(iii) if estimated cost of the equipment is | Member; 
Rs. 1 Crore or above one specialist 
working in area of research relevant to 
purchase 
(iv) project Investigator of the project Member; 

(d) The responsibilities of the Technical Purchase Committee are: 

(i) finalising the most suitable generic technical requirement for 
the purchase to meet the objectives of the projects; 

(ii) drafting technical specification of the equipment purchase; 

(iii) providing technical assistance during the tender process; 
and the purchase shall be done by the Tender Committee of 
Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya; 

(९) Procurement of goods or services out of public funding shall 
comply with the General Finance Rules, Rules of Government of 
India, these rules are relaxed for the procurement of goods or 
services out of private funding by keeping in view of the timely 
deliverables and quality aspects, however, the private funding 
procurement shall comply with the University norms as 
applicable. 


(25) Budgetary Norms: 


With respect to research projects, the total charges of a research 
project shall consist of the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya overhead, 
and actual expenses of the project covering following, namely:- 
(a) Permanent equipment to be procured or fabricated 
equipment or models; 
(b) Consumable materials; 
(c) Travel expenses in connection with the project work 
(domestic and foreign if budgeted or allowed by sponsor); 
(d) Computational or other charges payable to any other outside 
agency; 
(e) All contingency expenses for report preparation of report 


334 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


and literature (books, journals) and any other item budgeted 
under the proposal and approved by the funding agency; 

(f) Project Staff compensation; expenses for work to be carried 
out on payment basis, remuneration to student assistants 
etc.; 

(g) Insurance on equipment and manpower during travel. 


(26) Project Monitoring Committee: 
For large projects the Project Monitoring Committee with the 
following composition shall review and assess the progress of the 
project periodically (at least once a year) for timely completion of 
the projects, the Committee may advice Faculty-in-Charge in any 
matter on the project, the composition of Project Monitoring 
Committee shall be as follows:- 


(a) Faculty in Charge Chairperson; 
(b) Faculty in Charge of the concerned Member; 
Center or domain or his nominee 

(c) Faculty in Charge (Laboratories) Member; 


(d) One Faculty Member of the relevant field Member; 
(e) One specialist from outside the Gati 


Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Member or 
in relevant field, if required: representative of 
funding agency; 
(f) Project Investigator Convener. 


The Vice Chancellor is the Competent Authority to constitute and or 
approve the Project Monitoring Committee. 


(27) Disagreement Or Disputes: 
(a) Any disagreement within the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya 
arising at any stage of a Consultancy Project shall be resolved in 
consultation with Faculty-in-Charge or Vice Chancellor to ensure an 
expeditious removal of bottlenecks and smooth functioning of the 
project; 
(b) In case of any dispute arising between Investigator(s) and 
funding agencys at any stage of Consultancy Project, only the 
Investigators shall be responsible for settlement of the dispute. 
(c) The Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya holds no liability in any matter 
of dispute arising out of the project. 
(d) All legal action shall be subject to jurisdiction at Civil Courts at 
Vadodara only. 


(28) Arbitration: 

(a) In the event of any dispute or difference at any time arising 
between the parties relating to Consultancy Project, or any other 
clause or any content of the right and liabilities of the parties or 
other matters specified therein or with reference to anything arising 
out of the Consultancy or otherwise in relation to the terms, whether 
during the Consultancy or thereafter, such disputes or differences 
shall be endeavored to be resolved by mutual negotiations. 

(b) If, such negotiations are infructuous, the dispute shall be finally 
settled through Arbitration and Conciliation Act, 1996 (26 of 1996), 
the arbitrators shall give reasoned and speaking award. 


[भागवता--खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 335 


(29) Limited Liability: In case, any legal dispute arises between the 
Investigator and the funding agency, in that case the Investigator are 
in any way held responsible to make good the losses incurred by the 
funding agency, such liability shall be restricted to a maximum 
liability calculated as follows: 


Maximum Liability = Total Fee Charged for the project — 
Expenditure already incurred in the project, the Project Investigator 
shall be responsible to bring this fact to the notice of the funding 
agency. 

(30) Guidelines for Memorandum of Understanding or Agreement: 

(a) Intellectual Property Rights- The  Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya shall try to keep its rights intact as far as 
possible, the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya must protect its right 
to use the information generated from research at any point in 
the future without having to seek permission in case Intellectual 
Property Rights is owned by other party, the developer shall 
have the right to publish the paper and this shall be expressly 
indicated in the Memorandum of Understanding; 

(b) Developer, Faculty-in-Charge or Project Investigator.- 
Memorandum of Understanding shall carry the name of the 
developer, who shall accomplish the goals of the project and he 
is responsible for the execution of the project; 

(c) Deliverables.- The wording of this section is most crucial in the 
text of the Memorandum of Understanding, accuracy is 
paramount, the timeline shall be attainable, deliverables shall be 
clearly mentioned as an article or clause of the agreement, 
statement of work and dates of milestones to be achieved are 
spelt out and a full project proposal may be attached for 
completeness; 

(d) Overheads and Taxes.- Budget shall be inclusive of Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya overheads as per rules and Goods and Services 
Tax (if applicable), budget shall be annexed to the agreement 
and there shall be some initial payment (e.g. 25%) to start the 
Project; 

(e) Conflict Resolution.- The Memorandum of Understanding shall 
stipulate the jurisdiction of the local courts (including New 
Delhi), this is an important point for International Memorandum 
of Understandings, the agreement shall be governed by the 
Indian Laws, applicability of foreign laws for dispute resolution 
is not allowed, alternately, dispute may be referred for 
Arbitration under the provisions of 

(i) the Arbitration and Conciliation Act, 1996 (26 of 1996); 

(ii)the rules of Arbitration of the International Chamber of 
Commerce. Or the Arbitration Rules of the United Nations 
Commission on International Trade Law and place of 
arbitration for domestic arbitration shall be Vadodara and 
for International, New Delhi (India), alternately, in case a 
party files a complaint (Plaintiff), the Defendant (against 
whom a claim or charge is brought) shall have choice to 
designate the place of arbitration; 

(f) Confidentiality.- The Project Investigator and team members 
shall be responsible for non-disclosure of confidential 
information and perpetual disclosure of confidential information 


336 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY [PART III—SEc.4] 


shall be avoided as far as possible; 

(g) Technology Transfer Agreement (Generic).- 

(i) | the agreement shall give reference of the technology to be 
transferred (in terms of publications or book); 

(ii) any information, materials, services, intellectual property, 
other property or rights, granted or provided by Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya pursuant to this agreement are on an as- 
is-where-is basis; 

(iii) Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya does not make any warranty 
of any kind, either expressly or implicitly for any matter 
including, but not limited to, warranty of fitness for 
particular purpose, or merchantability, exclusivity or 
results obtained from use; 

(iv) Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya cannot give warranty of 
successful exploitation of the technology; 

(v) in case, the other party transfers or assigns Intellectual 
Property Rights acquired in technology transfer agreement 
developed by Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya, or transfers or 
sells Intellectual Property Rights or product developed 
based on the technology during any project to any third 
party, the above clauses shall also be applicable to third 
party, 

(vi) technology transfer agreement shall be a standalone 
agreement, any further development for manufacturing the 
product or commercialisation of the technology shall be 
governed by a separate service or consultancy agreement; 

(vil) any penalty or security deposit clause is not acceptable; 

(vili) gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya shall not bear any charges for 
the transfer of assets or technology; and 

(ix) equipment purchased out of the project funds shall be 
owned by the Gati Shakti Vishwavidyalaya and shall not 
be returned to the funding agency (unless and otherwise 
specifically agreed to); 


(h) When Memorandum of Understanding is clear from all angles and 
acceptable to both the parties, it shall be sent to the Vice- 
Chancellor for approval along with the summary of the 
Memorandum of Understanding, duly signed by Project 
Investigator, forwarded by Faculty-In-Charge (Research and 
Development), after approval, the same may be signed by the 
authorised signatories along with witnesses, a copy of 
Memorandum of Understanding signed by both the parties shall 
be kept in records at Office of Research and Development, and 
placed at the subsequent Executive Council meeting for its 
perusal. 


55. Purchase policy. - (1) Objectives: The Purchase policy intends to meet the following 

objectives: 

(a) To maintain uninterrupted flow of goods and services to support 
the development schedules of the University; 

(b) To procure goods and services economically at a cost consistent 
with the quality and service required; 

(c) To maintain organisation reputation and credibility in the market 
by fair evaluation of bids and quotations, transparent, timely 


[AMT 1--खण्ड 4] 


भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 337 


issue of purchase order or contracts and timely payment of dues. 


(2) Procurement type and Financial Limits: 
As a guiding principle, the type of procurement and financial limits 
shall be in accordance with General Finance Rules 2017 as follow:- 


SI. जा type Reference General 
No. (2) Finance Rules. 2017 
(1) (3) 

1 No Tender or Direct Purchase Rule 154 

2 Three-member committee Rule 155 

3 Limited Tender Rule 158, 160 

4 Open Tender Rule 160, 161 


(3) Submission of Indent: 

(a) Indenting for purchase by end user departments OR centers OR 
sections is made after notification of budgetary allocations by 
the Competent Authority in the beginning of every financial 
year or as long as fund is available; 

(b) Indent is put up as per normal Indent Format with adequate 
justifications for purchase of the required item along with (a) 
Details Specifications; (b) Proposed Purchase Committee; and 
(c) Proposed vendor list (in case of limited Notice Inviting 
Quotation); 

(c) In case of indent for furniture, in addition to the above, 
appropriate drawing and design of the required furniture items 
are also to be provided; 

(d) The Indent is to be submitted to the Stores and Purchase 
Section; 

(e) Approval of indents is done by the competent authorities as per 
delegation of financial power; 

(f) A demand for goods shall not be divided into small quantities 
to make piece meal purchases to avoid the necessity of 
obtaining the sanction of higher authority required with 
reference to the estimated value of the total demand. 


(4) Constitution of Purchase Committee and procurement: 
The procurement may be carried out through Government e- 
marketplace or Central Public Procurement Portal or other means as 
provided under the extant rules as per the General Finance Rules 
provisions as amended from time to time and through the Committee 
as authorised; as constituted by the Registrar or Vice Chancellor, as 
per the delegation of financial powers. 


(5) Procurement through Spot Purchase committees: 


(a) There may be circumstances when some items of material 
(normally available off the shelf) are purchased on the spot by 
calling offers through physical visit of the premises of the firms 
by the nominated officers and this mode of purchase is 
popularly known as ‘Spot Purchase’; 

(b) Conditions for resorting to Spot Purchase:- 

(i) the purchase through Spot Purchase Committee may be 


338 


THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY 


resorted to under exceptional circumstances as detailed 
below:-. 

(ii) this mode shall be used for purchase of goods which are 
required occasionally wherein making the detailed 
specifications for the same is not practicable or 
economical and for items of consumable nature, where 
the quality of material in the market is rapidly changing 
and such materials can be better selected by personal 
examination for feel, finish, appearance, users' comfort, 
suitability for decor, aesthetics, taste, colour 
combination or contrast, etc. based on visual perception 
or personal examination, etc. e.g. like furniture to suit 
ambience of rooms, crockery, cutlery, curtains, carpets, 
cloth, furnishing items and like; 

(iii) it is only when the well-defined or well- honed up 
system like tender system cannot be adopted that spot 
purchase is resorted to. 


(c) Competent Authority to sanction Spot Purchase.- 


(i) Vice-Chancellor shall be the Competent Authority for 
sanctioning procurement on Spot Purchase committee, as per 
delegation given for various paragraphs of Gati Shakti 
Vishwavidyalaya Standard Operating Policy, Vice- 
Chancellor's administrative approval be taken before sending 
the proposal to finance as Vice-Chancellor is The Competent 
Authority. 

(ii) The proposal of purchase through Spot Purchase 
Committee duly concurred by finance shall be sent to 
procurement in charge by the logistic in-charge, thereafter, it 
shall be submitted for specific sanction of Vice-Chancellor. 


(d) The composition of Spot Purchase Committee.- 


(i) The spot purchase shall invariably be made by a committee 
of three officers, one from Stores or Admin (may be Deputy 
Registrar or Deputy Comptroller), one from Accounts 
Department and one from Indenting or user Department. 

(ii) Registrar or Deputy Registrar or Chief Finance Officer or 
Deputy Finance Officer or Professor or Associate Professor or 
Assistant Professor in Level-12 in the pay matrix (Rs. 78800- 
209200) officers shall be nominated as members of Spot 
Purchase Committee- The level of Spot Purchase Committee 
shall be as given in the Table:-

(e) If there are more than one items to be spot purchased 
simultaneously, each purchase (quantity X rate for each item) 
shall be within such ceiling and the Indenter shall act as the 
convener of the Committee. 


(6) Procedure. (a) Spot Purchase is only a mode of purchase, therefore, 
there shall be proper requisitions or Demand for items duly 
vetted as per Clause 2 (1.1) of "Stores and Purchase" of Gati 
Shakti Vishwavidyalaya Standard Operating Policy; 

(b) The Committee shall undertake the market survey and visit 
minimum three shops or firms and try to obtain quotations from 
suitable sources so that competition and hence economy shall 
be enhanced, in conducting their survey, the committee shall 
give due regard to the 'Standards of Financial Propriety’, since 
officers of minimum level-11 in the pay matrix (Rs. 67700- 
208700) are to form the Store Purchase Committee and it is 
expected that the aspects of transparency, economy, etc. are 
taken care of; 

(c) The quality and suitability of materials being purchased by the 
Committee shall be certified by the Member from the 
consuming department serving on Committee;

(7) Payments: Payment to the suppliers shall be through cheque or 
National Electronic Funds Transfer or Real Time Gross 
Settlement by accounts department, the procedure of payment 
through associate accounts may be followed, depending upon the 
agreed terms and conditions and payment shall be made after 
taking delivery of accepted material and in no case payment shall 
be made to the firm in anticipation of the supply of material. 


56. Schedule of financial General Principles of delegation of powers or Delegation of 
elcid financial powers:- 

(1) Any official of the University shall not incur any expenditure 
or enter into any liability involving expenditure unless the 
approval for the same has been taken from the Competent 
Authority of the University. 

(2) In case where it becomes necessary to issue a sanction to 
expenditure before funds or grants are released, the sanction 
shall specify that such expenditure is subject to funds or grants 
being disbursed. 

(3) Lapse of Sanction: A sanction for any expenditure, unless it is 
specifically renewed, shall lapse within a period of twelve 
months from the date of issue of such sanction provided that: 

(a) When the period of sanction is prescribed in the sanction 
itself, it shall lapse on the expiry of such period; 

(b) When there is a specific provision in a sanction that the 
expenditure shall be met from budget provision of 
specified financial year then the sanction shall lapse at 
the end of financial year. 

(4) Unless otherwise specified, all delegations are on per occasion 
basis, in order to get the approval of higher Competent 
Authority, splitting shall be avoided and discouraged. 

(5) Delegated powers may be delegated further without prior 
approval of the authority that originally delegated the powers. 

(6) All the power delegated hereunder are subject to budget 
allocation under the relevant budget-head and after following 
the prescribed procedure and guidelines indicated in the Act or 
Statutes issued from time to time by Government of India or 
Executive Council. 

(7) In case of ambiguity or conflicts between the provisions 
stipulated in this handbook and in the original source such as 
General Finance Rules, etc. or the prevailing rules or 
guidelines or law of Government of India, the provisions 
contained in the original source Government of India rules shall 
prevail. 

(8) All purchase proposals shall be processed as per the procedure 
prescribed in the General Finance Rules, purchase procedures, 
all Authorities concerned shall submit the proposals to the 
Vice- Chancellor OR Registrar for administrative approval. 

(9) For cases other than Works: All cases of expenditure shall be 
concurred by Finance. 

(10) A formal sanction for creation of posts or incurring of 
expenditure shall be communicated to all concerned, indicating 


[भाग पा--_खण्ड 4] भारत का राजपत्र असाधारण : 341 


reference to the financial concurrence and existence of budget 

provision. 

(11) Notwithstanding these delegations, in cases where adequate 
competition does not exist or emergent purchases are to be 
made, expenditure is to be incurred in relaxation of the 
prescribed procedure, approval of the Vice-Chancellor with the 
concurrence of Finance shall be necessary. 

(12) Notwithstanding these delegation of Financial Powers, Vice 
Chancellor can exercise powers of any of the officers to whom 
the power has been delegated. 

(13) The constitution of the Committee for procurement shall be done 
by the Registrar OR Vice Chancellor as per the delegated 
powers. 

(a) All the officers having Financial powers in Delegation of 
Financial Powers Rules are required to furnish Quarterly 
report to Vice-Chancellor by 15th day of the month 
following the expenditure incurred during previous Quarter. 

(b) The Vice Chancellor, if so desires, may revoke or limit the 
financial powers of an authority in case the concerned 
authority has not been complying with the financial powers 
delegated to him or her under this Delegation of Financial 
Powers. 


These delegated financial powers are subject to review. 


RAJEEV KUMAR, Registrar
